### [1] [Limiting Law of Local Fields for the Mean Field Ghatak-Sherrington Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10515)
*Yunhui Chen,Keaton Fierro*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了平均场Ghatak-Sherrington模型在高温区间中局部场（local field）的极限分布，利用腔方法（cavity method）和Sheng-Wu的定量重叠与自重叠估计，证明了非中心腔场的中心极限定理，并将局部场的极限分布识别为高斯分布的随机有限混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部场的极限分布是无序自旋玻璃理论中的一个核心问题，对于理解高温区域的统计行为至关重要。此前Sheng-Wu给出了定量的重叠和自重叠估计，本研究在此基础上进一步推导局部场的极限定律。

Method: 论文采用腔方法（cavity method）结合Sheng-Wu的定量重叠与自重叠估计（quantitative overlap and self-overlap estimates），证明非中心腔场的中心极限定理（central limit theorem）。证明基于矩方法（moment method），并给出了定量误差界。

Result: 在高温区域，局部场的极限分布被识别为高斯分布的随机有限混合（finite mixture of Gaussian distributions），并获得了定量的误差界。

Conclusion: 对于自旋取值为{0,±1,…,±S}的Ghatak-Sherrington模型，高温局部场可由高斯混合分布精确刻画，该结果推广了经典自旋玻璃模型的相应结论。

Abstract: We study the limiting law of local field in the mean field Ghatak-Sherrington model with spin values $\{0,\pm1,\ldots,\pm S\}$ in the high-temperature regime. Using the cavity method and the quantitative overlap and self-overlap estimates of Sheng-Wu~\cite{SW24}, we prove a central limit theorem for the non-centered cavity field. As a result, we then identify the local field limiting law as a random finite mixture of Gaussian distributions. The proof is based on moment method and gives a quantitative error bound.

</details>


### [2] [The sharp SAT/UNSAT phase transition in random ellipsoid fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10184)
*Theodor Misiakiewicz,Garrett G. Wen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Saunderson、Parrilo 和 Willsky 关于椭圆拟合（ellipsoid fit）半定可行性问题的猜想：当 $n,d \to \infty$ 时，该问题在 $n \sim d^2/4$ 处发生尖锐相变（sharp transition）。当 $n/d^2 < 1/4$ 时椭球拟合几乎必然存在，且可选取特征值有界且适定的矩阵 $S$；当 $n/d^2 > 1/4$ 时则几乎必然不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Saunderson, Parrilo 和 Willsky 提出了一个关于高维高斯向量椭球拟合问题存在性临界点的猜想，即相变发生在 $n \sim d^2/4$ 处。Bandeira 与 Maillard (2025) 建立了高斯等价（Gaussian-equivalence）框架，但留下了两个缺口：精确拟合的建立和算子范数约束的去除。本文旨在填补这两个缺口，从而完整证明该猜想。

Method: 在可满足情形下，本文引入双向量（dual vector）的头部-尾部分解（head-tail decomposition），对稀疏头部约束进行精确修正，并对低影响力尾部采用高斯比较原理（Gaussian comparison principle）。在不可满足情形下，将候选矩阵拆分为低秩谱头（low-rank spectral head）与 Schatten-3 弥散体（diffuse bulk），在给定头部条件下对体部高斯化，并应用投影戈登逃逸论证（projected Gordon escape argument）。阈值由正半定锥（positive semidefinite cone）的统计维度 $d(d+1)/4$ 决定。

Result: 当 $\lim \sup n/d^2 = \alpha^* < 1/4$ 时，椭圆拟合以趋于1的概率存在，且可选择所有特征值落在仅依赖 $\alpha^*$ 的固定区间 $[\lambda_-, \lambda_+] \subset (0,\infty)$ 内的矩阵 $S$。反之，当 $\lim \inf n/d^2 > 1/4$ 时，无需任何谱限制，椭圆拟合以趋于1的概率不存在。

Conclusion: 该结果完全证实了 Saunderson、Parrilo 和 Willsky 的猜想，确立了椭圆拟合半定可行性问题在 $n \sim d^2/4$ 处的尖锐相变，且相变阈值由正半定锥的统计维度决定，表明了该现象与高维凸几何中的内在维度结构密切相关。

Abstract: Let $x_1,\ldots,x_n$ be independent standard Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$. An \emph{ellipsoid fit} is a matrix $S \succeq 0$ such that $x_i^\top S x_i =d$ for every $i$, so that all the points lie on the boundary of the centered ellipsoid $\{ x : x^\top S x = d\}$. Saunderson, Parrilo and Willsky conjectured that, as $n,d \to \infty$, this semidefinite feasibility problem undergoes a sharp transition at $n \sim d^2/4$. We prove this conjecture. If $\lim \sup n/d^2 = α^* <1/4$, then, with probability tending to one, an ellipsoid fit exists; moreover, one can choose $S$ with all eigenvalues in a fixed interval $[λ_- , λ_+] \subset (0,\infty)$ depending only on $α^*$. Conversely, if $\lim \inf n/d^2 > 1/4$, then, with probability tending to one, no ellipsoid fit exists, without any spectral restriction.
  Our proof builds on the Gaussian-equivalence framework developed by Bandeira and Maillard (2025) and closes the two gaps left open in their work: establishing exact fitting and removing the operator-norm constraint. On the satisfiable side, the new ingredients are a head-tail decomposition of the dual vector, exact correction of the sparse head constraints, and a Gaussian comparison principle for the low-influence tail. On the unsatisfiable side, we split a candidate into a low-rank spectral head and a Schatten-3 diffuse bulk, Gaussianize the bulk conditionally on the head, and apply a projected Gordon escape argument. The threshold is governed by the statistical dimension $d(d+1)/4$ of the positive semidefinite cone.

</details>

### [3] [Quadrupolar phase transition in superconducting lanthanum hydride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10428)
*Abhishek Raghav,Kousuke Nakano,Marco Cherubini,Ryotaro Arita,Michele Casula*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过团簇多极矩分析（cluster multipole moment analysis）揭示了氢化镧（LaH₁₀）中R3̄m→Fm3̄m结构相变由四极T₂g序参量触发，并具有弱一级相变特征；结合路径积分分子动力学与MACE神经网络势表明，相变伴随光学T₂g声子的不连续软化，其对称性使其携带显著的电声耦合，为超氢化物中增强超导性提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢化镧因在约170 GPa压力下具有250 K的高超导临界温度而被广泛研究，其R3̄m→Fm3̄m结构相变与超导相的出现密切相关，但该相变的精确性质及其序参量（order parameter）尚未被详细表征。

Method: 对氢子晶格施加团簇多极矩分析（cluster multipole moment analysis），以确定相变的序参量及其性质；同时采用耦合消息传递原子团簇展开（MACE）神经网络势的路径积分分子动力学（path integral molecular dynamics），该势基于Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof（PBE）密度泛函理论构型训练。

Result: 研究发现该相变由四极T₂g序参量触发，且呈现弱一级（weak first-order）相变特征；相变处序参量的坍塌同时伴随光学T₂g声子的不连续软化，这些声子的对称性使其在LaH₁₀中承载不可忽略的电声耦合（electron-phonon coupling）。

Conclusion: 具有低频四极畸变的结构不稳定性可能是增强超氢化物超导性的关键因素，为在富氢化合物中发现新的高T_c超导体提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Lanthanum hydride (LaH$_{10}$) has been widely studied for its high superconducting critical temperature of 250 K at about 170 GPa pressure. Although the structural ${R\bar{3}m}$-to-${Fm\bar{3}m}$ transition under pressure connected to the emergence of the superconducting phase in this material is broadly understood, the detailed characterization of its nature and its order parameter are still missing. By applying the cluster multipole moment analysis to the hydrogen sublattice, we reveal that this transition is triggered by a quadrupolar $T_{2g}$ order parameter, and we provide evidence for its weak first-order nature. By performing path integral molecular dynamics coupled to a message-passing atomic cluster expansion (MACE) neural network potential, trained on Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) density functional theory configurations, we show that the collapse of the order parameter at the transition is simultaneously associated with the discontinuous softening of the optical $T_{2g}$ phonons. Their symmetry lets them carry a non-negligible electron-phonon coupling in LaH$_{10}$, while the weak first-order nature of the transition makes them soft. The presence of structural instabilities with low-frequency quadrupolar distortions can be a key ingredient to enhance superconductivity in superhydrides and provides guidance for the discovery of new high-$T_c$ superconductors in hydrogen-rich compounds.

</details>

### [4] [Reflection Positivity in Free Fermionic Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10685)
*Carl Handrack,Manfred Salmhofer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于自由费米子理论，若协方差为狄拉克算子的实有理函数，则反射正性成立当且仅当所有极点为实数、单重且具有非负留数；并显式展示了指数型截断因子会破坏反射正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射正性（reflection positivity）是欧几里得量子场论的 Osterwalder–Schrader 公理之一，它确保重建的相对论性希尔伯特空间具有正定内积，是联系欧几里得理论与真实时空间理论的关键性质，因此需要系统地刻画其成立条件。

Method: 针对协方差为狄拉克算子（Dirac operator）的实有理函数的自由费米子理论，作者采用解析方法分析反射正性的充要条件，即考察有理函数的极点在复平面的分布（实性、阶数与留数符号），并对具有指数型截断因子（exponential regulator）的协方差进行具体反例构造以展示其破坏作用。

Result: 证明了反射正性成立当且仅当协方差的所有极点均为实数、单重且带有非负留数；同时通过显式例子表明，带有指数型截断因子的协方差将导致反射正性失效。

Conclusion: 该结果为自由费米子欧几里得量子场论中的反射正性提供了明确、可检验的代数判据，并警示指数型截断在构造欧几里得理论时可能违反 Osterwalder–Schrader 公理。

Abstract: Reflection positivity is one of the Osterwalder--Schrader axioms for Euclidean quantum field theory, ensuring that the reconstructed relativistic Hilbert space carries a positive-definite inner product. For free fermionic theories whose covariance is a real rational function of the Dirac operator, we prove that reflection positivity holds if and only if all poles are real, simple, and carry non-negative residues. We also explicitly show the failure of reflection positivity for covariances with exponential regulators.

</details>


### [5] [Emergence of fractal structures from breather interactions in the $(2+1)$-dimensional Konopelchenko--Dubrovsky equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10797)
*Snehalata Nasipuri,Prasanta Chatterjee,Saugata Dutta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Hirota双线性方法研究(2+1)维Konopelchenko-Dubrovsky（KD）方程中非线性呼吸子（breather）相互作用生成的分形结构，采用三维基于体素的盒计数法计算分形维数，并通过多种误差分析验证维数估计的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性耦合色散系统中呼吸子相互作用是否能够自然产生分形几何与多尺度结构，并对其几何复杂性进行定量表征。该研究旨在为流体动力学、等离子体物理和非线性波传播中的能量局域化与尺度依赖结构提供新的理论见解。

Method: 首先推导系统的双线性形式（bilinear form），再通过适当的辅助函数解析构造呼吸子相互作用解。随后采用三维基于体素的盒计数法（box-counting method）计算结构的几何维数，并进行相对误差分析、标准误差估计、bootstrap标准差及收敛性分析以检验结果的稳健性。

Result: 呼吸波在耦合非线性环境中的相互作用产生了高度复杂的多尺度图案，在连续放大下表现出自相似行为。计算得到的维数为非整数，确认了所生成图案的分形本质。多种误差分析方法表明维数估计具有较好的稳健性和可重复性。

Conclusion: 非线性呼吸子相互作用为分形几何和多尺度结构的涌现提供了可能的内在机制。所建立的分析与定量相结合框架可为非线性能量局域化及跨尺度结构的理解提供进一步洞见。

Abstract: Fractal structures generated through nonlinear breather interactions are investigated for the $(2+1)$-dimensional Konopelchenko--Dubrovsky (KD) equation by means of the Hirota bilinear method. The bilinear form of the system is first derived, after which breather interaction solutions are constructed analytically through suitable auxiliary functions. It is shown that the interaction of breather waves in the coupled nonlinear environment gives rise to highly intricate multiscale patterns exhibiting self-similar behaviour under successive magnification. To characterize the geometric complexity of the obtained structures, a three-dimensional voxel-based box-counting method is employed. The computed dimensions are found to be non-integer, confirming the fractal nature of the generated patterns. In addition, relative error analysis, standard error estimation, bootstrap standard deviation and convergence analysis are performed to examine the robustness and reproducibility of the estimated dimensions. The present work suggests that nonlinear breather interactions in coupled dispersive systems may provide a natural mechanism for the emergence of fractal geometries and complex multiscale structures. The combined analytical and quantitative framework developed here may provide further insight into nonlinear energy localization and scale-dependent structures arising in fluid dynamics, plasma physics and nonlinear wave propagation.

</details>


### [6] [The Renormalization Group as a Stochastic Exploration Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11106)
*Denis Bernard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将重整化群（renormalization group）重新诠释为一个随机过程（stochastic process），该过程逐尺度地探索物理相空间（phase space），并逐步揭示小尺度结构的细节。这一视角将重整化群与 Schramm-Loewner 演化等随机探索过程联系起来，并把 RG 变换、有效作用量等概念映射到条件期望值、鞅等概率论工具上，同时与随机量子化（stochastic quantization）建立关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重整化群是分析复杂物理系统的强大框架，但其传统表述对概率论背景的读者而言不够直观。本文旨在通过将 RG 重新诠释为随机过程，建立其与概率论领域已有随机探索方法（如 Schramm-Loewner 演化）的自然联系，从而提升该框架对概率学界受众的可及性。

Method: 本文将重整化群重新解读为一个逐尺度探索物理相空间的随机过程，在此基础上建立 RG 概念与概率论工具的对应关系：将 RG 变换、有效作用量等映射为条件期望值、鞅等概率工具，并进一步与随机量子化框架建立联系。

Result: 该随机过程视角成功建立了重整化群与其他随机探索过程（如 Schramm-Loewner 演化）之间的自然关联，并为概率论受众提供了一种更贴合其学科语言的重整化群表述方式。

Conclusion: 将重整化群视为概率意义上的随机过程，不仅揭示了其与 Schramm-Loewner 演化等经典随机工具的深层联系，也为理解 RG 变换、有效作用量的概率本质提供新路径，并将 RG 框架与随机量子化相衔接。

Abstract: The Renormalization Group (RG) is a powerful and versatile framework for analyzing complex physical systems. Here, we reinterpret it as a stochastic process that explores physical phase spaces, scale by scale, progressively revealing finer details of small-scale structures. This perspective establishes a natural connection to other random exploration processes, such as the Schramm-Loewner evolution, and is more suited for a probabilist audience. It links RG concepts such as RG transformations, effective actions, etc, to usual probabilistic tools such as conditional expectation values, martingales, etc, but also makes contact with stochastic quantization.

</details>


### [7] [Nonlinear systems of PDEs admitting infinite-dimensional Lie algebras and their connection with Ricci flows. II: The two-dimensional space case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11128)
*Roman Cherniha,John R. King*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文延续作者此前发表于Stud Appl Math. 2024年的工作，构造了所有允许无限维李代数（infinite-dimensional Lie algebra）的(1+2)维二阶偏微分方程双组分演化系统，证明将这一李代数自然推广到更高维并不会产生更一般的结果，因为无限维对称性会被破坏。研究还识别了与Ricci流相关的系统为其特例，并在径向对称情形下构建了该系统所有可能的稳态解和若干含时精确解，证明了在适当参数下解是有界且光滑的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续以往工作，系统构造所有具有无限维李代数的(1+2)维二阶演化系统，并探究将该李代数推广至更高维是否能够产生更一般的结果。

Method: 通过系统性分类方法构造所有允许无限维李代数的(1+2)维二阶偏微分方程双组分演化系统；利用约化常微分方程系统的李代数来分析径向对称情形下的稳态解与含时精确解。

Result: 证明无限维李代数向更高维的自然推广不会产生更一般结果；识别出与Ricci流相关的系统为所获演化系统中的一个特例；在径向对称情形下构造了该系统的所有稳态解及若干含时精确解；证明了约化系统的李代数可约化为含两个二阶常微分方程的最简系统的十五维代数。

Conclusion: 所构建的解在任意参数适当指定的条件下是有界且光滑的，表明系统的动态行为具有良定性（well-posedness）。

Abstract: The work is a natural continuation of that published in Stud Appl Math. 2024; 153:e12737. All possible two-components evolutions systems of (1+2)-dimensional second-order PDEs admitting an infinite-dimensional Lie algebra are constructed. It is shown that a natural generalisation of this Lie algebra to the higher-dimensional case does not lead to a more general result because the infinite-dimensional symmetry is broken. The recently derived system, which is related to Ricci flows, is identified as a very particular case among the evolution systems obtained. All possible stationary solutions of this system in the radially symmetric case are constructed using the surprisingly rich Lie algebra of the reduced system of ODEs. Moreover, it is proved that this Lie algebra is reducible to the fifteen-dimensional algebra of the simplest system of two second-order ODEs. Several time-dependent exact solutions in the radially symmetric case are constructed as well. It is shown that the solutions obtained are bounded and smooth provided arbitrary parameters are correctly specified.

</details>

### [8] [Nucleon electromagnetic form-factors in a minimal Gari-Krümpelmann model including the explicit two-pion continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10907)
*Wulf Krümpelmann,Yong-Hui Lin,Ulf-G. Meißner*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文在Gari–Krümpelmann（GK）唯象模型框架的基础上，将色散的两π介子连续谱（dispersive two-pion continuum）显式引入同位旋矢量道，通过仅5个参数即可描述质子电磁形状因子及电子-质子散射微分截面的众多实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Gari--Krümpelmann (GK) model provided one of the first semiphenomenological descriptions of the electromagnetic nucleon form-factors over a wide range of momentum transfer. The present work extends this framework by including the dispersive two-pion continuum explicitly in the isovector channel. The two-pion contribution is taken from modern dispersive analyses and embedded into the intrinsic high-$Q^2$ structure of the GK ansatz. The physical $ρ$ dynamics is then interpreted as part of the correlated two-pion spectral function. The direct coupling terms remain essential for the transition to the asymptotic regime. Conclusion: 延迟耦合网络中的同步行为可借助扩展后的主稳定函数框架加以分析和优化，异质性与非互惠性成为比传统同质全连接结构更有利的同步策略。

Abstract: Time delays are ubiquitous in physical and biological networked systems, playing a fundamental role in the emergence and stability of collective behavior. Yet, existing theoretical methods for synchronization analysis of delay-coupled systems are largely limited to networks with homogeneous degree distributions. Here, we extend the master stability function framework to a broad class of degree-heterogeneous, weighted, and directed delay-coupled networks. The analysis reveals that synchronization can be enhanced in heterogeneous networks compared to homogeneous ones, including all-to-all networks, which are known to be optimal in non-delayed systems. Our results show that, in delay-coupled networks, heterogeneity and nonreciprocity are key resources for synchronization.

Abstract: We compute the Casimir force between perfectly conducting parallel plates in a spacetime endowed with constant axial torsion. Working within an effective field theory framework where torsion couples to the electromagnetic sector via a gauge-invariant Chern--Simons-type interaction, we derive the modified photon dispersion relation and mode spectrum. Using zeta-function regularization, we obtain the vacuum energy and the resulting Casimir pressure to second order in the torsion parameter. Our calculation yields a correction scaling as $ΔP/P_0 = -\frac{5ξ^2 S_z^2 a^2}{4π^2} + \order(S_z^4)$, which corresponds to a slight weakening of the attractive Casimir force. We acknowledge a known subtlety in the literature: because the Chern-Simons interaction is a total derivative, some analyses conclude that it should not contribute to the Casimir energy for standard boundary conditions, implying the leading correction is of higher order, $\order(S_z^4)$. For experimentally accessible plate separations ($a \sim \qty{1}{\micro\meter}$), the effect remains well below current detection thresholds ($|ΔP/P_0| \lesssim 10^{-30}$) due to stringent bounds on macroscopic torsion from spin-torsion coupling experiments. Nevertheless, the calculation establishes a consistent, gauge-invariant bridge between quantum vacuum phenomena and non-Riemannian geometry. We discuss finite-temperature effects and geometric asymmetries as potential pathways for enhancing sensitivity, while placing the results in the broader context of dynamical torsion, condensed matter analogs, and quantum information protocols. Our results are consistent with the CPT-odd photon sector of the Standard-Model Extension and provide a geometric interpretation of Lorentz-violating coefficients in terms of spacetime torsion.

For the quartic potential, such regions can occur in both $R_n$ and $S_n$, whereas the DSSYK model can exhibit multiple transition regions in $R_n$, with the recursion function remaining accurate in the smooth intervals between them. Applying the Complexity-Action proposal, we demonstrate that a relative renormalization prescription extracts this same universal contribution from the Wheeler and DeWitt action. Abstract: This is a review paper intended to illustrate a few critical issues concerning Dirac, Weyl and Majorana fermions and their differences. The first part consists in basic introductions to fermions, and more in detail to fermions in 4d, focusing in particular in what differentiate the three type of fermions: chirality, helicity, mass, field equations, properties under discrete symmetry transformations, Actions, Observables. On this basis we tackle a series of challenging and sometime controversial problems: the non-existence of Weyl fermion propagators, and the ways to circumvent it; the regularizations for Weyl fermion amplitudes, in particular the appropriateness of using the PV regularization; the definition of a functional integral for Weyl fermion, the difference between Weyl and massless Majorana fermions and the difference between the Dirac and Majorana mass terms. Most notably we come to the conclusion that the trick of replacing the non-existing Weyl fermion propagator with a massless Dirac one, although it may yield in some cases correct results, is flawed at the very origin by a logical loophole. We then show which is the correct way to proceed in this case. We consider also the topic of applying the Wick rotation at the classical Action level for Weyl fermions and conclude that this procedure leads to a nonequivalent theory. Finally, although anomalies are not the central focus here, we have deemed it useful to summarily review the relation, and its cohomological basis, that exists between the lack of a Weyl fermion propagator (non-invertibility of the Weyl-Dirac operator) and the appearance of dangerous anomalies in the theory.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相场法（phase-field method）的三维有限元大变形数值框架，用于统一模拟纳米晶（nanocrystalline, NC）合金中的晶界溶质偏聚、溶质析出以及外加载荷对微观结构演化的影响，并通过多种案例验证了该框架的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为纳米晶合金领域提供了一个统一处理溶质晶界相互作用与力学效应的数值建模方案，弥补了此前文献中缺乏综合考虑溶质偏聚与力学耦合的完整模型之不足。

Abstract: Nanocrystalline (NC) alloys hold much promise as structural alloys due to their superior mechanical properties over traditional coarser grained microcrystalline alloys. Strength of metallic alloys is related to the underlying grain size - as represented by the classical Hall-Petch relation. Generally, a metals strength increases with decreasing mean grain size from the micrometer scale to the nanometer scale, until about a mean size of 20 nm. Any further decrease of grain size results in decreasing strength. Thus, there is an optimal range of mean grain size for most metals about which maximum material strength can be obtained. In the context of NC alloys, stabilization of the grain size in this optimal range is one of the primary synthesis challenges. Since nm-scale mean grain sizes are desired, phenomena like GB solute segregation and solute precipitation are utilized during alloy synthesis to mitigate grain growth. Numerical modeling the phenomena of GB-solute interactions and the evolution of these stabilized GBs under mechanical load are of immense interest to the NC alloy community. To enrich the numerical modeling formulations available in this space, we present here a phase-field method based numerical framework to model GB segregation, solute precipitation and effect of external loading on NC alloys. While some of these effects have been modeled in isolation, a unified treatment of the solute and GB segregation with mechanics interactions has not be considered in the literature. We present a 3D FEM finite-strain phase-field formulation for modeling grain evolution and microstructure stabilization. Beyond the formulation, various case studies demonstrate the applicability of this framework. Further, thermodynamic and kinetic arguments are provided based on the evolution of GB energy to explain the effects of solute drag, GB pinning and mechanical deformation.

</details>

Motivation: 理解关联量子物质需要准确描述低能相互作用的单粒子态（包括色散、能带几何、轨道成分和电荷密度）。然而在许多情况下，特别是摩尔材料（moiré materials）的拓扑能带中，找到少数局域轨道的实空间描述并不容易，因此需要系统的方法来识别最优局域自由度。

Result: CNO的本征值度量了目标能带中每个CNO的占据数，量化了跨单位胞边界的纠缠程度以及多轨道特征的重要性。作者通过构造扭转双层WSe₂的晶格模型验证了该方法，该模型能够追踪不同扭转角度下的轨道成分变化。

Abstract: Understanding correlated quantum matter starts with an accurate model of the single-particle states that interact at low energies: their dispersion, band geometry, orbital content and charge density. In many cases, notably the topological bands of moire materials, it is not straightforward to find a real-space description with a few local orbitals that accomplishes this task. Here we provide a systematic way to identify the local degrees of freedom that best capture the band geometry and charge density of any chosen set of bands. We use the unit-cell one-particle reduced density matrix (UC-1pRDM), obtained by restricting the projector onto the target bands to a single unit cell. Its eigenstates, which we call cell natural orbitals (CNOs), form a local, symmetric basis uniquely determined by the Bloch wavefunctions and the choice of real-space partition. Their eigenvalues measure the occupation of each CNO in the target bands, quantifying entanglement across unit-cell boundaries and the importance of multi-orbital character. A set of CNOs that maximizes total spectral weight and reproduces the target band symmetries provides optimal trial states for Wannierization. We exemplify this by constructing a lattice model for twisted bilayer WSe$_2$ that tracks the orbital content across twist angles.

Abstract: A recent work introduced the Simulator Collection for Atomic-to-Continuum Scales (SCACS) toolkit, a framework for improving finite element predictions of heat flow by mapping atom-resolved thermal conductivity into the stiffness matrix of the Galerkin finite element formulation [Ugwumadu et al., Phys. These include pronounced Rashba spin splitting, non-trivial Z2 topology, Berry-curvature-dipole-driven nonlinear anomalous Hall effect, and strain control of the Berry curvature dipole. Notably, the 4d transition-metal-based TiMoCO2 and 5d transition-metal-based TiWCO2, with M" elements from the same column of the periodic table, display markedly different behaviors. Conclusion: 4H-SnS2被确立为一种有前景的极化驱动电子与光电子材料体系，可用于非易失性存储器（nonvolatile memory）及光铁电（photoferroelectric）功能器件。

Piezoresponse force microscopy confirms room-temperature ferroelectricity in the 4H phase, while the 2H phase exhibits no ferroelectric response. The photoresponse likewise exhibits polarization-assisted carrier separation, sublinear power-law scaling, a nonmonotonic temperature response correlated with the characteristic Raman modes, ferroelectric hysteresis, and a pronounced dependence on the circular and linear polarization states of the incident laser beam. These results establish 4H-SnS2 as a promising material system for polarization-driven electronic and optoelectronic technologies, including nonvolatile memory and photoferroelectric functionalities.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入一个物理信息驱动的同步分析与设计框架，包含两部分组件：(i) 有限应变二次Hencky（对数应变）本构模型，其各向同性热特征应变在对数应变空间中允许精确的可加性分解；(ii) 钛-铜-钢材料系统中温度依赖的热导率、热膨胀系数和弹性模量。在三种设计温度下，分别采用基线模型和完整物理模型对热执行器和热夹持器进行优化，并施加质量和可制造性约束；随后用验证过的非线性有限元求解器在完整因素组合中对每个收敛设计进行重新评估。

Because a linear optimizer also steers away from the rotation-rich mechanisms that would expose this bias, the model can deceptively appear trustworthy when validated against its own designs. Designing with the full physics yields consistently stronger and more temperature-robust devices at a modest increase in design-time cost.

This requires an energy theory capable of extremely high throughput. Abstract: This thesis utilizes a multiscale method by connecting molecular dynamics (MD) based information to hydrodynamic macroscopic calculations for investigating the shock response and spallation of metals. Using particle swarm optimization (PSO), Nucleation and Growth (NAG) parameters are extracted for Cu, Nb, Mo, and Al, yielding free surface velocity (FSV) profiles matching experiments within $8\%$ for Al. Third, deformation across ten Al tilt (STGB) and twist (STwGB) bicrystals at rates of $10^{10}$-$10^{11}$ s$^{-1}$ proves that threshold spallation depends on boundary misorientation. Phase transitions near GBs lower spall strength at high $U_p$, whereas GB plasticity prolongs pull-back and delays spallation (e.g., $14.2^\circ$ STwGB). FSV methods are found to underestimate true peak tensile strength. Fourth, the framework is extended to polycrystals via 11 STGB and 12 STwGB configurations. Voids consistently nucleate at weakened GBs. Unique NAG parameters are fitted using PSO for all 23 bicrystals. An Average Void Growth in a Fluid Element (AVGFE) model is introduced to map these distinct boundary properties into 1D hydrodynamic codes. The combined model is validated against empirical Al flyer impacts at $518$, $1588$, and $2275$ m/s. The simulated temporal FSV curves mirror experiments, with spall strength deviations tightly bounded within $2.15\%$, $2.9\%$, and $6.0\%$, respectively, and spall thickness deviations within $2.4$-$4.0\%$.

Abstract: X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) probes the local chemical environment of the absorbing atom and is one of the most powerful characterization techniques for battery materials. Here we present a database of simulated oxygen K-edge XAS spectra for the layered cathode material LiNi0.8Mn0.1Co0.1O2 (NMC811), built on the atomic structures of our recent work[1]. All spectra were obtained using the excited electron and core-hole (XCH) method with the R2SCAN meta-GGA functional, as implemented in the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP). The database covers benchmark binary oxides (TiO, Ti2O3, TiO2, Mn3O4, Mn2O3, MnO2) together with a realistic NMC811 supercell containing 60 transition metal sites at three states of charge. Because each spectrum is resolved at the level of individual oxygen sites, the database links O K-edge spectral features to specific oxygen environments defined by their local coordination and transition metal neighbors. All data are freely available and can serve as a reference for spectral fingerprinting, for direct comparison with experiments, and as training data for machine learning models.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 工作流程筛选出209个超低功函数表面（低于2.0 eV）和227个超高功函数表面（高于6.0 eV），分别对应136种和172种独特材料。候选材料呈现的规律符合既有化学原理，例如碱金属和碱土金属端面倾向表现低功函数；同时发现新的结构特征——富含镧系元素（lanthanide）的表面终止与超低功函数强相关，而顶层含类金属（metalloid）或磷的表面与超高功函数相关。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种可扩展的策略，利用机器学习模型与多保真度计算结合，加速具有极端功函数材料的发现，可服务于先进电子、能量转换和催化应用。

Abstract: Work function plays a pivotal role in technologies ranging from energy conversion and electronics to catalysis. In this work, we integrated machine learning (ML) with multi-fidelity screening to develop a data-driven framework for accelerating the discovery of materials with extreme work functions. We augmented a previously published Random Forest (RF) model for work function to include prediction uncertainty calibration and domain of applicability assessment to enhance prediction robustness. By combining the augmented RF model with universal ML interatomic potential simulations and targeted ab initio calculations, we screened 5.5 million compounds from the GNoME and Alexandria databases. This workflow identified 209 surfaces with extreme low work functions below 2.0 eV and 227 surfaces with extreme high work functions above 6.0 eV, corresponding to 136 and 172 unique materials, respectively. The resulting candidates revealed trends consistent with established chemical principles, including the tendency of alkali- and alkaline-earth-terminated surfaces to exhibit low work functions. While it also uncovered less conventional motifs: lanthanide-rich surface terminations were strongly associated with extremely low work functions, whereas surfaces containing metalloids or phosphorus at the top layer were correlated with exceptionally high work functions. This work demonstrates a scalable strategy that leverages ML models and multi-fidelity computational efforts to accelerate the discovery of materials with extreme work functions for advanced electronic, energy-conversion, and catalytic applications.

Quantum transport regime observed in magnetoresistance is also independently confirmed by thermal conductivity measurements.

Abstract: Scintillator counters employing embedded wavelength-shifting fibers have been used in particle physics experiments for several decades. An advantage of arrays of triangular counters is that their position resolution can be greatly enhanced by interpolation between adjacent counters using their relative light yields. We report here on a testbeam study of the position resolution of such a scintillator hodoscope and compare the results to a simulation. We find an order of magnitude improvement in the position resolution over that found by simply using the fiber separation.

### [51] [An efficient and robust fifth-order HWENO scheme with gradient reconstruction for compressible Navier--Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10569)
*Zijun Guo,Chuan Fan,Zhuang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效稳健的五阶有限体积Hermite加权本质无振荡（HWENO）格式，通过为每个耗散变量构造弱导数矩并进行分量式HWENO重构来提供黏性通量所需的梯度，结合保正值限制器，实现对可压缩Navier–Stokes方程的高精度、鲁棒求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩Navier–Stokes方程需要同时高精度地处理对流项和黏性项，而传统直接微分会降低重构阶数，且强激波或低密度等极端流动场景对数值稳健性提出了挑战。

Method: 对每个耗散变量取两个HWENO界面迹线的算术平均构造弱导数矩，再经标准标量非线性HWENO重构公式按分量处理，仅输入矩与对流重构不同；同时引入保正值限制器（positivity-preserving limiter）作用于守恒态，而黏性梯度仍保留非线性HWENO重构。

Result: 数值实验表明该格式达到五阶精度和高分辨率，计算效率具有竞争力，并能稳健求解有限雷诺数（finite-Reynolds-number）Navier–Stokes流，即使在保正值限制器被频繁触发的情况下仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该格式只需最高四次候选多项式即可实现五阶精度，避免了直接微分导致的阶数退化，且额外计算成本可忽略；保守重构与梯度重构共享相同紧凑模板和候选多项式结构，便于单一实现，不增加算法复杂性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an efficient and robust fifth-order finite-volume Hermite weighted essentially non-oscillatory (HWENO) scheme with gradient reconstruction for the compressible Navier--Stokes equations. The key idea is to construct weak-derivative moments for each dissipative variable by taking the arithmetic average of two HWENO interface traces, which are then processed componentwise through the standard scalar nonlinear HWENO reconstruction formula, with only the input moments differing from those used in the convective reconstruction, thereby supplying the gradients needed for the viscous fluxes. This reconstruction achieves fifth-order accuracy using only candidate polynomials of degree at most four, avoids the order reduction that would result from direct differentiation, and introduces negligible additional computational cost. To ensure robustness, especially in extreme test cases involving strong shocks or low densities, we apply a positivity-preserving limiter to the conservative states while retaining the nonlinear HWENO reconstruction for the viscous gradients. This combination is essential for stable computations in flows with strong gradients. Both the conservative and gradient reconstructions share the same compact stencils and candidate-polynomial structure, enabling a single implementation routine without introducing additional algorithmic complexity. Numerical results confirm that the proposed scheme delivers fifth-order accuracy and high resolution, offers competitive computational efficiency, and robustly resolves challenging finite-Reynolds-number Navier--Stokes flows in which the positivity-preserving limiter is actively engaged.

</details>

### [52] [High-energy electron-positron beam collisions with large-angle disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10988)
*W. Zhang,T. Grismayer,L. O. Silva*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在高能电子-正电子（e^-e^+）对撞中引入新无量纲参数ε，用以表征束流与场动力学行为。研究发现ε≳1时存在全新的极端物理机制，其中横向运动趋于强相对论性，制动效应（braking effect）完全阻止并反转束流传播；该理论模型与电磁粒子云模拟（particle-in-cell simulation）高度一致，而传统束束研究仅适用于ε≪1的弱区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统束束（beam-beam）效应研究及其遗留数值代码仅在弱扰动参数（ε≪1）下有效，无法准确描述高能对撞中大角度扰动情形下的束流行为与碰撞亮度（luminosity）。因此需要提出新的描述框架，识别并研究超越传统适用范围的新极端物理机制。

Method: 作者引入无量纲参数ε来刻画束流偏转角：偏转效应（disruption effect）使粒子横向偏转角等于ε，纵向速度减速（制动效应）正比于ε²，由此激发的纵向电场幅度正比于ε。基于该参数构建理论模型，并与电磁粒子云模拟结果进行对比验证。

Result: 理论模型与电磁粒子云模拟高度吻合。在ε≳1的大角度偏转区域，横向运动进入强相对论性（strongly relativistic）状态，制动效应完全停止并反转束流传播方向。同时，传统束束研究及遗留数值代码在此区域内显著高估束束效应（包括束致辐射（beamstrahlung）和粒子对产生（pair production）），无法捕捉正确的束流特征与碰撞亮度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示ε≳1构成一个全新的极端物理区域，现有束束模型在此失效，必须采用全电磁粒子云数值代码来正确研究该区域的动力学与碰撞物理。这一结论对高能对撞机物理设计及相关效应评估具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We show that the beam and field dynamics in high-energy electron-positron ($e^-e^+$) collisions are characterized by a new dimensionless parameter introduced as $\varepsilon$ in this study. The disruption effect deflects the particles transversely at angles equal to $\varepsilon$. The particles simultaneously undergo deceleration of longitudinal velocities (a ``braking effect"). The deceleration scales as $\propto \varepsilon^2$. A longitudinal electric field is further provoked, whose amplitude scales as $\propto \varepsilon$. We identify $\varepsilon \gtrsim 1$ (with large-angle disruptions) as a novel extreme regime, where the transverse motion becomes strongly relativistic. The braking effect completely stops and further reverses the beam propagation. Our theoretical model is in excellent agreement with electromagnetic particle-in-cell simulations. The previous beam-beam studies, including legacy numerical codes, apply only to the $\varepsilon \ll 1$ regime. They fail to capture the correct beam features and collision luminosities, and overestimate beam-beam effects (including beamstrahlung and pair production) for considerable $\varepsilon$, thus demonstrating the need for fully electromagnetic particle-in-cell codes to study these regimes.

</details>

### [53] [Signatures of auxeticity in microgels at low and ultralow crosslinker concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10269)
*Susana Marín-Aguilar,Leah Rank,Emanuela Zaccarelli*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过计算机模拟（in silico simulations），研究了超低交联浓度（ultralow crosslinking, ULC）下热响应微凝胶（thermoresponsive microgels）在体积相变（volume phase transition）过程中的力学响应，发现约1%交联浓度的微凝胶在体积相变附近呈现拉胀行为（auxetic behavior），且超低交联微凝胶在整个溶胀态（swollen regime）均表现出轻微负泊松比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉胀行为（负泊松比）是一种反直觉的力学响应，在某些聚合物网络中已有报道，但在超低交联浓度微凝胶这一此前未探索的体系中尚不清楚其力学响应特性。作者旨在填补这一空白，并通过模拟预测引导未来实验研究。

Method: 采用计算机模拟方法考察不同交联浓度（直至超低条件）的热响应微凝胶在体积相变中的力学响应；针对超低交联微凝胶极为稀疏且形状各向异性的特点，改进了基于平衡形状涨落（equilibrium shape fluctuations）估算弹性模量的方法，并进一步分析了网络内部区域的拉胀响应。

Result: 交联浓度约1%的微凝胶在体积相变附近出现拉胀行为；超低交联微凝胶在整个溶胀态均呈现轻微负泊松比；对网络内部区域的分析表明，超低交联微凝胶的拉胀响应在所有长度尺度上均存在，提示其为弱连接聚合物网络的固有属性。

Conclusion: 超低交联微凝胶中的拉胀行为是其弱连接网络结构的本质属性，这一数值预测可能为测量单个低交联及超低交联微凝胶泊松比分数的实验研究提供新的方向。

Abstract: Auxetic behavior, characterized by a negative Poisson's ratio, is a counterintuitive mechanical response exhibited, among other systems, by certain polymer networks. Here, through in silico simulations we investigate the mechanical response of thermoresponsive microgels across the volume phase transition upon varying crosslinker concentration down to ultralow conditions, a regime so far unexplored.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文采用综述性分析方法，从液线断裂这一经典例子出发，将有限时间奇点问题统一到软物质的广义框架中。核心思路是考察奇点附近动力学是否呈现自相似（self-similar）collapse——即不同时刻的剖面在按收缩局部长度重新标度后重合——并分析该自相似行为的普适性边界，以及外围流场和替代理想散度的小尺度物理如何共同决定可测量输出。

Conclusion: 奇点的重要性在于其标志着连续介质理论不再适用的边界，并揭示小尺度截断如何决定印刷、涂覆、气溶胶和可拉伸固体等实际应用中的关键输出。收缩区在奇点处既遗忘外部环境的某些细节（体现普适性），也保留由供料流动和小尺度物理赋予的依赖，这正是理解软物质奇点现象的统一视角。

Abstract: When a liquid thread pinches off, its neck thins as it separates into two unconnected regions. Using continuum mechanics, we can predict that the neck reaches zero radius in finite time while its curvature grows without bound. Together, the vanishing neck and diverging curvature form a finite-time singularity. However, a real fluid does not realise these mathematical limits as molecular or material physics takes over once the neck becomes sufficiently small. Similar singularities arise throughout soft matter whenever a smooth continuum description is used at vanishing length scales. This review asks what the shrinking region forgets, what it retains, and which material length, time, or stress cuts off the apparent divergence. The dynamics near a singularity often become self-similar, with profiles at different times collapsing onto one shape when rescaled by the shrinking local length. Sometimes that collapse is universal enough that the surrounding geometry and forcing no longer determine the local dynamics. Nonetheless, the measured output could still depend on how the shrinking region is fed by the surrounding flow and on the small-scale physics that finally replaces the ideal divergence. Complex fluids and active matter change the same local balance by bringing their own timescales into the shrinking region. Beyond interfaces, the same logic applies when the localised object is a stress concentration or a defect in geometry or order rather than a moving surface. Singularities matter because they show where continuum theory stops being the relevant description and how the small-scale cutoff sets the outputs that count in printing, coating, aerosols, and stretchable solids.

</details>

Abstract: This study deals with both spontaneously-formed and electrically-induced structures observed in the nematic phase of two dielectrically negative twist-bend nematogens. Above a few hertz, flexoelectric instability ceases; the sequence of patterned states then is oblique roll to bimodal-grid to normal-roll to chevron. Our measurement of threshold voltage corresponding to different frequencies (or electrical conductivity and permittivity values) in this regime agrees well with the earlier theoretical predictions relating to the inertial conduction instability.

Abstract: Traditional models for stress-strain behavior of glassy polymers are based on the assumption that the critical features of the stress-strain response can be explained by changes in the molecular mobility. By performing direct optical experiments using the photobleaching technique simultaneously with the mechanical four-step experiments it is shown that a lower molecular mobility upon partial unloading does not take place. As traditional models cannot account for these experimental results, a new model has been developed where the changes of molecular structure manifest not in the relaxation time, but in the shear modulus, which is function of an internal variable that is the fraction of the efficiently packed material. This fraction obeys a population balance equation, where the steady-state fraction is controlled by the applied stress. In the absence of deformation, the efficiently packed fraction increases, which explains the increase in the modulus in the course of physical aging below Tg. The model qualitatively describes the four-step experiment as well as single step loading experiments.

Abstract: Amorphous solids subjected to athermal quasistatic oscillatory shear undergo a transition from periodic reversible dynamics to irreversible diffusive dynamics at yielding. How irreversibility arises in such deterministic, dissipative dynamics remains unclear. Here we show that trajectories remain locally stable, with perturbations decaying rather than growing even in the irreversible regime, ruling out the sustained exponential sensitivity to initial conditions associated with chaotic dynamics. Rather than diverging continuously, nearby trajectories initially remain close before eventually separating through rare branching events, after which their separation grows diffusively. A mean-field soft-spot model reproduces the same branching statistics and reveals their microscopic origin. We find that branching originates from competition between nearly-degenerate plastic instabilities, in which a small perturbation changes which instability activates first and thereby alters the subsequent sequence of plastic events. These results identify instability-selection-induced branching as a dynamical mechanism for irreversibility in cyclically driven amorphous solids.

Abstract: Ultra-heavy asymmetric dark matter (DM) particles captured by compact stars can thermalize, self-gravitate, and collapse to form an endoparasitic black hole (EBH), whose subsequent growth may transmute the host star into a black hole. The continued existence of old millisecond pulsars (MSPs) and white dwarfs (WDs) thus places powerful constraints on the DM particle mass $m_χ$ and the DM-nucleon scattering cross-section $σ_{\rm nχ}$. We derive an analytical expression for the transmutation timescale by solving the EBH growth equation, consistently accounting for Bondi accretion of stellar matter, Hawking evaporation, and sustained DM feeding of the EBH in a steady-state capture regime. We also incorporate quantum effects in baryonic accretion when the hydrodynamic description breaks down, providing a unified treatment of EBH growth across both particle and fluid regimes. Adopting a physically transparent collapse criterion for fermionic and bosonic asymmetric DM, we compute EBH transmutation timescales for representative MSPs and WDs in environments with different DM densities. Although the physical ingredients are broadly similar to previous studies, this work derives updated constraints through a closed-form analytical treatment of EBH growth and an adopted prescription for the EBH formation timescale, yielding a lower critical EBH mass for sustained growth and revised transmutation timescales. Requiring the transmutation time to exceed $\sim 1$ Gyr for MSPs and $\sim 10$ Gyr for WDs, we derive revised constraints on $σ_{\rm nχ}$ over $m_χ\sim 10^{6}$--$10^{14}$ GeV, and show that EBHs with initial masses as small as $\sim 4\times10^{4}$ kg can undergo sustained growth, extending the region of DM parameter space probed by compact stars.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 核加热显著影响抛射物动力学并延迟均匀膨胀（homologous expansion）；对长寿命残余天体（long-lived remnants）的非对称双星系统影响最大，使含90%重元素质量的极区角度从约15度拓宽至约30度。确认56Ni→56Co→56Fe衰变链在约100天主导加热，钴衰变产生846.77和1238.288 keV的伽马射线谱线。三维与二维平均光变曲线对比显示二维结果大致稳健但应视为上限，增加维度普遍降低热光度（bolometric luminosity），且单纯提高维度不太可能使理论与AT2017gfo观测一致。

Conclusion: 长时间三维模拟揭示了核加热对抛射物动力学和重元素空间分布的重要影响，但增加维度本身不足以解释当前理论模型与AT2017gfo观测的偏差，未来需在其他方面改进模型以弥合理论与观测的差距。

Abstract: We present long-term, three-dimensional simulations of ejecta from four binary neutron star mergers to second-long time-scales. Numerical-relativity data serve as boundary conditions for a general-relativistic hydrodynamics evolution incorporating an equation of state valid outside nuclear statistical equilibrium and an effective nuclear-heating prescription based on reaction-network calculations. We investigate the ejecta's dynamical and geometrical properties, the impact of nuclear heating, the formation and spatial distribution of elements, and compute multi-angle kilonova light curves. %
Nuclear heating significantly affects ejecta dynamics, delaying homologous expansion beyond second time-scales and reshapes the spatial distribution of heavy nuclei. This effect is largest for asymmetric binaries with long-lived remnants; extending the evolution from ${\sim}150$~ms to ${\sim}1$~s widens the angular polar region containing 90\% of the heavy-element mass (\eg, $Z=56$, $Z=79$) from $|θ|\lesssim15^{o}$ to $|θ|\lesssim30^{o}$. Our nucleosynthesis results confirm that the $^{56}$Ni$\rightarrow^{56}$Co$\rightarrow^{56}$Fe decay chain dominates the heating at $\sim$100 days, with cobalt decay producing gamma-ray lines at 846.77 and 1238.288~keV. %
Comparing kilonova ray-by-ray light curves obtained from multi-angle 3D profiles and averaged 2D profiles, we find the latter approach broadly robust, although 2D light curves should be treated as upper limits. Increasing dimensionality generally lowers the bolometric luminosity, with binary asymmetry strengthening the viewing-angle dependence. For observers aligned with a lanthanide curtain's densest region, 3D emission can match its 2D counterpart in brightness. We conclude that increased dimensionality alone is unlikely to reconcile current theoretical models with AT2017gfo observations.

Abstract: An asymmetric relativistic outflow from a magnetar giant flare produces a step-like gravitational-wave (GW) memory. In the magnetized pair plasma surrounding the star, such GW memory drives transverse electromagnetic (EM) X-mode/fast-magnetosonic responses at the corresponding frequencies. We use this GW-memory spectrum to drive a global wave equation for a cold electron--positron pair plasma. With radiative boundary conditions excluding incoming waves, the driven response forms an outgoing EM mode that reaches the outer boundary of the source region. For central-engine energy-release timescales of $10$, $1$, and $0.15\,μ\mathrm{s}$, 90\% of the integrated response energy lies below 23.3 kHz, 233 kHz, and 1.51 MHz, respectively. The principal response occupies the 10 kHz--1 MHz frequency band; a substantial extension into the 1--3 MHz frequency band also appears when a submicrosecond asymmetric-energy component is present. The electromagnetic response can escape from the source region and potentially propagate in the interstellar medium.

Method: 作者基于广义能量均分原理构建了一个确定平衡分布的框架。该框架在弱非线性区域能够恢复已有修正结果，其关键在于对强非线性情况下相关微扰贡献的处理，使得方法在传统方法失效的强非线性区域仍然有效。

Abstract: Obtaining equilibrium distributions of nonlinear systems is essential for accurately computing macroscopic observables. Conventional theoretical corrections are typically limited to weak nonlinearities, where interaction terms can be treated as effectively uncorrelated perturbations and the random phase approximation applies. In this Letter, we develop a framework to determine equilibrium distributions based on the generalized energy equipartition principle. Our approach recovers existing corrections in the weakly nonlinear regime and, crucially, remains valid for strong nonlinearities, where perturbative contributions become correlated and conventional approaches break down. Numerical simulations of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, the Majda-McLaughlin-Tabak model, and the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou model demonstrate accurate corrections for nonlinearities more than an order of magnitude stronger than those accessible to conventional theories.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFPT是物理、化学和生物系统中输运、反应、搜索及切换过程的普适时间度量，理解其对外部扰动的响应对于预测和控制至关重要，但此前缺乏系统性的理论框架。

Method: 建立了一个紧凑的理论框架，核心工具是精确对应关系，将MFPT本质上瞬态的响应映射为辅助系统的稳态响应。基于此推导出任意识态对之间MFPT的精确普适响应关系，并实现响应分解与若干推论。

Result: 获得了MFPT响应的因子化物理分解（线性上游、线性下游及非线性贡献），并推导出响应曲线推断规则、MFPT响应的基本界限、高阶响应的解析表达式及多速率响应公式；同时发现该方法在计算MFPT和稳态分布上具有计算优势。

Conclusion: 通过分析一个生物启发的折叠网络验证了理论，并澄清了近期报道中关于MFPT的一个悖论，证明该框架具有普适性与实际应用价值。

Abstract: The mean first-passage time (MFPT) provides a universal temporal measure of transport, reaction, search, and switching processes in physical, chemical, and biological systems. Understanding how MFPTs respond to perturbations is therefore crucial for prediction and control, yet a systematic theory has been lacking. We establish a compact theoretical framework for linear and nonlinear MFPT response in continuous-time Markov processes. The key tool is an exact correspondence that maps the intrinsically transient response of MFPTs onto the steady-state response of an auxiliary system. This correspondence yields exact and universal response relations for MFPTs between arbitrary state pairs, expressed entirely in terms of unperturbed MFPTs and steady-state probabilities. We then obtain a factorized physical decomposition of the MFPT response into linear upstream, linear downstream, and nonlinear contributions. Further corollaries include response-curve inference rules, fundamental bounds on MFPT responses, analytical expressions for higher-order responses of MFPTs and steady-state probabilities, and multi-rate response formulas. Additionally, our result offers computational advantages in calculating both MFPTs and steady-state distributions. Finally, a biologically motivated folding network is analyzed, and a recently reported paradox on MFPT is clarified.

Motivation: 此前研究已识别出追踪子机制（tracer mechanism）并预言了畴壁态和零磁化情形下的标度指数与极限形状，但连续时间过程的振幅参数仍然缺失。本文旨在从微观XNOR动力学出发预测这些缺失的振幅，并将图像推广至均匀有偏初态。

Method: 从微观XNOR随机动力学出发，推导长时间自旋电流分布的关键振幅参数；通过提出三个带有显式振幅的猜想（高斯极限、半正态极限、M-Wright极限），并利用无拟合参数的数值模拟验证各猜想。

Result: 三个猜想均获得数值支持：非零磁化均匀初态在t^{1/4}尺度上呈现高斯极限；相反磁化畴壁态在t^{1/4}尺度上呈现半正态极限；零磁化均匀初态在t^{1/8}尺度上呈现M-Wright极限。此外，无拟合参数的模拟验证了预测的振幅正确性。

Conclusion: 本文为随机XNOR过程中的自旋电流长时间涨落提供了完整的定量描述，填补了此前理论框架中缺失的振幅参数，并扩展至更一般的均匀有偏初态情形。附带的数学论文提供了AI生成的候选证明，展现了生成式AI在研究工作中的潜在辅助作用。

Abstract: We consider fluctuations of the spin current in the stochastic XNOR process, a kinetically constrained hopping model in one spatial dimension. We formulate three conjectures with explicit amplitudes for the long-time current distributions: a Gaussian limit on the $t^{1/4}$ scale for homogeneous initial states with nonzero magnetization, a half-normal limit on the $t^{1/4}$ scale for domain-wall states with opposite magnetizations, and an M-Wright limit on the $t^{1/8}$ scale for homogeneous initial states at zero magnetization. Earlier work identified the tracer mechanism and anticipated the scaling exponents and limiting shapes in the domain-wall and zero-magnetization settings. Starting from the microscopic XNOR dynamics, we predict the missing amplitudes for the continuous-time process and extend the picture to homogeneous biased initial data. Simulations provide numerical support for all three conjectures without fitted parameters. A separate mathematical companion paper presents an extensively AI-generated candidate proof. Generative-AI tools were used in the research and writing workflow.

</details>


### [69] [Steady-state phase transition in one-dimensional hybrid contact process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10948)
*Lin Shang,Shuai Geng,Xingli Li,Jiasen Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维混合接触过程（hybrid contact process）中的稳态相变，通过有效场框架下的单点与团簇平均场近似揭示了系统可能的全部稳态，观测到稳定吸收相、活性相及双稳态区域的共存，并借助相干异常方法（coherent anomaly method）提取了连续相变的临界点和临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合接触过程作为一类非平衡系统模型，其稳态相变行为（尤其是连续与不连续相变的共存及临界标度性质）尚不清楚，现有方法难以准确获取其非经典临界指数。

Method: 采用基于有效场的单点平均场（single-site mean-field）与团簇平均场（cluster mean-field）近似方法，枚举系统的全部可能稳态；利用相干异常方法（coherent anomaly method）提取真实临界点及临界指数，以刻画连续相变的非经典标度行为。

Result: 系统在长时间极限下存在稳定的吸收相与活性相，以及双稳态区域；吸收相与双稳态区域之间的边界由鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation）控制，对应不连续相变；而吸收相到活性相的转变则是连续的。

Conclusion: 一维混合接触过程展现混合性质的相变行为：既存在不连续（一阶）相变，也存在连续（二阶）相变；后者通过相干异常方法获得的临界指数呈现非经典标度行为，为理解此类非平衡相变机制提供了定量依据。

Abstract: We investigate the steady-state phase transition in a one-dimensional hybrid contact process. We implement the single-site and cluster mean-field approximations based on the effective fields and present all the possible steady states of the system. We show the existence of the stable absorbing and active phases, and the bistable region in the long-time limit. The saddle-node bifurcation is observed at the boundary between the absorbing phase and the bistable region, suggesting a discontinuous phase transition. While the absorbing to active phase transition is continuous. To characterize the nonclassical scaling behavior of the continuous phase transition, we extract the true critical points and exponents by means of the coherent anomaly method.

</details>


### [70] [Jamming transition in an active exclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11041)
*Kavita Jain,Sakuntala Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过一维活性粒子（active particles）硬芯相互作用模型，结合蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）和平均场理论（mean field theory），研究了活性对堵塞相变（jamming transition）的影响。主要发现是活性会阻碍甚至抑制堵塞；堵塞转变的临界密度随切换率（switching rate）降低而减小，当切换率足够小时系统只存在流体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明活性可以诱导或抑制相变，或改变被动系统的临界行为，但活性对堵塞相变这一被动系统非平衡相变的典型范例的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 考虑一维具有硬芯相互作用和可逆转自推进方向的活性粒子系统，粒子的自推进方向以给定切换率反转；当切换率趋于无穷大时模型退化为一类被动系统。采用蒙特卡洛模拟和平均场理论，研究粒子的平均迁移率（mean mobility）和空穴团簇分布（hole cluster distribution）随密度和有限切换率的变化。

Result: 主要结果是活性阻碍堵塞的形成，甚至可能完全抑制堵塞。具体而言，堵塞相变发生的临界密度随切换率的减小而降低；当切换率足够小时，系统仅存在于流体相，不存在堵塞相。

Conclusion: 活性能够通过抑制堵塞相的形成来改变被动系统的堵塞相变行为，这一发现表明活性机制可有效调控非平衡系统中的相变特性。

Abstract: Multiple studies on active matter have shown that activity can induce or suppress a phase transition, or modify the critical behavior of a passive system. Here we investigate how activity affects the jamming transition which is a paradigmatic example of nonequilibrium phase transitions in passive systems. We consider a one-dimensional system of active particles with hardcore interactions and a direction of self-propulsion which can be reversed at a given switching rate. For a class of particle hop rates and infinite switching rate, our model reduces to a passive system which is known to exhibit a transition between a high-density fluid phase and a low-density jammed phase in the stationary state. Using Monte Carlo simulations and a mean field theory, we study how the mean mobility of the particle and the hole cluster distribution vary with density and finite switching rate. Our main result is that activity hinders the formation of jam and can even inhibit it; more precisely, we find that the jamming transition occurs at a critical density that decreases with decreasing switching rate, and at sufficiently small switching rate, the system exists only in the fluid phase.

</details>


### [71] [Exact Expressions of Entropy for Classical Non-interacting Many-body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11104)
*Yuheng Wu,Henrik Heelweg,Rigel Galgana*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种普适系综理论（universal ensemble theory），仅基于平稳性（stationarity）和无偏性（unbiasedness）两条公设，即可为任意尺寸的经典非相互作用多体系统在各统计系综中推导出精确的熵表达式，并证明各系综下的熵表达式在大系统极限下收敛到一致的大偏差理论（large deviation theory）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学极限下各系综等价，但有限尺寸系统中系综不等价性显著，需要一种能够严格量化有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）的普适系综理论，为任意尺寸系统提供精确的熵表达式及全部平衡热力学量的精确值。

Method: 提出一个基于两条公设的理论框架：平稳性公设要求物理定律在时间平移下保持不变，无偏性公设要求平衡混合态在给定约束下最大化熵。在此框架下推导任意尺寸经典非相互作用多体系统在各统计系综中的精确熵表达式。

Result: 得到了各系综下精确的熵表达式；进一步证明这些表达式在不同系综中收敛到共同的渐近形式 S ∽ ln(((4πm e E)/(3N))^(3N/2)·(V/N)^N)+N，与大偏差理论的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该理论为任意尺寸的经典非相互作用多体系统提供了统一的系综描述框架，精确刻画了有限尺寸系统中的系综不等价性，并在热力学极限下与大偏差理论结果自洽。

Abstract: In the thermodynamic limit, the equilibrium state of a many-body system can be characterized by three pairs of conjugate thermodynamic variables: $E/T,V/P,N/μ$. In this limit, the thermodynamic properties in all ensembles are equivalent up to the leading order of $E,V,N$. However, for systems of finite size, this ensemble equivalence is no longer exact, and the thermodynamic properties may differ substantially among ensembles. To quantify these finite-size effects rigorously, it is desirable to develop a universal ensemble theory applicable to systems of arbitrary size, providing exact expressions for entropy and, thereby, giving rise to the precise value of all equilibrium thermodynamic quantities. In this work, we propose a theory that determines the exact entropy expressions for classical non-interacting many-body systems of arbitrary size across all statistical ensembles, based on only two postulates: \textbf{stationarity}, requiring that the physical laws be invariant under time translation, and \textbf{unbiasedness}, requiring that the equilibrium mixed state maximize the entropy subject to the prescribed constraints. Moreover, we show that the entropy expressions obtained in different ensembles converge to the common asymptotic form $S \asymp \ln\!\left( \left( \frac{4πm e E}{3N} \right)^{3N/2} \cdot \left(\frac{V}{N}\right)^N \right)+N$, consistent with the predictions of the large deviation theory.

</details>

### [72] [T1, T2, and complex permittivities of hydrogels, paramagnetic salt solutions, and oils at 0.35, 1.5, and 3 Tesla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11117)
*H Michael Gach,Kerri Prinos,Hannah Sechaga*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 该研究系统测量了多种MRI体模（phantom）候选材料的T1、T2弛豫时间及复介电常数，评估其在不同磁场强度和浓度下的磁学与电学特性，为高场MRI体模的构建提供材料选择依据。结果表明钠藻酸盐、Miller氏LB琼脂和黄原胶是优质水凝胶候选材料，Mn(NO3)2具有最高弛豫率，三种植物油适用于低介电常数高场应用体模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁共振成像（MRI）体模的制备需要精确控制弛豫时间和介电性质，但现有材料选择缺乏系统性表征数据。候选材料（水凝胶、顺磁电解质和油类）的T1、T2弛豫率及介电特性与磁场强度和浓度的依赖关系尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 研究者测量了候选MRI体模组分（水凝胶、顺磁电解质和油类）在不同磁场强度和浓度下的T1、T2驰豫时间及复介电常数，并通过对比弛豫率随浓度的变化规律来评估各材料的适用性。

Result: 除聚乙二醇（PEG）外，T1随浓度增加而降低；PEG、聚乙烯吡咯烷酮（PVP）、聚丙烯酸钠和去离子水的T1与T2弛豫率不随场强显著变化，明胶和藻酸钠的T2弛豫率亦与场强无关。所有水凝胶和顺磁电解质溶液具有高静态介电常数（εs≈70），且多数材料的电导率随浓度上升，但PEG和PVP例外。Mn(NO3)2弛豫率最高，三种油类的静态介电常数低于10。

Conclusion: 聚丙烯酸钠、PVP和PEG水凝胶难以制备一致性体模；藻酸钠、Miller氏LB琼胶和黄原胶是良好水凝胶候选材料。Mn(NO3)2具有最高的弛豫率。菜籽油、蓖麻油和葡萄籽油适合作为低介电常数（εs<10）高场应用体模材料，但其电导率较低。

Abstract: Approach: The T1, T2, and complex permittivities of candidate MRI phantom constituents (hydrogels, paramagnetic electrolytes, and oils) were measured at different magnetic field strengths and concentrations. Data: T1 decreased with increasing concentration except for PEG. The T1 and T2 relaxivities of polyethylene glycol (PEG), polyvinylpyrrolidone (PVP), sodium polyacrylate, and deionized water did not significantly vary with field strength. The T2 relaxivities of gelatin and sodium alginate did not significantly vary with field strength. All of the hydrogels and paramagnetic electrolyte solutions had high static dielectrics (e.g., εs~70) similar to water. The static electrical conductivities of Miller's LB agar, gelatin, PVA, sodium alginate, sodium polyacrylate, xanthan gum, CuSO4, NiCl2, and Mn(NO3)2 rose with concentration. However, the conductivities of PEG and PVP did not rise with concentration. Conclusions: Sodium polyacrylate, PVP, and PEG hydrogels are challenging for generating consistent phantoms. Sodium alginate, Miller's LB agar, and xanthan gum were good hydrogel candidates. Mn(NO3)2 had the highest relaxivities of the tested samples. All three oils (canola, castor, and grapeseed) are good candidates for low dielectric (εs<10) phantoms for high field applications) but with low conductivities.

</details>

### [73] [Analytic Boundary Terms for Arbitrary Crystal Geometries and Direct-Sum Evaluation of Madelung Constants in Triclinic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10041)
*Yang He,Zhonghan Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了Madelung常数直接求和（direct-sum evaluation）中形状依赖边界项的闭式解析表达式，适用于任意晶体几何。对于一般的三斜晶格（triclinic lattice），该边界项精确映射为带电平行四边形集产生的静电势；同时证明了有限尺寸修正项随特征尺寸p按(2p+1)^{-2}衰减。基于此发展了用于任意三斜晶格Madelung常数精确计算的稳健直接求和法，并在代表性Bravais晶格（Bravais lattice）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Madelung常数的直接求和计算受到格子求和（lattice sums）条件收敛（conditional convergence）的困扰，由此产生的边界项依赖于晶体形状。作者旨在解决这一长期存在的数学困难，为任意晶体几何提供边界项的解析表达，从而建立精确且稳健的直接求和计算方法。

Method: 作者推导出边界项的闭式解析表达式（closed-form analytic expression），该表达式对任意晶体几何均有效。对于三斜晶格的一般情况，证明边界项精确对应于一组成均匀带电平行四边形产生的静电势。同时推导了特征尺寸p的晶体的有限尺寸修正的衰减行为为(2p+1)^{-2}，并据此构建了直接求和算法。

Result: 成功获得任意晶体几何下边界项的闭合解析形式；证明了在一般三斜晶格中该边界项与均匀带电平行四边形静电势的精确对应关系；确定了有限尺寸修正以(2p+1)^{-2}的速率衰减；开发了适用于任意三斜晶格Madelung常数精确计算的直接求和法，并在代表性Bravais晶格上通过显式计算验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次解决了Madelung常数直接求和中条件收敛带来的形状依赖边界项的解析表征难题，为任意三斜晶格的Madelung常数提供了精确且稳健的直接求和计算框架，并在代表性Bravais晶格上获得了验证。

Abstract: The direct-sum evaluation of Madelung constants is complicated by the conditional convergence of lattice sums, which gives rise to a shape-dependent boundary term. In this work, we present, for the first time, a closed-form analytic expression for this boundary term that is valid for arbitrary crystal geometries. For general triclinic lattices, this boundary term maps exactly onto the electrostatic potential generated by a set of uniformly charged parallelograms. In addition, we demonstrate that the residual finite-size correction for a crystal of characteristic size $p$ decays as $(2p+1)^{-2}$. Building on these results, we develop a robust direct-sum method for the accurate computation of Madelung constants in arbitrary triclinic lattices and validate its effectiveness through explicit calculations on representative Bravais lattices.

</details>

*Inna M. Vishik*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米气体模型是理解真实金属中复杂电子行为的重要出发点，本文旨在利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）这一实验技术直观呈现该模型，以辅助教学和理解。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）技术，基于光电效应（photoelectric effect）测量固体中电子的能量与动量关系（energy vs momentum relations），并将实验结果作为费米气体模型的直观展示工具。

Result: 通过ARPES实验获得了固体中电子能量-动量关系的直接测量结果，以实验可视化方式呈现了费米气体模型的核心物理图像。

Conclusion: ARPES技术能够作为可视化费米气体模型的有效教学工具，该模型是进一步理解真实金属中复杂电子行为的理论基础。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) can measure electrons' energy vs momentum relations in solids via photoelectric effect. This pedagogical paper experimental results from this technique as a tool for visualizing the Fermi gas model in quantum statistical mechanics. This model is an important starting point for understanding more complex electronic behaviors in real metals.

</details>


### [75] [Future perspective of muons; a quantum particle measuring quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10856)
*Adam Berlie,Sayani Biswas,Alex Louat,Rhea Stewart,John Wilkinson*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文是一篇展望性（perspective）论文，聚焦于将μ子视为量子粒子来开展μ子谱学（muon spectroscopy）研究，强调这一量子处理方法是该技术的未来关键发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: μ子谱学虽然常被视为小众技术，但能对从硬凝聚态到生物样本等多种材料提供独特且互补的洞察。尽管以往多在经典框架下解释实验，但μ子本身是量子粒子，研究人员越来越需要在思考μ子谱学实验时考虑其量子本质。

Method: 本文采用展望性论述的方式，系统阐述将μ子谱学置于量子框架（quantum framework）中进行解释的优势与潜力，以此作为该技术未来发展的核心方向。

Result: 论文指出，μ子质量约为质子质量的九分之一（即电子质量的207倍），在物质中是量子态的局域探针（local probe），能够聚焦于材料的体性质（bulk properties）。

Conclusion: 作者认为，采用量子处理方法理解μ子谱学是该技术未来发展的关键方向，具有重要的研究潜力。

Abstract: Although considered a niche technique, muon spectroscopy provides a unique and complementary insight into a range of different materials from hard condensed matter to biological samples and everything in between. In matter, the muon has a mass of $\frac{1}{9}~m_p$ or $207~m_e$, and is a local probe of quantum states that can provide a focus on the bulk properties of materials. While often interpreted in a classical framework, the muon is itself a quantum particle and it is increasingly common for researchers to take account of this when thinking about muon spectroscopy experiments. In this perspective, we focus on the power of using this quantum treatment of muon spectroscopy, which is a key future direction for the technique.

</details>

### [76] [Search for a resonance in events with four top quarks decaying into two leptons and jets in proton-proton collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10148)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文呈现了一项在含有四个顶夸克（top quark）的事件中搜寻重共振态（heavy resonance）的研究，该研究针对仅与顶夸克相互作用的新型矢量（vector）、标量（scalar）或赝标量（pseudoscalar）玻色子模型进行解释，分析了CMS探测器在13和13.6 TeV质心能量下采集的质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度分别为138和35 fb⁻¹），未观察到显著超出，并在95%置信水平（confidence level）下对共振态产生截面设置了上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测仅与顶夸克耦合的新型玻色子共振态，以寻找标准模型（standard model）之外的新物理信号，这是首次在双轻子通道中对这些模型进行专门搜寻。

Method: 选用含有双轻子（电子或缪子）和喷注（jet）的事件样本；利用可变半径喷注聚类算法（variable-radius jet clustering algorithm）从成对喷注中重构重共振态质量；采用机器学习（machine learning）方法识别喷注是否源自全强子衰变的顶夸克；从数据中估计由误判喷注（misidentified jets）构成的主要背景，并作为双喷注不变质量（dijet invariant mass）的函数进行处理。

Result: 数据中未观察到显著超出；在95%置信水平下，对质量介于500 GeV至4 TeV、相对宽度（relative width）为4%至50%范围内的共振态产生截面设定了上限。在矢量中介（vector-mediator）基准模型中，相对宽度为50%的共振态被排除至质量高达850 GeV，预期排除极限为1000 GeV。

Conclusion: 本研究未发现与仅与顶夸克相互作用的新型玻色子相关联的重共振态信号，并首次在双轻子通道中针对这些模型设定了排除上限，为相关新物理模型的参数空间提供了实验约束。

Abstract: A search for a heavy resonance in events containing four top quarks is presented and interpreted in models featuring a new vector, scalar, or pseudoscalar boson that interacts exclusively with top quarks. Proton-proton collision data collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC at centre-of-mass energies of 13 and 13.6 TeV are analysed, corresponding to integrated luminosities of 138 and 35 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. Events with two leptons (electrons or muons) and jets are selected. The mass of the heavy resonance is reconstructed from a pair of jets that are formed using a variable-radius jet clustering algorithm. A machine-learning approach is used to identify whether these jets originate from top quarks that decay fully hadronically. The dominant background from misidentified jets is estimated from data as a function of the dijet invariant mass. No significant excess is observed in data. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the resonance production cross section for resonance masses between 500 GeV and 4 TeV, and relative widths ranging from 4 to 50%. In the vector-mediator benchmark, resonances with 50% relative width are excluded up to masses of 850 GeV, with an expected exclusion limit of 1000 GeV. This is the first dedicated search for these models in the two lepton channel.

</details>


### [77] [Design of ALPHA Phase I: A Plasma Haloscope for 10--20 GHz Post-Inflation Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10292)
*ALPHA Collaboration,Xiran Bai,Rustam Balafendiev,Sean E. Barrett,Eunice Beato,Pavel Belov,Charles D. Brown,Eduardo A. Castro Muñoz,Jan Conrad,Marcel Demarteau,Alex Droster,Joseph Dubois,Jonathan Echevers,Ali Elhadi,Jim Enriquez,Maryam Haytham Esmat,Andrea Gallo Rosso,Eleanor Graham,Chloe Greenstein,Jon E. Gudmundsson,Karsten M. Heeger,Ishaan Iyer,Heather Jackson,Junu Jeong,Michael J. Jewell,Tyler Johnson,Shriram Jois,Gagandeep Kaur,Claire Laffan,S. K Lamoreaux,K. W. Lehnert,Samantha M. Lewis,Jacob Lindahl,Reina H. Maruyama,Philip Mauskopf,Andrew M. Meyer,Alexander J. Millar,Dylan R. Miller,Hiranya V. Peiris,Jianyang Qi,Elizabeth P. Ruddy,Sharada Sahoo,Denis Sakhno,Michael Sekatchev,Max Silva-Feaver,Shenyang Shi,Aarav M. Sindhwad,Gaganpreet Singh,Sukhman Singh,Danielle H. Speller,Dajie Sun,Noshin Tabassum,Karl van Bibber,Yongqi Wang,Frank Wilczek,Mackenzie Wooten,Sabrina Zacarias*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ALPHA实验第一阶段的详细设计与灵敏度预估。ALPHA是一个位于耶鲁大学Wright实验室的国际合作项目，利用等离子体haloscope（plasma haloscope）技术搜索质量范围在10 GHz至20 GHz（约40 μeV至80 μeV）的QCD暗物质轴子（axion）。实验采用线阵等离子体谐振器，将物理尺寸与谐振频率解耦，突破了传统微波腔体的限制，可实现接近KSVZ耦合强度的宽带灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子是一种动机良好的假想粒子，能同时解决强CP问题和暗物质之谜，而近期暴胀后宇宙学模拟倾向于支持质量高于40 μeV的轴子。传统微波腔探测方案在该质量区间面临灵敏度不足和尺寸受限的挑战，因此需要等离子体haloscope这类新方法以实现理论上偏好的灵敏度。

Method: ALPHA实验采用线阵列等离子体谐振器（wire-array plasma resonator）作为探测核心，通过将物理尺寸与谐振频率解耦来规避传统微波腔体在几何尺寸上的限制。实验搜索频率范围为10 GHz至20 GHz，对应约40 μeV至80 μeV的轴子质量区间。

Result: 本文给出了ALPHA实验第一阶段的详细设计与灵敏度预估结果，表明该设计可在目标质量范围内实现接近KSVZ耦合强度的宽带（broadband）灵敏度探测能力。

Conclusion: ALPHA第一阶段实验通过等离子体haloscope技术方案，有望在40 μeV至80 μeV质量区间对QCD暗物质轴子进行有效搜索，为宇宙学模拟所偏好的高质量轴子区间提供了实验验证途径。

Abstract: The axion is a well-motivated hypothetical particle capable of resolving both the strong CP problem and the dark matter mystery, with recent post-inflationary cosmological simulations favoring masses above 40 μeV. Plasma haloscopes serve as a promising experimental approach to reach theoretically preferred sensitivities in this mass range. ALPHA, hosted at Yale Wright Laboratory, is an international collaboration developing plasma haloscopes to search for QCD dark matter axions. In this letter we present the detailed design and sensitivity projection for the first phase of the ALPHA experiment, which will search the mass range from 10 GHz to 20 GHz (~40 μeV to 80 μeV). This search will make use of wire-array plasma resonators to decouple the physical size from the resonant frequency, a limitation typically faced by traditional microwave cavities, allowing broadband sensitivity approaching KSVZ coupling strengths.

</details>

Zhukov,J. Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb探测器在2016–2018年采集的5.4 fb⁻¹数据，对赝矢量介子$D_{s1}(2460)^+$和$D_{s1}(2536)^+$衰变至三体$D_s^+π^+π^-$末态的过程进行了研究，首次测量了$D_{s1}(2536)^+→D_s^+π^+π^-$的分支比，并通过对两种衰变道的联合振幅分析（amplitude analysis），发现二者的Dalitz图分布显著不同，暗示两态内部结构存在差异，并观察到$D_s^+π^±$道存在位于DK阈值以下的奇异（exotic）贡献证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝矢量粲奇介子（pseudovector charmed-strange mesons）$D_{s1}(2460)^+$和$D_{s1}(2536)^+$的内部结构是理解轻夸克QCD动力学的重要课题，此前对二者三体衰变道的系统比较研究尚不充分，且$D_{s1}(2536)^+→D_s^+π^+π^-$分支比从未被测量过。

Method: 研究基于LHCb探测器在2016–2018年间采集、对应积分亮度为5.4 fb⁻¹的数据样本，该样本通过b强子衰变至$D_{s1}^+$态并伴随缓子（muon）的事件选择获得。采用$D_{s1}(2536)^+→D^+K^+π^-$衰变作为参考道来测量$D_{s1}(2536)^+→D_s^+π^+π^-$的分支比，并对$D_{s1}(2460)^+$和$D_{s1}(2536)^+→D_s^+π^+π^-$衰变进行了联合振幅分析。

Result: 首次测量了$D_{s1}(2536)^+→D_s^+π^+π^-$的分支比；Dalitz图分析显示两个态的分布显著不同，表明其内部结构存在差异；在$D_s^+π^±$通道中发现极低于DK阈值以下的奇异贡献证据；同时对两个态的质量进行了测量，并为$D_{s1}(2460)^+$的宽度设定了上限。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，$D_{s1}(2460)^+$与$D_{s1}(2536)^+$虽然同为赝矢量粲奇介子，但其衰变动力学和内部结构存在显著差异，且$D_s^+π^±$道中的奇异贡献提示可能存在超出常规夸克模型描述的物理现象，为理解相关强子结构提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: Decays of the pseudovector $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ and $D_{s1}(2536)^+$ mesons to the three-body $D_{s}^+π^+π^-$ final state are studied. The data sample is based on decays of beauty hadrons into $D_{s1}^+$ states accompanied by a muon from the $b$-hadron decay chain collected by the LHCb detector during 2016--2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 5.4 fb${}^{-1}$. The \mbox{$D_{s1}(2536)^+\to D_s^+π^+π^-$} branching fraction is measured for the first time, with the $D_{s1}(2536)^+\to D^+K^+π^-$ decay used as a reference. A simultaneous amplitude analysis of the $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ and $D_{s1}(2536)^+\to D_s^+π^+π^-$ decays is performed. The Dalitz-plot distributions of the two decays are found to be significantly different, suggesting differences in the internal structure of the two states, with evidence of exotic contributions to the $D_{s}^+π^{\pm}$ channel with the pole below the $DK$ threshold. Measurements of the masses of the $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ and $D_{s1}(2536)^+$ states are performed, and an upper limit on the $D_{s1}(2460)^+$ width is set.

</details>


### [79] [Particle tracking at high luminosities using a novel reconstruction approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10900)
*B. K. Sirasva,S. Dugad,Y. Kumar,P. Suryadevra,M. Yadav*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对大型强子对撞机（LHC）高亮度时代的CMS Phase2径迹探测器，提出了一种基于机器学习（machine learning）架构的新型径迹重建算法，用于识别与每条带电粒子径迹关联的击中点并准确确定其运动学参数。该算法在Pythia8和Geant4模拟的高亮度强相互作用样本上进行了验证，与传统方法相比展现了优异的重建效率、高分辨率以及极低的假径迹率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子的高精度径迹追踪对CMS实验等对撞机实验至关重要，而在高亮度LHC（High Luminosity LHC）时代，探测器面临更高的堆积和更复杂的径迹环境，传统径迹重建算法可能难以满足精度和效率要求，因此需要开发新型算法。

Method: 提出了一种面向CMS Phase2外径迹探测器（outer tracker）的新型径迹重建算法，该算法首先识别与每条径迹相关联的击中点（hits），随后利用机器学习（machine learning）架构精确确定每条径迹的运动学参数（$p_t$、$\eta_0$、$\phi_0$）。算法在基于Pythia8和Geant4框架模拟的高亮度LHC硬相互作用大样本上进行了测试，采用与CMS实验外径迹探测器等效的几何结构。

Result: 算法性能通过重建效率（reconstruction efficiency）、假径迹率（fake rate）和分辨率（resolution）等关键指标进行评估，与传统方法对比显示该算法具有优异的效率和分辨率，同时保持极低的假径迹率。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于机器学习的径迹重建算法在高亮度LHC环境下表现出稳健（robust）的性能，相比传统方法具有显著优势，适用于CMS Phase2径迹探测器设计。

Abstract: Tracking charged particles with high precision is of vital importance for collider experiment like those operating at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), CERN. The tracking detector in the CMS experiment is composed of multi-layer silicon based tracker with 3-dimensional position sensitivity. High precision position data from tracker operated in high magnetic field, is used to reconstruct the trajectories of charged particles and obtain their kinematic parameters ($p_t$,$η_0$, $φ_0$) with high accuracy. In this paper, for Phase2 CMS tracker design, we present a novel track reconstruction algorithm for high luminosity (HL) era of the LHC. The algorithm identifies hits associated with each track and utilizes them to accurately determine the kinematic parameters of each track using machine learning (ML) architecture. The proposed algorithm has been applied on a large sample of hard interactions simulated at high luminosity (HL) era of the LHC using Pythia8 and Geant4 framework for equivalent geometry of the outer tracker of the CMS experiment. Performance of the proposed algorithm has been studied using the key indicators such as reconstruction efficiency, fake rate and resolution. Comparison with traditional methods demonstrates robust performance with excellent efficiency and resolution with minimal fake rate.

</details>


### [80] [Particle Identification at Future Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11028)
*Roberto Preghenella*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文回顾了未来对撞机实验（如FCC-ee和电子-离子对撞机）中粒子鉴别（particle identification, PID）技术的最新研究进展，涵盖紧凑型环形成像切伦科夫探测器、簇计数漂移室、定时增强切伦科夫探测器以及基于先进硅传感器的精密定时系统等多种新概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子鉴别是未来对撞机实验物理项目中的关键组成部分，传统探测器的带电强子鉴别面临挑战。FCC-ee和电子-离子对撞机对动量覆盖范围、探测器集成和材料预算提出了严格要求，亟需开发新型PID概念以满足需求。

Method: 本文综述了多种正在研究中的PID方法，包括紧凑型环形成像切伦科夫探测器（Ring-Imaging Cherenkov detectors）、簇计数漂移室（cluster-counting drift chambers）、定时增强切伦科夫探测器（timing-enhanced Cherenkov detectors）以及基于先进硅传感器的精密定时系统，并讨论了新兴光电探测器技术和耐辐射性（radiation tolerance）考虑。

Result: 本文总结了LHCP 2026特邀报告的核心内容，聚焦于若干具有代表性的技术方案，而非对该领域进行全面的综述。文章强调了新型光电探测器技术在未来对撞机环境中的作用以及辐射耐受性问题的重要性。

Conclusion: 面向未来对撞机设施，多种创新PID方案正在并行研究和验证，覆盖不同物理需求与技术路径，为下一代实验的探测器设计提供了多样化的潜在解决方案。

Abstract: Particle identification (PID) remains a key ingredient of the physics programmes of future collider experiments. While traditional
  collider detectors rely on calorimetry, tracking and muon systems for particle classification, the identification of charged hadrons
  requires dedicated measurements of particle velocity through Cherenkov radiation, ionisation or time-of-flight techniques. Future
  facilities such as FCC-ee and the Electron-Ion Collider place demanding requirements on momentum coverage, detector integration and
  material budget, motivating the development of novel PID concepts. This contribution reviews several approaches currently under
  investigation, including compact Ring-Imaging Cherenkov detectors, cluster-counting drift chambers, timing-enhanced Cherenkov
  detectors and precision timing systems based on advanced silicon sensors. The role of emerging photodetector technologies and
  radiation-tolerance considerations for future collider environments is also discussed. This contribution summarises the main topics
  presented in the invited LHCP 2026 talk and focuses on selected representative examples rather than a comprehensive review of the
  field.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永久电偶极矩是超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）新物理的重要探针，对缪子EDM进行直接测量可为新物理提供约束。该研究的动机在于利用缪子g-2实验的追踪探测器数据，寻找缪子EDM与实验室磁场之间相互作用的证据。

Method: 利用追踪探测器测量缪子衰变产生正电子（positron）的平均垂直衰变角（average vertical decay angle），从而探测可能的缪子EDM d_μ 与实验室参考系中磁场的相互作用。

Result: 测得的缪子电偶极矩为 d_μ = (-0.35 ± 0.19_stat ± 0.34_sys) × 10^-19 e·cm，与零一致；在95%置信水平下给出的新直接上限为 |d_μ| < 1.10 × 10^-19 e·cm。

Conclusion: 该结果与零无显著偏离，但为缪子EDM设定了新的直接测量上限，对限制相关新物理模型具有重要意义。

Abstract: A limit on the permanent electric dipole moment (EDM) of the positive muon is presented based on data from the Fermilab Muon g-2 Experiment taken between 2019 and 2020. The tracking detectors measure the average vertical decay angle of positrons from muon decays, enabling a search for an interaction between a possible muon EDM $d_μ$ and the lab-frame magnetic field. The result, $d_μ= (-0.35 \pm 0.19_{\mathrm{stat}} \pm 0.34_{\mathrm{sys}}) \times10^{-19}~e\cdot$cm, is consistent with zero and sets a new direct limit on the muon EDM of $|d_μ|<1.10\times10^{-19}~e\cdot$cm at the 95 percent confidence level.

</details>

### [82] [Orbital-selective oxygen holes in cuprate ladders beyond the Zhang-Rice paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10228)
*Chengyun Hua,Tianran Chen,Isaac C. Ownby,Colin L. Sarkis,Garrett Granoth,Masaaki Matsuda,Jiaqiang Yan,Ho Nyung Lee,Jeongkeun Song,Yuya Shinohara,Masatomo Uehara,Jun Akimitsu,Oleksandr Prokhnenko,Eugen Weschke,Takeshi Egami,D. Alan Tennant*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过偏振依赖的共振软X射线散射（resonant soft X-ray scattering, RSXS）和中子非弹性散射（inelastic neutron scattering）的组合实验，揭示了自旋梯铜氧化物Sr14Cu24O41中掺杂空穴主要局域在梯子氧格点上的平面非成键O 2pz (pπ) 轨道，而非传统认为的张-赖斯单态（Zhang-Rice singlet, ZRS）构型，从而排除了以Cu-O杂化轨道为基础的标准图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋梯铜氧化物Sr14Cu24O41的电子结构挑战了张-赖斯单态框架及仅基于Cu-O杂化轨道的模型的普适性，因此需要对传统铜基超导理论中孔洞配对和轨道分配的假设进行重新审视。

Method: 本研究结合了O K边偏振依赖的共振软X射线散射与中子非弹性散射两种实验技术，分别用于分辨氧亚晶格中孔洞的轨道归属，以及表征耦合的晶格和磁激发行为。

Result: 偏振分辨的RSXS实验确凿识别出掺杂空穴主要分布于梯子氧格位（rung oxygen）的平面非成键O 2pz (pπ) 轨道；同时，晶格和磁激发测量揭示该氧亚晶格电荷序导致了反常的对角拉伸声子、缺失的非公度磁涨落（incommensurate magnetic fluctuations）以及反常的自旋波劈裂（magnon splitting），这些结果共同排除了常规σ键合单态的图景。

Conclusion: 这些发现促使学界重新评估梯子铜氧化物中的孔穴配对机制，并质疑以铜为中心的模型对于铜基超导体（cuprate superconductors）解释是否足够全面。

Abstract: The electronic structure of the spin-ladder cuprate Sr14Cu24O41 challenges the presumed universality of the Zhang-Rice singlet (ZRS) framework and models based exclusively on Cu-O hybridized orbitals. Combining polarization-dependent resonant soft X-ray scattering at the O K-edge with inelastic neutron scattering, we show that doped holes in the Cu2O3 ladders localize predominantly in planar non-bonding O 2pz (pπ) orbitals of rung oxygen sites rather than forming conventional ZRS states. Polarization-resolved RSXS uniquely identifies this orbital assignment, while lattice and magnetic excitations reveal its coupled consequences, establishing a unified microscopic picture that excludes the conventional σ-bonded singlet. This oxygen-sublattice charge order produces an anomalous diagonal stretching phonon and explains the absence of incommensurate magnetic fluctuations and anomalous magnon splitting. These findings motivate a reassessment of hole pairing in ladder cuprates and the sufficiency of copper-centric models for cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [83] [Some features of high-temperature superconductivity on flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10231)
*V. R. Shaginyan,A. Z. Msezane,S. A. Artamonov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究平带（flat band）与非吸引性配对相互作用下高温超导体的形成机制，发现平带在超导态下会发生形变（倾斜）并获得有限有效质量，从而保证超流权重（superfluid weight）和超电流（supercurrent）不会消失，结果与实验数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨平带的存在如何即使在排斥性配对相互作用（repulsive pairing interactions）条件下也能导致高温超导体的形成，并解释平带超导态中超流动性的来源。
