The two as-grown phases are distinct in electronic structure, magnetic ground state, and transport. Along this annealing series, the Hall response evolves systematically while the magnetic response changes in a structurally insensitive manner, with ferromagnetic order emerging within the same $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}\mathrm{R}30^\circ$ structural class only above a critical annealing temperature, experimentally disentangling carrier density and moment geometry. The Hall magnitude and sign evolution point to a low-carrier-density system in which $k_{\mathrm{F}}$ is susceptible to modest external tuning. For integer spins, the relevant branch softens and evolves into a Higgs-like amplitude mode on approaching the large-$D$ transition, endowing the excitation with a sizable dipolar component and producing a pronounced enhancement of piezomagnetism. By contrast, in half-integer systems the higher-spin branches are progressively separated from the low-energy dipolar sector as the anisotropy increases, which suppresses their contribution to the response. This integer-half-integer contrast directly links macroscopic piezomagnetism to the low-energy fate of multipolar excitations. Our results establish piezomagnetism as a probe of higher-spin quantum dynamics and identify higher-spin altermagnets as a promising setting for quantum magnetoelastic responses.

Abstract: The altermagnetic spin-splitter effect converts an electric field into a transverse pure spin current, with no net magnetization and no charge-Hall counterpart. In established materials this function is tied to crystal-fixed spin-split bands that lock the polarization axis to the lattice. We show that the noncoplanar counter-spiral ground state of a frustrated honeycomb magnet instead carries the altermagnetic operation through a $\mathbf Q$-locked helicity mirror $g$. The mirror selects the spin-current polarization and forbids the perpendicular one, while an antitranslation $Θ$ forbids even-parity spin splitting. Band splitting and spin-splitter response therefore rest on different symmetry elements. Either element alone enforces the charge-Hall zero---a redundancy absent from other spin--orbit-free noncollinear routes---and a charge Hall appears only when both elements are removed. Hole doping then realizes a \emph{spin splitter without spin-split bands}---the symmetry-allowed odd-parity residual below $2\times10^{-7}$ of the hopping $t$ at the Fermi level---with $σ_H^{(s_y)}=0.082\,e^2/h$ without spin--orbit coupling and with zero charge Hall response. Selecting among the three degenerate $\mathbf{Q}$ orientations rotates the polarization axis in exact $120^\circ$ steps at fixed magnitude and charge-Hall zero; the selection rules persist in a $32$-site cell accessible to programmable photonic and circuit lattices.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用数值量子蒙特卡洛模拟方法，在一维SSH模型中引入两种版本的局域Hubbard型相互作用，系统性地研究轴向电荷密度与电导率之间的比例关系是否保持。模拟参数涵盖了从弱相互作用到足以驱动系统进入Mott绝缘体相的强相互作用区间。

Abstract: We consider the 1D Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model. It was recently shown that, for the noninteracting model in its Dirac semimetal phase, the linear response of the axial charge density to an external electric field is proportional to the electrical conductivity in the presence of finite dissipation, with the proportionality factor determined by the coupling constants. This relation may be viewed as a manifestation of the chiral effect, which is a dimensional reduction of the 3D chiral magnetic effect. In the present work, we investigate the same model in the presence of two versions of local Hubbard-type interactions using numerical Quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We find, within the numerical resolution and for the parameters studied, that, even in the regime where sufficiently strong interactions drive the system into a Mott insulating phase, the proportionality between the induced axial charge density and the electrical conductivity remains unchanged. This result indicates that the chiral effect is not renormalized by local Hubbard interactions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相的保护零模通常在尖锐界面处呈指数局域化，但真实物理系统中界面往往是光滑的，此前的认识可能不适用于光滑界面情形。作者旨在探究光滑界面如何改变这一固有的拓扑图像。

Method: 将精确格点解（exact lattice solution）与非均匀狄拉克描述（inhomogeneous Dirac description）相结合，对一类广泛的非均匀SSH模型进行解析分析，研究任意光滑跳跃势（smooth hopping profiles）下零模的空间行为。

Result: 对于任意光滑跳跃势，零模的格点扩展长度普遍按系统尺寸的平方根标度，与界面微观细节无关。在此介观临界区内，关联函数呈代数衰减，之后才过渡为指数衰减；近界面的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）按该涌现长度的对数标度。

Conclusion: 光滑拓扑界面存在一个普适的临界长度（universal critical length），主导低能物理行为，并定义了连接两个能隙相的介观临界区域，从而推广了传统尖锐界面的拓扑零模图像。

Abstract: Protected zero modes are a hallmark of topological phases of matter and are exponentially localized at sharp interfaces between distinct gapped phases. We investigate how this picture changes for smooth interfaces in a broad class of inhomogeneous Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) models. Combining an exact lattice solution with an inhomogeneous Dirac description, we show that the associated Jackiw-Rebbi zero mode becomes spatially extended. For arbitrary smooth hopping profiles, its lattice extension universally scales as the square root of the system size, independently of the microscopic details of the interface. This emergent length defines a mesoscopic critical region separating two gapped phases, within which correlations decay algebraically before crossing over to exponential decay. In addition, the entanglement entropy scales as the logarithm of the emergent length near the interface, confirming the interpretation of a mesoscopic critical region. Our results establish a universal critical length governing the low-energy physics of smooth topological interfaces.

</details>

*Fellipe B. Carneiro,Zéetény Bacsó,Kevin Allen,Aly H. Abdeldaim,Rebecca Scatena,Jaime M. Moya,Roger D. Johnson,Emilia Morosan,Alessandro Bombardi*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁织构探测假设局部测量结果能代表整个体系的磁性行为，但该假设的适用性尚缺乏直接验证，尤其是对于可能具有空间不均匀性的磁性材料。

Conclusion: 局部与全局探测到的磁织构存在明显差异，表明空间分辨探针对准确描述此类材料磁性行为至关重要，传统将局部测量视为整体代表的假设需要重新审视。

Abstract: Here, we use resonant elastic x-ray scattering (REXS) to investigate the inhomogeneity of the zero-field magnetic spin texture of Eu(Al$_{0.4}$Ga$_{0.6}$)$_4$. By using spatially-resolved REXS, we show that the two magnetic transitions at T$_{N1}$ = 17 K and T$_{N2}$ = 14 K originate from two nearly degenerate, yet distinct, orthogonal pairs of q-vectors. The corresponding phases are segregated spatially, such that one fraction of the sample comprises coexisting orthogonal spin-density-wave domains, while another fraction hosts coexisting orthogonal helical domains. Additionally, the helical state forms inversion domains indicating that the inversion symmetry is not broken prior the magnetic transition. Our results suggest that the magnetic state is single-q and revealed a large variation of spin textures across a 0.8 - 1 mm area of the sample surface. These results demonstrate clear differences between the locally and globally probed magnetic textures, typically assumed to be representative of the system as a whole, highlighting the importance of spatially-resolved probes for accurately describing the magnetic behavior of this class of materials.

</details>


### [92] [Sum rules and density-wave modes in spin-singlet fractional quantum Hall fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11133)
*Ritajit Kundu,Rakesh K. Dora,Dung Xuan Nguyen,Ajit C. Balram*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过将正交关联拉盖尔多项式基（orthogonal associated Laguerre polynomials）应用于两分量自旋单态（spin-singlet）的Halperin和Jain分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）流体的对关联函数及其傅里叶变换——静态结构因子，结合大系统蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）数据进行热力学平面拟合，并施加精确短距离行为和长波行为求和规则约束，从而获得稳定的长波GMP/对称密度波激发隙和反对称密度波模式隙，并计算变分能量、构建双层FQH系统的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态是强相互作用拓扑有序系统的典型例子，需要准确计算其对关联函数和静态结构因子的长波行为，以获取可靠的激发隙和相图信息，从而帮助理解近期双层FQH实验中通过调节层间距和密度不平衡/层极化所绘制的相图。

Method: 论文采用正交关联拉盖尔多项式基对两分量自旋单态Halperin和Jain FQH流体的对关联函数及其傅里叶变换（静态结构因子）进行展开，通过拟合大系统蒙特卡罗数据（基于试验波函数）确定展开系数。拟合过程中同时约束波函数的精确短距离行为，并推导和强制实施静态结构因子长波展开必须遵循的求和规则（sum rules）。此外，该方法被推广到自旋分辨（spin-resolved）密度关联函数，以评估反对称密度波模式的激发隙。

Result: 研究表明，施加短距离行为约束和长波求和规则约束对于获得长波Girvin-MacDonald-Platzman（GMP）/对称密度波激发隙的数值稳定且精确的值至关重要。该方法还被扩展至自旋分辨密度关联函数，从而能够评估自旋单态FQH态的反对称密度波模式激发隙。最后利用密度关联函数计算了态的变分能量，并构建了双层FQH系统的相图。

Conclusion: 所提出的带约束拟合方法能够可靠地提取长波行为相关的物理量，所构建的双层FQH相图可能对理解近期双层FQH实验中通过调节层间距和密度不平衡/层极化绘制的相图具有参考价值。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states are prototypical examples of strongly interacting topologically ordered systems. In this work, we obtain thermodynamic fits on the plane for the pair correlation function, and its Fourier transform, the static structure factor, of two-component spin-singlet Halperin and Jain FQH fluids by expanding them in the recently introduced basis of the orthogonal associated Laguerre polynomials [Fulsebakke et al., SciPost Phys. 14, 149 (2023), https://doi.org/10.21468/SciPostPhys.14.6.149 ] and ascertaining the expansion coefficients by fitting them to large-system Monte Carlo data evaluated using their trial wavefunctions. In this fitting procedure, aside from constraining the exact short-distance behavior of the wavefunction, we also derive and enforce the sum rules that the long-wavelength expansion of the static structure factor must adhere to. We show that incorporating these constraints is crucial for obtaining numerically stable and accurate values of the long-wavelength Girvin-MacDonald-Platzman (GMP)/symmetric density-wave excitation gap. We further extend this approach to spin-resolved density-correlation functions, enabling the evaluation of the gap of the antisymmetric density-wave mode for these spin-singlet FQH states. Finally, we use the density-correlators to compute variational energies of the states and construct phase diagrams for bilayer FQH systems. These could be relevant for understanding recent bilayer FQH experiments that map out the phase diagram by tuning the interlayer separation and density-imbalance/layer-polarization.

</details>


### [93] [Floquet Green's functions for lattice electrons driven by Gaussian quantum light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11189)
*Atsushi Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了非相互作用单带晶格电子在由库稳态单模高斯量子光源驱动下的Floquet格林函数（Floquet Green's function）理论框架。该框架将Peierls耦合（Peierls coupling）非微扰处理，并在经典极限下恢复经典Floquet理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Floquet工程框架主要基于经典光驱动，尚未系统地描述由量子光源驱动的电子系统。作者旨在建立一个理论框架，以处理由外部指定的量子光源（包括压缩真空和压缩相干态）驱动的凝聚态系统。

Method: 通过构造共享历史的四端点核（four-endpoint kernel）与连续库核卷积得到小/大格林函数分量，利用库正则反对易关系给出等时协方差以播种推迟和非推迟单腿传播；Peierls耦合在指定源模型中非微扰处理，经典Floquet理论作为适当极限恢复。

Result: 在一维最小模型上的数值计算表明，相较于经典驱动，有限耦合量子源修正产生了谱重构（spectral reconstruction）和占据重分布（occupancy redistribution）。压缩参数和相位为边带结构与占据权重提供了超出经典振幅调制的额外控制旋钮。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用外部指定的量子光源进行凝聚态系统的量子Floquet工程（quantum Floquet engineering）提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We formulate Floquet Green's functions for noninteracting single-band lattice electrons driven by a reservoir-stabilized single-mode Gaussian quantum light source. The source is prescribed externally and is not updated by the many-electron polarization, while an active electronic probe still conditions the source evolution through the Peierls coupling. The two time arguments of a Green's function share one source history: the lesser and greater components are obtained by convolving a shared-history four-endpoint kernel with the continuous bath kernels before the final source trace, while the bath canonical anticommutation relation yields an equal-time covariance that seeds the retarded and advanced one-leg propagations. The Peierls coupling is treated nonperturbatively within the prescribed-source model, and classical Floquet theory is recovered in the appropriate limit. Numerical calculations on a minimal one-dimensional model show finite-coupling quantum-source corrections beyond a prescribed classical drive, together with spectral reconstruction and occupancy redistribution for squeezed vacuum and squeezed coherent sources. The squeezing parameter and phase provide additional control knobs, beyond classical amplitude modulation, for both sideband structure and occupied weight. This work provides a theoretical framework for quantum Floquet engineering of condensed matter with an externally prescribed quantum light source.

</details>

### [94] [Flat Galactic Rotation Curves Interpreted as Evidence for the Mach's Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10073)
*Andres Escala*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种对盘状星系渐近平坦旋转曲线的马赫式（Machian）解释，作为Λ冷暗物质（CDM）范式和修正牛顿动力学（MOND）之外的替代方案；将MOND加速度标度 $a_0$ 与宇宙学尺度联系起来，重写重子Tully-Fisher关系（BTFR），并用高红移观测数据检验，结果倾向于一个平坦、仅含重子、Λ主导的准德西特宇宙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为盘状星系中渐近平坦旋转曲线这一现象提供马赫原理（Mach’s Principle）框架下的解释，以替代当前的主流范式ΛCDM和MOND。作者质疑MOND加速度标度作为新基本常数的地位，希望从宇宙学尺度出发给出更根本的物理来源。

Method: 作者论证 $a_0 \sim c^2/R_u \sim c^4/GM_u \sim c/t_u$，将MOND标度与可观测宇宙的尺度相联系；随后将重子Tully-Fisher关系改写为宇宙学形式 $\frac{M_{\rm bar}}{M_u} = (V/c)^4$，指出马赫式解释必须满足此类关系；最后将这一框架对BTFR归一化的宇宙学演化预测与红移 $z \sim 2$ 和 $z \sim 5$ 的高红移观测数据进行比较。

Result: 高红移数据倾向于一个平坦、仅含重子、Λ主导的准德西特宇宙（$\Omega_m \approx 0.05$，$\Omega_\Lambda \approx 0.95$），优于标准MOND或ΛCDM参数下的预测；平坦的星系旋转曲线因此可能被视为支持马赫原理（至少其某些版本）的有力证据。

Conclusion: 论文认为平坦的星系旋转曲线可能并非暗物质或修正引力定律的证据，而是马赫原理的体现——当局部加速度足够小以致能感受到全局宇宙边界时，系统进入非牛顿区域。该框架还自然预测了BTFR归一化随可观测宇宙质量的宇宙学演化。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of a Machian interpretation for the phenomena associated with asymptotically flat rotation curves in disk galaxies, providing an alternative to both the Lambda-Cold Dark Matter ($Λ$CDM) paradigm and MOdified Newtonian Dynamics (MOND). We argue that the MOND acceleration scale $a_0$ is likely not a new fundamental constant, but rather a manifestation of the cosmological scales of the observable Universe ($a_0 \sim c^2/R_u \sim c^4/GM_u \sim c/t_u$). We rewrite the Baryonic Tully-Fisher Relation (BTFR) in a cosmological format, $\frac{M_{\rm bar}}{M_{u}} = \left( \frac{V}{c} \right)^4$, suggesting that the transition to the non-Newtonian regime occurs when local accelerations become small enough to feel the global cosmic boundary, and showing that any Machian interpretation must fulfill a relation of this form. Furthermore, this framework naturally predicts a cosmological evolution of the BTFR normalization as the mass in the observable Universe evolves. We test these predictions against recent high-redshift observations ($z \sim 2$ and $z \sim 5$), finding that the data
  favor a flat, baryon-only, $Λ$-dominated quasi-de Sitter universe ($Ω_m \approx 0.05$, $Ω_Λ\approx 0.95$) over standard MOND or $Λ$CDM parameters. These results suggest that flat galactic rotation curves may be interpreted as compelling evidence in favor of Mach's Principle, in at least some of its versions.

</details>

### [95] [Inelastic spreading of viscoelastic drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10851)
*Mete Abbot,Thijs Varkevisser,Manglesh Singh,Antoine Gaillard,Devranjan Samanta,Daniel Bonn*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验解耦粘弹性流体中弹性效应与剪切稀化效应，证明弹性性质（即使在高浓度达1000 ppm时）不改变液滴撞击固体表面时的最大铺展比；通过能量平衡推导无量纲判据Γ，发现所有实验条件下Γ远小于1，表明弹性效应在液滴铺展过程中能量上可忽略，与韦森伯格数（Weissenberg number）和德博拉数（Deborah number）的预测相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴撞击固体表面时，添加剂诱导的弹性效应被已知可抑制回弹和飞溅，但弹性对最大铺展半径的影响仍存在争议；难点在于难以将弹性阻力与粘性剪切稀化引起的增强铺展效应分离，亟需一种能将二者解耦的实验方法。

Method: 采用恒定粘度且具高弹性特征的博格流体（Boger fluid），分别以聚环氧乙烷（PEO）和聚丙烯酰胺（PAAM）为溶质制备液滴进行撞击实验，从而在实验中分离弹性效应与剪切稀化效应；随后建立包含惯性、毛细、粘性和弹性贡献的能量平衡模型，推导出估计弹性效应程度的无量纲判据Γ。

Result: 实验表明弹性性质不改变最大铺展比，即使在溶质浓度高达1000 ppm时亦然；在所有测试撞击条件下均得到Γ远小于1的结果，证明弹性贡献在能量上可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 液滴铺展过程中弹性效应在能量上可忽略，这一结论与仅凭韦森伯格数和德博拉数所做出的预测相反，说明在评估此类流动问题时需以能量平衡分析为准而非无量纲弹性数。

Abstract: When a liquid drop impacts a solid surface, rebound and splashing are known to be suppressed by additives that induce elastic effects; however, the influence of elasticity on the maximum spreading radius remains debated. The difficulty lies in isolating elastic resistance from the enhanced spreading caused by viscous shear thinning. Here, we experimentally decouple these effects by investigating Boger fluid drops (constant-viscosity solutions with high elasticity) of polyethylene oxide (PEO) and polyacrylamide (PAAM). We demonstrate that elastic properties do not alter the maximum spreading ratio, even at high concentrations up to 1000 ppm. We formulate an energy balance that accounts for the inertial, capillary, viscous, and elastic contributions, and yields a dimensionless criterion ($Γ$) that estimates the degree of elastic effects. We show that $Γ\ll 1$ for all impact conditions tested, demonstrating that elastic effects are energetically negligible during droplet spreading, opposite to what Weissenberg and Deborah numbers would predict.

</details>

### [96] [Sharp Tangential NEC Minimization and Israel Surface Layers in Schwarzschild Mass Interpolations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09963)
*Changsun Choi,Ryan E. Grady*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究施瓦西规范（Schwarzschild gauge）下静态球对称度规中质量函数从 M₁ 平滑增长到 M₂ 的构型，证明此类构型必然违反切向零能量条件（tangential NEC），并将该问题转化为定量变分问题：确定最小违反量及近极小化选出的几何。通过精确的 L¹ 松弛与显式求解，得到唯一极小元为归一化箱状廓线，并建立了平方根与线性分离率的定量稳定性以及薄环带极限下的互反宽度发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定质量函数在球壳 [R₁,R₂] 外于施瓦西半径平滑地从 M₁ 增至 M₂ 的静态球对称度规类，一个基本限制表明切向零能量条件的违反在此类构型中不可回避。这使得物理问题转化为定量问题：最小可能的违反是多少，以及近极小化会选择何种几何。

Method: 首先确定由此产生的非局部加权正变分泛函的精确 L¹ 松弛（L¹-relaxation）并显式求解松弛问题。接着通过精确亏损分解（exact deficit decomposition）建立定量稳定性分析，识别变分相变（variational phase transition），并借助分布收敛和 Israel 表面应力张量（Israel surface stress tensor）分析奇异层结构。

Result: 松弛问题的唯一极小元为归一化箱状廓线，给出违反量的下确界；松弛最小值可达而光滑下确界不可达。定量稳定性呈现平方根速率（非退化临界优化子）与线性速率（严格约束边界优化子），两种速率同时控制质量函数与度规系数。薄环带极限下互反宽度发散；所有光滑极小化序列收敛到同一局部 Lipschitz 度规，具有常密度 p=−ρ 体区域与两个类时表面层，其爱因斯坦张量分布收敛，奇异项与 Israel 表面应力张量一致。负切向零能量集中在内层，其积分负压等于精确变分代价。

Conclusion: 切向零能量条件的违反在该构型类中是结构性的且可定量刻画；箱状质量廓线是变分下确界的唯一实现，揭示了近极小化几何具有常密度负压体与表面层的分层结构，并建立了与微分几何和广义相对论表面层理论一致的分析框架。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric metrics in Schwarzschild gauge whose mass function increases smoothly from $M_1$ to $M_2>M_1$ across an annulus $[R_1,R_2]$ outside the larger Schwarzschild radius. An elementary obstruction shows that tangential NEC violation is unavoidable in this class. This reduces the physical question to a quantitative one: what is the least possible violation, and what geometry is selected by near-minimization? We first determine the exact $L^1$-relaxation of the resulting nonlocal weighted positive-variation functional and solve the relaxed problem explicitly. Its unique minimizer is a normalized box profile, which yields the infimum of violations: the relaxed minimum is attained, while the smooth infimum is not. An exact deficit decomposition yields sharp quantitative stability, with a square-root rate at a nondegenerate critical optimizer and a linear rate at a strict constrained boundary optimizer; the same rates control the mass function and metric coefficients. We identify a variational phase transition between these regimes and prove reciprocal-width divergence in the thin-annulus limit. Every smooth minimizing sequence converges to one locally Lipschitz metric with a constant-density $p=-ρ$ bulk and two timelike surface layers, its Einstein tensors converge distributionally, and the singular terms agree with the Israel surface stress tensors. The negative tangential null energy concentrates on the inner layer, whose integrated negative pressure equals the sharp variational cost.

</details>


### [97] [Observational Signatures of Static and Rotating Wormholes Embedded in Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09974)
*Zinnat Hassan,Paras Balani,P. K. Sahoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在两种不同的暗物质环境（尖点型 Navarro-Frenk-White 晕与有核孤立子波暗物质轮廓）中，基于矮星系 NGC 2366 的旋转曲线观测参数，构建静态与缓慢旋转的可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）时空，并系统比较了光子动力学、阴影结构与吸积盘成像等可观测量的差异，发现虫洞光子动力学强烈依赖暗物质轮廓，为探测暗物质微观性质提供了潜在观测途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虫洞研究通常假设真空或简单的周围环境，忽略了真实星系中暗物质晕结构对虫洞光子动力学的影响。本文旨在探究不同暗物质轮廓（尖点型 NFW 与有核孤立子波暗物质）如何塑造虫洞的透镜现象与可观测特征。

Method: 采用 Morris-Thorne 框架求解爱因斯坦场方程获得虫洞的形状函数与红移函数，并通过 Teo 度规（含 Lense–Thirring 框架拖曳项）扩展至缓慢旋转情形；对四种时空和六个喉半径进行零测地线追踪，计算特定强度剖面、极坐标阴影图，并构建含完整相对论多普勒效应的吸积盘图像。

Result: 两种暗物质轮廓在光子动力学上差异显著：NFW 势过浅无法支撑分离的光子球面，临界冲击参数接近喉半径；孤立子核心则能容纳不稳定的真实光子轨道，临界冲击参数约为喉半径的 1.18–1.26 倍。这一差异影响光子环锐度、阴影尺寸、吸积盘外观以及旋转情形下框架拖曳导致的阴影不对称度。

Conclusion: 孤立子阴影尺寸通过 ρc∝m_b^(−2) 追踪底层超轻玻色子质量，在 m_b≈10^(−18) eV 附近出现从完全捕获到主要偏折透镜的转变，该特征在 NFW 情形下不存在，为直接探测暗物质微观物理提供了观测途径。

Abstract: We study static and slowly rotating traversable wormholes embedded in two contrasting dark matter environments, the cuspy Navarro-Frenk-White (NFW) halo and the cored solitonic wave dark matter ($ψ$DM) profile, using observational parameters set by the rotation curve of the dwarf galaxy NGC\,2366. For each profile, we solve the Einstein field equations in the Morris-Thorne framework to obtain the shape and redshift functions, then extend to slow rotation via the Teo metric with a Lense--Thirring frame-dragging term. Across the four resulting spacetimes and six throat radii, we trace null geodesics, compute specific intensity profiles and polar shadow maps, and construct accretion disk images including the full relativistic Doppler effect. We noticed these two profiles differ sharply in photon dynamics. The NFW potential is too shallow to support a detached photon sphere, so its critical impact parameter stays close to the throat radius. The soliton core, by contrast, is focused enough to host a genuine unstable photon orbit that raises the critical impact parameter to about $1.18$--$1.26$ times the throat radius. This difference in photon-sphere structure propagates through nearly every observable, including photon-ring sharpness, shadow size, accretion-disk appearance, and, in the rotating case, the degree of shadow asymmetry from frame dragging. The soliton shadow size also tracks the mass of the underlying ultralight boson through $ρ_c\propto m_b^{-2}$, with a transition from total capture to mainly deflecting lensing near $m_b\sim10^{-18}$\,eV, a feature absent for NFW that offers a direct probe of dark matter microphysics. These results show that wormhole photon dynamics are shaped by the dark matter profile, pointing to a possible observational route to probe the nature of dark matter.

</details>


### [98] [Horizon-Brightened Acceleration Radiation and the Deflection Angle Near a Degenerate Photon Sphere of Schwarzschild-like Quantum-Corrected Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09976)
*Yunqiao Xu,Uktamjon Uktamov,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Chengxun Yuan*

Method: 在量子修正黑洞时空中，利用无奇异性处方（nonsingular prescription）从边缘轨道附近贡献中提取偏转角积分的发散部分；采用Lindblad主方程框架描述辐射场的近场动力学，并建立其热稳态与热力学关系。

Abstract: We investigate horizon-brightened acceleration radiation (HBAR) and a strong-deflection expansion for the deflection angle of light rays scattered in the vicinity of a degenerate photon sphere, within the context of a quantum-corrected black hole spacetime. We characterize the horizon structure and thermodynamics, and we extract the divergent part of the deflection-angle integral from the near-marginal-orbit contribution using a nonsingular prescription at marginality, obtaining a unique leading power-law term. In terms of the closest-approach radius, the strong-deflection leading coefficient factorizes into a universal branch constant and a local factor involving the third derivative of the effective potential at the degenerate photon sphere. On the quantum side, we develop the near-horizon reduction relevant to HBAR, demonstrating that the dominant sector governing the detector response exhibits conformal behavior and yields a thermal excitation spectrum characterized by the horizon temperature. We adopt a Lindblad master-equation framework for the radiation field, establish the existence of a thermal steady state, and obtain an HBAR entropy-energy relation that satisfies a Clausius-type first-law structure. ### [99] [Density-preserving core-Einasto black holes with Event Horizon Telescope bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09977)
*Ali Övgün,Reggie C. Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出牛顿零晕密度（Newtonian halo density）不能唯一确定相对论时空，并系统分析了这种完成的（completion）模糊性对地平线尺度信号（horizon-scale signals）的影响。以基于FIRE-2模拟校准的反馈核心Einasto轮廓（feedback-cored Einasto profile）为例，作者推导了保密度（density-preserving）几何的封闭质量形式、单/三地平线判据与固定晕第一定律；证明当前的EHT阴影偏差数据无法约束光滑的千秒差距核心Einasto晕，环境信号需依赖紧凑内成分或不同相对论源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿晕密度不足以唯一确定一个相对论性时空，导致的完成模糊性可能主导预测的地平线尺度信号，这要求对该依赖关系进行显式刻画。作者希望澄清不同完成惯例（如旋转曲线完成）与保密度完成之间在黑洞阴影等可观测信号上的巨大差异。

Method: 在Schwarzschild规范（gauge）中，作者用爱因斯坦方程构造渐近平坦、保密度的几何，并由有效各向异性流体（effective anisotropic fluid）支撑，不假设无碰撞暗物质的微观描述。他们推导出封闭形式的包围质量、单地平线判据与三地平线相变边界、固定晕第一定律，并开展协变极化薄盘（covariant polarized thin-disk）计算，同时与旋转曲线完成方案进行对比。

Result: 一般情况下，球形黑洞阴影的领先阶分数位移等于真空光子球（photon sphere）内环境质量除以中心黑洞质量。应用于已发表的EHT阴影偏差摘要，得到Sgr A*为4.8×10²³ M⊙ pc⁻³、M87*为4.1×10¹⁷ M⊙ pc⁻³的单侧95%可信上限，远高于现实光滑晕密度。银河系校准预测分数阴影位移为1.7×10⁻²⁵，而旋转曲线完成方案在同样银河输入下给出大19个数量级的位移。

Conclusion: 当前地平线尺度图像不能约束光滑的千秒差距尺度核心Einasto晕。旋转曲线完成方案人为放大了环境信号的强度，而保密度完成方案显示极弱的核心抑制效应；可观测的环境信号需依赖紧凑内成分或物理上不同的相对论源。

Abstract: A Newtonian halo density does not uniquely determine a relativistic spacetime, and the resulting completion ambiguity can dominate predicted horizon-scale signals. We make this dependence explicit for the feedback-cored Einasto profile calibrated on FIRE-2 simulations. In Schwarzschild gauge, the Einstein equations give an asymptotically flat, density-preserving geometry supported by an effective anisotropic fluid; no microscopic description of collisionless dark matter is assumed. By contrast, a commonly used rotation-curve completion replaces the finite seed core by an inverse-square source cusp. For the density-preserving solution we derive the enclosed mass in closed form, obtain a sharp one-horizon criterion and the extremal boundaries of a three-horizon phase, and establish the fixed-halo first law. More generally, the leading fractional shift of a spherical black-hole shadow is the environmental mass enclosed within the vacuum photon sphere divided by the central black-hole mass. Applying this result to published Event Horizon Telescope shadow-deviation summaries gives illustrative one-sided 95\% credible limits of $4.8\times10^{23}\,\Msun\,{\rm pc}^{-3}$ for $\sgr$ and $4.1\times10^{17}\,\Msun\,{\rm pc}^{-3}$ for $\mseven$, far above realistic smooth-halo densities. A Milky-Way calibration predicts a fractional shadow shift of $1.7\times10^{-25}$, whereas the rotation-curve completion gives a shift 19 orders of magnitude larger for the same galactic inputs. A covariant polarized thin-disk calculation shows the same weak-core suppression. Thus current horizon-scale images do not constrain a smooth kiloparsec-scale core-Einasto halo; an observable environmental signal would instead require a compact inner component or a physically different relativistic source.

</details>


### [100] [A fast, differentiable neural-network surrogate for precessing binary black-hole waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09978)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波参数估计需要每次事件数百万次的波形评估（waveform evaluations），这一计算成本严重制约了实时推断和群体研究（population studies）的效率，亟需更快的波形生成方法。

Result: 在6×10⁵个波形上训练后，固定取向匹配度（match）均值0.975（中位数0.988），取向平均匹配度均值0.940（中位数0.975）；应变振幅保持物理性（中位比0.98）。单个波形生成仅需12毫秒，批处理下每秒约3.5×10⁴个波形，并获得了经有限差分验证的全13维Fisher矩阵。

Conclusion: 该替代模型兼具高速与完全可微性，支持内在和外在参数的梯度计算，为实时引力波参数估计和群体研究提供了一种高效可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave parameter estimation requires millions of waveform evaluations per event, a cost that constrains real-time inference and population studies. We present a fast, fully differentiable neural-network surrogate for the precessing numerical-relativity model \NRSur{}, spanning its full intrinsic parameter space $λ=(q,\,\boldsymbolχ_1,\,\boldsymbolχ_2)$ together with the reference orbital frequency $ω_0$. Rather than a single polarization at a fixed orientation, the surrogate predicts the \emph{inertial-frame spherical-harmonic modes} $h_{\ell m}$ ($\ell\leq 4$), so that both polarizations $h_+,h_\times$ at an arbitrary orientation $(ι,\varphi,ψ)$ are reconstructed from one network evaluation through an analytic, differentiable mode-to-strain projection. Trained on $6\times10^5$ waveforms, it attains a fixed-orientation match of mean $0.975$ (median $0.988$) and an orientation-averaged match of mean $0.940$ (median $0.975$) for $q\in[1,4]$, $|\boldsymbolχ_{1,2}|\leq 0.8$, while keeping the overall strain amplitude physical (median ratio $0.98$). It generates a waveform in $\SI{12}{ms}$ (single) and ${\sim}3.5\times10^4$ per second in batches on a single GPU. Because the mode-to-strain projection is analytic, the surrogate is differentiable in both intrinsic and extrinsic parameters, yielding a full 13-dimensional Fisher matrix (validated against finite differences) and gradient-based (HMC/NUTS) parameter estimation.

</details>


### [101] [Thermodynamic Supercriticality and Complex Phase Diagram for Charged AdS Black Holes in Trace Anomaly Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09980)
*Minyan Ou,Xiangdong Zhang,Zhang-Yu Nie*

TL;DR: 本文将Lee-Yang相变框架扩展至四维迹异常引力（trace anomaly gravity）中的带荷反德西特（AdS）黑洞，通过将视界半径视为复变量推导出完全解析的复相图，发现了修正引力背景下的新型超临界现象。迹异常使临界压力和临界温度均被压低；Widom线被严格识别为复Lee-Yang零点在实物理相平面上的投影，并与热力学响应函数方法的定义在近临界区高度一致。跨越Widom线呈现光滑连续的交叉行为，区别于临界点以下的非连续一级相变。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将黑洞视界半径（horizon radius）推广为复变量，在复平面上求解Lee-Yang零点，从而构造完整的复相图；同时采用热力学响应函数方法独立求解Widom线，并对两种定义进行定量与标度性比较。

Result: 发现迹异常压低临界压力和临界温度；在近临界区域，Widom线的Lee-Yang投影定义与响应函数定义具有极佳定量一致性和相同的普适标度行为（universal scaling behavior）；跨越Widom线时热力学响应函数表现为光滑连续的交叉（crossover），而非一级相变。

Conclusion: 迹异常引力中的带荷AdS黑洞在超临界区域展现出不同于标准Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞的新物理，为量子修正黑洞的热力学提供了具体而可验证的图像。

Abstract: We extend the Lee-Yang phase transition framework to charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes in four-dimensional trace anomaly gravity. By treating the horizon radius as a complex variable, we derive a fully resolved complex phase diagram that uncovers novel supercritical phenomena within this modified gravity setting. Relative to the standard Reissner-Nordström-AdS black hole, the trace anomaly shifts the location of the critical point: for a representative set of anomaly parameters, both the critical pressure and critical temperature are suppressed. The Widom line is rigorously identified as the projection of the complex Lee-Yang zeros onto the real physical phase plane, a trajectory that demarcates the small-black-hole-like and large-black-hole-like phases throughout the supercritical regime. We also independently recover this same Widom line via the thermodynamic response function method, demonstrating that the two definitions are in excellent quantitative agreement in the near-critical region and share identical universal scaling behavior. Furthermore, our analysis reveals a smooth, continuous crossover across the Widom line as probed by thermodynamic response functions--a behavior fundamentally distinct from the discontinuous first-order phase transitions that occur below the critical point. These results offer new, physically concrete insights into the thermodynamics of quantum-corrected black holes.

</details>


### [102] [Bianchi-Type Black Holes in Higher-Dimensional $R^2$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09982)
*Seçil Şentorun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究在 R² 修正引力理论中，利用外微分形式（exterior differential forms）方法构造了高维 Bianchi 型静态与稳态精确解，并分析其热力学性质，发现临界点处存在有限霍金温度（Hawking temperature）但 Wald 熵（Wald entropy）恒为零的非常规热力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维时空中的精确解是理解修正引力理论（modified gravity theory）深层结构的重要途径。研究旨在考察 R² 引力理论在高维情形下是否允许具均匀但各向异性（anisotropic）视界几何的解决方案，并揭示高阶曲率修正对热力学性质的影响。

Method: 采用外微分形式的形式主义构造 R² 修正引力理论中的高维 Bianchi 型精确解；通过求解场方程获得静态与稳态两类构型，并在临界点处分析有效引力耦合（effective gravitational coupling）消失时允许的常曲率几何空间扩展，进而计算其热力学量。

Result: 理论允许具有超尺度违反（hyperscaling violating）特征的黑洞解，其视界几何为均匀但各向异性的，涵盖静态与稳态构型。在临界扇区中，这些解具有有限的霍金温度而 Wald 熵恒为零，构成一种不寻常的热力学态。

Conclusion: 高阶曲率修正（higher-curvature corrections）在临界点处引发非传统的热力学特征，表明该理论中存在一个有效引力耦合消失、常曲率几何空间被放大的特殊参数区域，该区域呈现出独特的热力学行为。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate higher-dimensional static and stationary Bianchi-type solutions in an $R^2$ gravity theory using the formalism of exterior differential forms. We construct a broad class of exact higher-dimensional Bianchi-type solutions in an $R^{2}$-corrected gravity theory. It is shown that the theory admits hyperscaling violating black hole solutions associated with homogeneous but anisotropic horizon geometries, encompassing both static and stationary configurations. We further analyze some thermodynamic properties of these backgrounds and demonstrate that higher-curvature corrections give rise to unconventional features. In particular, we show that within the critical sector, the solutions possess a finite Hawking temperature despite an identically vanishing Wald entropy, signaling an unusual thermodynamic regime. These configurations arise at the critical point of the theory, where the effective gravitational coupling vanishes and the space of admissible constant-curvature geometries is enlarged.

</details>


### [103] [Probing nonlinear electrodynamics-sourced black holes via light and orbital mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10071)
*Marco A. A. de Paula,Ednaldo L. B. Junior*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了ModMax黑洞（ModMax black hole）的几何与轨道力学性质，通过多个可观测效应（夏皮罗时间、萨格纳克效应、引力与运动学位移、轨道进动）揭示了非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics, NED）修正的显著影响，并利用S2恒星轨道进动约束了NED参数与电荷质量比的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性电动力学（NED）理论中的双参数电磁拉格朗日密度可导致真空双折射（vacuum birefringence）现象，使光子遵循两种不同的有效几何。近几十年来，理解NED理论中有效光锥的作用并约束理论中的额外自由参数是该领域的重要目标。

Method: 以基于双参数拉格朗日密度的ModMax黑洞几何为研究对象，从光力学与轨道力学两个角度开展分析，具体考察夏皮罗时间（Shapiro time）、萨格纳克效应（Sagnac effect）、引力与运动学位移（gravitational and kinematic shifts）以及轨道进动（orbital precession）等可观测效应。

Result: 发现非线性电磁场引起的修正可能非常显著。夏皮罗时间延迟与引力红移在两个有效度规下完全相同，因此对ModMax电动力学的双折射结构不敏感；而萨格纳克效应与运动学位移仍能区分两种有效几何。基于S2恒星绕人马座A*（Sagittarius A*）的近星点进动分析，给出了NED参数与ModMax黑洞电荷质量比之间耦合的观测约束。

Conclusion: 文章提供了一套逐步的程序方法，用于从理论上探索NED场的特征并约束NED源黑洞模型的自由参数。

Abstract: Well-founded models of nonlinear electrodynamics (NED) are typically characterized by an additional parameter associated with their deviation from linear electrodynamics. In addition to that, from a phenomenological point of view, one of the most intriguing results of NED theory is that photons follow null geodesics according to an effective geometry. In particular, for a two-parameter electromagnetic Lagrangian density, we might observe the vacuum birefringence phenomenon, i.e., photons can follow null geodesics according to two effective geometries. Over the past few decades, efforts have been made to better understand the role of effective light cones in NED theory, as well as to constrain the additional free parameters provided by the theory. We investigate the ModMax black hole (BH) geometry, which is a well-motivated NED-sourced BH based on a two-parameter Lagrangian density, via light and orbital mechanics, considering the Shapiro time, the Sagnac effect, the gravitational and kinematic shifts, and the orbital precession. We find that the corrections introduced by nonlinear electromagnetic fields can be significant. In particular, we show that the Shapiro time delay and the gravitational redshift are identical for both effective metrics, making these observables insensitive to the birefringent structure of ModMax electrodynamics. By contrast, the Sagnac effect and the kinematic shifts remain capable of distinguishing the two effective geometries. Moreover, the orbital mechanics investigation, considering the orbital periapsis precession of the S2 star around Sagittarius A$^{\star}$, provides an observational constraint on the coupling between the NED parameter and the charge-to-mass ratio of the ModMax BH. Generally speaking, we provide a step-by-step procedure for theoretically exploring signatures of NED fields and constraining the free parameters of NED-sourced BH models.

</details>


### [104] [Horizon flux-balance laws in the multiscale perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10093)
*Ali Seraj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Kerr背景下对黑洞事件视界的演化方程进行双时间尺度（two-timescale）微扰展开，推导出粗粒化视界动力学的强约束，包括领先阶绝热刚性（adiabatic rigidity）等性质，并提供了将体几何微扰解变换到视界适配坐标系统的系统方法，最终建立了与视界对称性相关的守恒律（包括能量、动力学熵和角动量）及其到二阶电荷和三阶通量的展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞事件视界的演化由一组控制其内禀和外禀几何的方程支配，但目前对这一动力学在微扰意义下的完整理解和在物理应用中的系统工具仍然不足。作者旨在通过双时间尺度微扰框架刻画粗粒化视界动力学并建立与视界对称性相关的守恒律，为极端质量比旋进（extreme mass-ratio inspiral）系统中的视界吸收与反作用（backreaction）研究提供理论框架。

Method: 本文采用双时间尺度微扰展开方法研究黑洞视界演化方程，以Kerr背景为基准。在领先阶推导粗粒化线性剪切消失及绝热刚性条件；随后给出将微扰体几何解（如Lorenz规范下给出）变换到内行Newman-Unti规范视界适配坐标系统的系统程序，从而直接从体度规提取微扰视界几何；最后利用视界对称性构造守恒律并展开电荷至二阶、通量至三阶微扰理论。

Result: 在领先阶，粗粒化线性剪切为零，视界表现出绝热刚性：角速度和非仿射参数（inaffinity）的领先修正在每个视界截面上保持均匀，且在慢时间尺度上演化。同时建立了视界规范化变换程序及包含能量、动力学熵和角动量在内的守恒律，并给出了相应的微扰展开阶数。

Conclusion: 该框架为极端质量比旋进系统中视界吸收与反作用的未来应用提供了理论基础，其导出的守恒律和视界几何提取方法有望用于量化微扰黑洞视界的能量和角动量转移等物理过程。

Abstract: Black-hole event horizons obey a set of evolution equations governing their intrinsic and extrinsic geometry. We study these equations in a \textit{two-timescale} perturbative expansion about a Kerr background and derive strong constraints on the coarse-grained horizon dynamics. At leading order, the coarse-grained linear shear vanishes, while the horizon exhibits an \textit{adiabatic rigidity}: the leading corrections to its angular velocity and inaffinity remain uniform on each horizon cut, while evolving on the slow timescale. We then provide a systematic procedure for transforming a perturbative bulk solution, given for example in Lorenz gauge, to an ingoing Newman--Unti gauge adapted to the horizon, allowing the perturbed horizon geometry to be extracted directly from the bulk metric. Finally, we formulate black-hole conservation laws associated with horizon symmetries, including energy, dynamical entropy, and angular momentum. We expand the charges through second order and their fluxes through third order in perturbation theory, providing a framework for future applications to horizon absorption and backreaction in extreme mass-ratio inspirals.

</details>


### [105] [Static anisotropic stars in Lovelock gravity: Universal closed-form equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10129)
*Sudan Hansraj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Lovelock引力中，将已知的静态完美流体特征多项式方法推广至完整的各向异性物质扇区，导出了闭式、无求和的密度、径向/切向压强及各向异性表达式，并证明内部Schwarzschild几何是任意Lovelock耦合下唯一的各向同性内部解，最后在d=10和d=11维度下积分了任意阶恒星方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态完美流体在Lovelock引力中的特征多项式表述已有先例（Phys. Rev. D 113, 084008），但该方法尚未推广到完整的各向异性物质扇区，后者对于研究更一般的恒星结构与宇宙物理场景至关重要。

Method: 在任意维度和独立耦合的曲率阶数下，基于单一多项式W及其导数、两个派生多项式V和U，推导出密度、径向压强、切向压强和各向异性的闭式无求和表达式；维度相关组合系数通过广义delta反对称化（generalized-delta antisymmetrisation）获得，并以归纳论证独立确认。通过施加压强各向同性恢复已知全导数方程，并将其解析为关于Lovelock阶仿射、维度线性的显式算子。

Result: 证明了除零角向截面曲率分支外，内部Schwarzschild几何是任意Lovelock耦合共有的唯一各向同性内部解；识别了在d=2N+1中纯Lovelock球体有界性的固定符号障碍等价于多项式恒等式V≡0，且低阶项或宇宙学项可消除此障碍；在d=10和d=11中积分所有允许阶数激活的恒星方程，表明广义相对论序列拓扑保持，而高阶项累积性地增大了最大质量。

Conclusion: 特征多项式方法成功推广至各向异性物质扇区，揭示了Lovelock引力中各向同性内部解的普适唯一性，并为任意阶恒星方程提供了解析整合途径；高阶Lovelock项在保持广义相对论定性结构的同时系统性提升最大质量。

Abstract: A characteristic-polynomial formulation is already known for static perfect fluids in Lovelock gravity [Phys. Rev. D 113, 084008 (2026)]. We extend it to the complete anisotropic matter sector. For arbitrary dimension and independently coupled curvature orders, we derive closed, summation-free expressions for the density, radial pressure, tangential pressure and anisotropy in terms of a single polynomial \(W\), its derivatives and two derived polynomials \(V\) and \(U\). The dimension-dependent combinatorial coefficients are obtained directly from the generalized-delta antisymmetrisation and independently confirmed through an inductive argument. Imposing pressure isotropy recovers the known total-derivative equation, which we resolve into an explicit operator affine in Lovelock order and linear in dimension. This structure proves that, apart from the branch of vanishing angular sectional curvature, the interior Schwarzschild geometry is the unique isotropic interior common to arbitrary Lovelock couplings. It also identifies the fixed-sign obstruction to bounded pure Lovelock spheres in \(d=2N+1\) with the polynomial identity \(V\equiv0\), and shows how a lower-order term or cosmological term removes this obstruction. Finally, we integrate the resulting arbitrary-order stellar equations with all admissible orders active in \(d=10\) and \(d=11\). For the positive single-parameter hierarchy of Lovelock couplings considered, the general-relativistic sequence topology persists while higher orders increase the maximum mass cumulatively.

</details>


### [106] [A parametrized test of general relativity for inspiralling eccentric binaries in LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10168)
*Pankaj Saini,Sylvain Marsat,Lorenz Zwick,János Takátsy,Johan Samsing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对偏心束缚轨道的广义相对论（GR）参数化检验框架，通过在LISA探测器观测的偏心黑洞双星系统中引入唯象偏差参数δα来修改GR预测的频率比预测。利用贝叶斯分析，研究发现LISA可以将|δα|约束至~10⁻⁴量级，且更大的初始偏心率能进一步增强约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA探测器将观测mHz频段的旋进黑洞双星，其中许多可能保留轨道偏心率。与准圆双星不同，偏心系统通过多个轨道谐波辐射，且相对论近星点进动（relativistic periastron precession）在波形中引入长期相位结构，这为利用该结构开展广义相对论检验提供了独特机会。

Method: 构造频域偏心波形模型，引入唯象偏差参数δα修改广义相对论对保守的方位角-径向频率比的预测。考虑两种参数化方案：第一种仅修改与进动相关的边带（sideband）结构；第二种将长期进动相位赋予主导的低阶角向载波并以重求和（resummed）形式保留，后者因主导波形成分相干累积修正相位而产生更强的响应。两种模型均在lisabeta软件中实现，并纳入时间与频率相关的LISA响应及时间延迟干涉（time-delay-interferometry）观测进行贝叶斯分析。

Result: 对于啁啾质量3000 M⊙、初始偏心率e₀=0.5、观测四年且信噪比（SNR）为50的双星系统，第二种模型给出90%置信度下|δα|≲10⁻⁴的约束。增大偏心率可引入更多谐波结构并减少双星参数间的简并，从而进一步收紧约束。

Conclusion: 所建立的框架具有一般性，可扩展到更完整的偏心波形模型。偏心轨道为基于LISA的广义相对论参数化检验提供了强有力的途径。

Abstract: The space-based detector Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe inspiralling black hole binaries in the mHz band, many of which may retain orbital eccentricity. In contrast to quasicircular binaries, eccentric systems radiate through multiple orbital harmonics, while relativistic periastron precession introduces a secular phase structure in the waveform. We exploit this structure to develop a parametrized test of general relativity (GR) for eccentric bound orbits. We construct a frequency-domain eccentric waveform model in which a phenomenological deviation parameter $δα$ modifies the GR prediction for the conservative azimuthal-to-radial frequency ratio, with $δα=0$ corresponding to GR. We consider two parametrizations of this deformation. In the first, $δα$ modifies only the explicit precession-dependent sideband structures of the waveform. In the second, the secular precession phase is assigned to the dominant lower-order angular carriers and retained in resummed form. The latter produces a substantially stronger response because the dominant waveform components coherently accumulate the modified phase. We implement both models within \textsc{lisabeta} and perform a Bayesian analysis including the time- and frequency-dependent LISA response and associated time-delay-interferometry observables. We find that LISA can place stringent constraints on the parametrized deviation. For a binary with chirp mass $3000 M_{\odot}$, initial eccentricity $e_0=0.5$ observed for four years at an SNR of $50$, the second model yields a $90\%$ credible bound of $|δα|\lesssim 10^{-4}$. Increasing eccentricity further sharpens the constraints by introducing additional harmonic structure and reducing degeneracies among the binary parameters. The framework developed here is general and can be extended to more complete eccentric waveform models.

</details>


### [107] [Late-Time Cosmic Acceleration in Hořava-Lifshitz Gravity: Observational Evidence from Cosmic Chronometers and Pantheon+SHOES Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10242)
*Shivani Sharma,R. Chaubey*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Horava-Lifshitz（HL）引力理论的宇宙学含义，采用一个红移依赖的减速参数q(z)参数化形式，通过MCMC方法结合宇宙学时钟（CC）与Pantheon+SHOES数据集进行约束，确认宇宙处于加速膨胀阶段，并发现该模型在低红移下与ΛCDM一致，在高红移下存在轻微偏差，最终宇宙年龄估计t₀≈13.7 Gyr，与Planck 2018约束一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Horava-Lifshitz（HL）引力的宇宙学预测与标准宇宙学模型进行对比，并通过一个具有对数修正的减速参数q(z)参数化形式来捕获宇宙从早期减速到晚期加速的转变过程，从而检验HL引力的有效性。

Method: 采用形式为q(z)=q₀+q₁ln(1+z)/[1+n·ln(1+z)]的红移依赖减速参数，由此解析推导出哈勃参数H(z)。利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法分别及联合用宇宙学时钟（cosmic chronometer, CC）和Pantheon+SHOES数据集约束模型参数H₀、q₀、q₁和n，并运用{r,s}态发现参数（statefinder）与Om(z)诊断对模型进行区分。

Result: 所有数据集组合下q₀<0，确认宇宙持续加速膨胀；重建的H(z)在低红移下与ΛCDM一致，高红移处存在轻微偏差；{r,s}态发现参数表明模型平缓演化，从Chaplygin气体行为出发，穿越ΛCDM固定点，最终进入类quintessence晚期阶段；Om(z)诊断呈现全负斜率，指示quintessence型暗能量（w>-1）；独立与联合数据集给出的现今宇宙年龄t₀≈13.7 Gyr，与Planck 2018结果一致。

Conclusion: 该q(z)参数化模型能够在HL引力框架下成功描述从减速到加速的宇宙演化转变，与观测数据良好吻合，并在低红移行为上与ΛCDM兼容；该模型呈现quintessence型暗能量的特征，且宇宙年龄估计与Planck 2018约束一致，表明HL引力在宇宙学层面具有可行性。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological implications of Horava-Lifshitz (HL) gravity using a redshift-dependent deceleration parameter of the form
  $q(z) = q_0 + \frac{q_1 \ln(1+z)}{1 + n\ln(1+z)}$, from which the Hubble parameter $H(z)$ is derived analytically. This parametrization captures the transition from early deceleration to late-time acceleration, with a logarithmic correction governed by $n$ that distinguishes it from standard kinematic models. Model parameters $H_0$, $q_0$, $q_1$, and $n$ are constrained via MCMC using cosmic chronometer (CC) and Pantheon+SHOES datasets, individually and in combination. Across all dataset combinations, $q_0 < 0$, confirming ongoing accelerated expansion. The reconstructed $H(z)$ is consistent with $Λ$CDM at low redshifts, with mild deviations at higher redshifts. The $\{r, s\}$ statefinder parameters indicate that the model evolves smoothly, beginning with Chaplygin gas-type behaviour, crossing the $Λ$CDM fixed point, and settling into a quintessence-like phase at late times. The $Om(z)$ diagnostic reveals negative slopes throughout, indicating quintessence-like dark energy ($w > -1$). Present-day cosmic age estimates from the individual and combined datasets yield $t_0 \approx 13.7$ Gyr, in agreement with Planck 2018 constraints.

</details>


### [108] [On the nature of oscillating modes of proto-neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10264)
*Dimitra Tseneklidou,Alejandro Torres-Forne,Pablo Cerda-Duran,Martin Obergaulinger,Jose Antonio Font*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于恢复力（restoring force）能量来源的新型分类方案，用于对核心坍缩超新星（core-collapse supernovae, CCSNe）中由停滞吸积激波包围的新生原中子星（proto-neutron star, PNS）的振荡模态进行分类。该方法应用于28个不同数值代码、不同前身星和状态方程的一维、二维和三维CCSN模拟，识别出与PNS和激波分别关联的两族f模和p模，以及源自两个对流稳定区域的两族g模，并确定PNS的f模为主导的高频辐射模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生原中子星是引力波星震学（gravitational wave asteroseismology）的潜在目标，但此前对模态按激发物理机制进行识别与分类存在分歧，需要一种物理动机明确且系统化的分类方案。

Method: 提出一种基于模态恢复力（restoring force）能量的物理驱动分类方案，通过考察模态所源自的CCSN不同区域来确定其性质，并将该方案应用于28个采用两种不同数值代码、不同前身星和状态方程（equation of state）的非旋转1D、2D和3D CCSN模拟。

Result: 识别出两类f模和p模族，分别与PNS和激波相关联；识别出两类g模族，源自PNS核心和表面附近的两个对流稳定区域。发现主要的高频辐射模态是PNS的f模，与其表面的强密度梯度相关。

Conclusion: 该分类程序是自动化的，对所有考虑的模型均表现一致，为系统研究PNS性质与模态频率的依赖关系铺平了道路，可直接应用于未来引力波信号观测的参数估计。

Abstract: Newborn proto-neutron stars (PNS) are potential targets of gravitational wave asteroseismology, the study of the (inner) structure of stars via their oscillation modes. To prepare for the eventual detection of such modes by current and future gravitational wave observatories, theoretical studies have obtained the possible spectrum of oscillations. However, there has been disagreement when it comes to identifying and classifying specific modes according to the physical mechanism that excites them. In this paper, we present a novel scheme to classify the oscillation modes of a newly born PNS surrounded by a stalled accretion shock in core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe). Our classification is physically motivated, as it is based on the energy of the restoring forces of the mode. We investigate the nature of the modes by considering the different regions of the CCSN that they stem from. We apply this scheme to a set of 28 non-rotating 1D, 2D and 3D CCSN simulations performed with two different numerical codes and using different progenitors and equations of state. In that way, we find that there are different families of f-, p- and g-modes in the system, living in different areas. More specifically, we identify two families of f- and p-modes associated with the PNS and the shock, respectively, and two families of g-modes originating from the two convectively stable regions, one in the PNS core and one near its surface. We also find that the dominant high-frequency emission mode is the f-mode of the PNS, associated with the strong density gradient at its surface. Our classification procedure is automatic, performs consistently for all the models considered, and paves the way to systematic studies of the dependence of the mode frequencies on the PNS properties, with direct application to parameter estimation from future observations of gravitational wave signals.

</details>


### [109] [Probing scalarized wormholes through quasi-periodic oscillations and spinning particle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10469)
*Asalkhon Alimova,Akbar Davlataliev,Farruh Atamurotov,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Phongpichit Channuie,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-标量场理论框架下，研究了三参数标量化虫洞时空中无自旋与自旋测试粒子的动力学，推导了轨道频率与共振谱，并分析了碰撞能量，发现标量耦合与自旋-曲率耦合的联合效应会在准周期振荡（QPO）频率和碰撞过程上留下可观测特征，可用于区分标量化虫洞与标准黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标量化虫洞（scalarized wormhole）与标准黑洞在可观测物理现象上的区别，通过粒子动力学、QPO谱和碰撞能量等信号寻找潜在的可鉴别特征。

Method: 对于无自旋粒子，推导了轨道频率和进动频率（epicyclic frequencies），并利用ER3和ER4共振模型计算双峰QPO谱；对于自旋粒子，采用Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon形式主义分析自旋-曲率耦合对有效势和最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）的影响；在虫洞喉部附近分析了粒子碰撞动力学。

Result: 标量耦合参数 g_s 使最内稳定圆轨道半径增大并系统性地修正3:2共振条件；自旋-曲率耦合显著改变有效势和ISCO参数，且最大物理允许自旋随标量耦合单调增大；反平行（anti-aligned）自旋构型在虫洞喉部附近产生显著更高的碰撞能量。

Conclusion: 标量耦合与自旋-曲率相互作用的联合效应会在QPO频率和碰撞过程中留下独特的可观测印记，为区分标量化虫洞与标准黑洞提供了潜在的可观测信号。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of test particles in a three-parameter scalarized wormhole spacetime within Einstein-scalar field theory. For spinless particles, we derive the orbital and epicyclic frequencies and compute twin-peak QPO spectra using the ER3 and ER4 resonance models. The scalar coupling parameter $g_s$ shifts the innermost stable circular orbit to larger radii and systematically modifies the characteristic 3:2 resonance condition. Extending to spinning particles via the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon formalism, we find that spin-curvature coupling significantly alters the effective potential and innermost stable circular orbit parameters. The maximum physically admissible spin increases monotonically with the scalar coupling. Analysis of particle collisions near the wormhole throat reveals that both scalar coupling and relative spin orientation determine collision energetics, with anti-aligned spin configurations producing substantially higher energies. Our results suggest that the combined effects of scalar coupling and spin-curvature interaction leave distinct imprints on QPO frequencies and collision processes, potentially providing observable signatures for distinguishing scalarized wormholes from standard black holes.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum-Corrected Entropy Bounds on Black Hole Merger Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10477)
*Jiswin Varghese*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究广义不确定性原理（Generalized Uncertainty Principle, GUP）描述下的量子引力修正对黑洞并合熵平衡的影响，针对两个非旋转、不等质量史瓦西黑洞的合并过程，分析了从初始双星系统到最终并合产物的热力学演化，并考虑引力波辐射造成的能量损耗。基于二次型GUP推导的对数修正熵，作者得到了作为引力波效率和质量比函数的广义熵变表达式。经典面积定理在适当极限下得以恢复，而GUP修正引入的量子修正熵界约束了最大允许的引力波能量辐射，该约束在近等质量并合时最为严格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应对黑洞热力学及并合过程的影响，特别是GUP修正如何改变经典黑洞面积定理所描述的熵平衡关系，以及这种修正对引力波能量辐射的约束作用。

Method: 聚焦两个非旋转、不等质量史瓦西黑洞的并合过程，分析从初始双星到最终并合残骸的热力学演化，显式计入引力波发射导致的能量损失。采用由二次型GUP激发的对数修正熵，推导作为引力波效率和并合组件质量比函数的广义熵变表达式。

Result: 推导出含GUP修正的广义熵变公式，在适当极限下恢复经典面积定理；GUP修正引入了一个量子修正的熵界，约束最大允许的引力波能量发射量，该约束对近等质量并合系统最为严格，表明对称系统对量子引力效应更敏感。

Conclusion: 量子引力修正（通过GUP刻画）显著影响黑洞并合过程中的熵平衡，并在对称性更高的（近等质量）并合系统中产生更明显的约束效应，为量子引力可观测效应提供了新的理论窗口。

Abstract: We examine the entropy balance of black hole mergers in the presence of quantum gravity corrections described by the Generalized Uncertainty Principle (GUP). Focusing on the coalescence of two non-spinning Schwarzschild black holes with unequal masses, we analyze the thermodynamic evolution from the initial binary system to the final merger remnant, explicitly accounting for energy loss through gravitational-wave emission. Using a logarithmically corrected entropy motivated by a quadratic GUP, we derive a generalized entropy change as a function of the gravitational-wave efficiency and the mass ratio of the merging components. The classical area theorem is recovered in the appropriate limit, while the GUP correction introduces a quantum-modified entropy bound that constrains the maximum allowed gravitational-wave energy emission. This bound is most restrictive for nearly equal-mass mergers, indicating an enhanced sensitivity of symmetric systems to quantum gravity effects.

</details>


### [111] [The generalized second law as a thermodynamic selection criterion for dynamical dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10495)
*Marcos H. Cruz,Miguel Cruz,Joel Saavedra,Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于广义第二定律（generalized second law, GSL）提出一种判别广义视界熵模型的物理原则，在一般修正Friedmann框架下推导出渐近熵标度指数k的模型无关约束，并证明多数常用广义熵模型受其限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着广义视界熵方案的增多，产生了大量修正宇宙学模型，但目前缺乏能够区分它们的通用物理原理。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用广义第二定律作为判别准则。

Method: 考虑由任意Hubble参数函数f(H)描述的一般修正Friedmann框架，允许视界与宇宙流体偏离热平衡，推导视界熵渐近标度S_A∝A^k的模型无关约束。同时分析精确热平衡极限下的动力学耦合限制，并将准则推广至含导数项的f(H,Ḣ)宇宙学。

Result: 幽灵（phantom）演化要求k≥(3ω-1)/(2ω)，而quintessence则给出互补的上界；热平衡极限下动力学耦合严格限制k≤2；两界在幻影分界处收敛至唯一值k=2，对应二次熵-面积标度（对数引力框架）。多参数广义熵模型天然与该渐近行为相容，而许多常用方案受到限制。

Conclusion: 广义第二定律提供了一种能够区分广义视界熵模型的普遍热力学选择原理，且该原则可推广至依赖导数的宇宙学框架，表明其适用范围超越仅依赖视界面积的熵泛函。

Abstract: The growing number of generalized horizon entropy proposals has led to a wide variety of modified cosmological models, yet there is currently no general physical principle capable of discriminating among them. We show that the generalized second law (GSL) of thermodynamics provides such a criterion. Considering a general modified Friedmann framework described by an arbitrary function $f(H)$ of the Hubble parameter, and allowing the apparent horizon and the cosmic fluid to evolve out of thermal equilibrium, we derive model-independent constraints on the asymptotic scaling of the horizon entropy, $S_A\propto A^k$. We find that phantom evolution requires $k\ge(3ω-1)/(2ω)$, with $ω$ the total fluid equation of state, whereas quintessence imposes the complementary upper bound. Additionally, in the exact thermal equilibrium limit, the dynamical coupling strictly enforces $k \le 2$, recovering the non-equilibrium quintessence bound. The two bounds converge to the unique value $k=2$ as the phantom divide is approached, indicating that a smooth crossing of the phantom divide is thermodynamically associated with a quadratic entropy-area scaling, where the effective theory degenerates into a logarithmic gravity framework. Applying this criterion to representative generalized entropy models shows that many commonly used proposals are constrained by the generalized second law, whereas multiparameter constructions are naturally compatible with the required asymptotic behavior. Finally, we demonstrate that the same thermodynamic selection principle extends to derivative-dependent cosmologies described by $f(H,\dot H)$, highlighting its robustness beyond entropy functionals that depend only on the horizon area.

</details>


### [112] [Two-Fluid Schwarzschild Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10552)
*Rico Zöllner,Burkhard Kämpfer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究包含两种流体的内施瓦西解（interior Schwarzschild solution），用于建模含混合暗物质（dark matter）的致密（中子）星。两种具有恒定能量密度的流体仅通过共同引力场相互作用，一般情形下双流体核心被单流体冕（corona）包裹，系统紧凑度上限不超过8/9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述含暗物质成分的中子星等致密天体，需要将经典内施瓦西解推广至双流体情形，从而研究两种流体在引力场中的共存与分布特性。

Method: 将内施瓦西解推广至两种恒定能量密度流体（e1、e2）的情形，流体间仅通过公共引力场相互作用；模型由能量密度与中心压力（pc1、pc2）等参数描述，并分析双流体核心与单流体冕的构型条件。

Result: 仅在特定参数下两种流体占据相同区域；在该区域两种能量密度叠加后，紧凑度（compactness）界限8/9仍未被超过。一种可能的物态是某种流体（如暗物质）从双流体核心显著泄漏至外围。

Conclusion: 双流体内施瓦西解为建模暗物质混合致密星提供了可行框架，且即使在两种流体重叠区域，紧凑度上限依然得到保持，并可容许暗物质向核心外围泄漏的构型。

Abstract: We consider the interior two-fluid Schwarzschild solution. That is a model of compact (neutron) stars with admixed dark matter. The two constant energy densities generalize the interior Schwarschild solution to the case of two fluids (representing standard model matter and dark matter, for instance), interacting mutually solely by the common gravitational field. In general, the two-fluid core is surrounded by a one-fluid corona (envelope). Only for special parameters (the model depends on energy densities $e_1,e_2$ and central pressures $p_{\mathrm{c}1},p_{\mathrm{c}2}$ of the fluids), both fluids occupy the same region. Despite the sum of two energy densities in that region, the compactness bound of 8/9 is not exceeded. A conceivable object is one with a stark leakage of one fluid (e.g. dark matter) beyond the two-fluid core.

</details>


### [113] [Octupolar Gravitational Radiation in de Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10593)
*Harsh,Omkar Shetye,Amitabh Virmani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在de Sitter时空的未来Poincaré片（future Poincaré patch）中，利用广义谐和规范（generalised harmonic gauge），在多重极展开（multipolar expansion）的八极阶（octupolar order）上推导了局域物质源产生的引力辐射，是de Sitter多重极展开首次超越四极阶（quadrupolar order）的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前de Sitter时空中的多重极展开仅限于四极阶，需要将其推广到更高阶以建立更系统的引力辐射理论。

Method: 在广义谐和规范下显式求解标量、矢量和张量微扰方程并纳入宇宙学尾部（cosmological tail）效应；引入一个普适的多极矩微分恒等式（multipole moment differentiation identity），将多极矩关系统一为单一递归公式；利用Blanchet、Damour和Iyer的对称无迹（symmetric trace-free）张量形式体系，将辐射解系统地转换到球谐函数（spherical harmonic）基下，并应用于四极和八极度量微扰。

Result: 成功推导了八极阶的de Sitter引力辐射解，并通过球谐函数表示使辐射的角向结构显式化，简化了线性化场方程的验证，促进了与Bondi规范（Bondi gauge）及其他de Sitter微扰处理的直接比较。

Conclusion: 该工作为建立系统的“多重极后de Sitter形式体系（multipolar post-de Sitter formalism）”迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We derive the gravitational radiation generated by a localised matter source in de Sitter spacetime at octupolar order in the multipolar expansion, working in generalised harmonic gauge in the future Poincaré patch. The scalar, vector, and tensor perturbation equations are solved explicitly, taking into account the cosmological tail. This constitutes the first extension of the de Sitter multipolar expansion beyond quadrupolar order. A key technical ingredient is a universal multipole moment differentiation identity, which unifies the relations between multipole moments into a single recursive formula and enables a controlled extension to higher multipoles. Using the symmetric trace-free tensor formalism of Blanchet, Damour, and Iyer, we further develop a systematic translation of the radiative solution into a spherical harmonic basis, and apply it to both the quadrupolar and octupolar metric perturbations. This representation renders the angular structure of the radiation manifest, simplifies the verification of the linearised field equations, and facilitates direct comparison with Bondi-gauge and other perturbative treatments of de Sitter spacetime. Our results constitute a step toward a systematic ``multipolar post-de Sitter formalism''.

</details>


### [114] [Radiative Signatures from Warp Drives Traveling Through the Earth's Atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10800)
*Shaun David Brocus Fell,Abraham Loeb*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟分析零ADM质量曲速驱动时空泡穿越地球大气层时的可观测特征，发现以相对论速度运动的飞行器尺度时空泡与大气相互作用可产生超过太瓦（terawatt）量级的亮度，从而为地外曲速驱动推进系统建立观测约束并提供识别框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨曲速驱动（warp drive）时空推进技术在地球环境中运行时会产生何种可探测的物理信号，进而为这种假设性推进方式建立观测约束条件。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，对零ADM质量曲速驱动时空泡处于不同运动状态（静止、低速及相对论速度）下穿越地球大气层时的相互作用过程进行建模，评估其产生的亮度特征。

Result: 模拟结果显示，当飞行器尺度的时空泡以相对论速度运动时，其与大气相互作用可产生超过一太瓦的发光度；而静止或相对于地球低速运动的时空泡则不会产生如此极端的亮度信号。特别地，以超过光速约10%的速度在大气层中穿行的曲速驱动会产生独特的明亮辉光。

Conclusion: 研究结果为大气环境中基于时空的推进系统建立了观测约束，同时为识别潜在的高速曲速驱动信号提供了方法论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the observable signatures of zero ADM mass warp drive spacetimes traversing Earth's atmosphere. Numerical simulations indicate that an aircraft-scale spacetime bubble moving at relativistic velocities would have a pronounced observational signature, where interaction with the atmosphere can produce luminosities exceeding one terawatt. The signature of a spacetime bubble at rest or moving at low velocity relative to the Earth would not generate such extreme luminosities. These results establish observational constraints on spacetime-based propulsion operating within the terrestrial environment and provide a framework for identifying potential high-velocity signatures. In particular, a warp drive traveling through the atmosphere at speeds exceeding approximately 10% of the speed of light would produce a unique brilliant glow.

</details>


### [115] [Conductors and superconductors in stationary spacetimes: generalized Schiff-Barnhill and Meissner effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10814)
*F. Masghatian,M. Nouri-Zonoz,H. Sadegh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于伦敦（London）唯象理论，在不采用弱场慢速极限假设的前提下，研究了稳态时空（stationary spacetimes）中超导体的电动力学，并在两种时空分解形式下推导了广义Schiff-Barnhill效应和Meissner效应，且从第二伦敦方程独立验证了广义Meissner效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对弯曲时空中导体和超导体及其引力感应电磁场的研究大多局限于弱场和低速极限，且忽略了这些现象的观测者依赖性（observer-dependence）。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立无需弱场和低速近似的超导体稳态时空电动力学理论。

Method: 作者遵循伦敦对超导的唯象方法（phenomenological approach），定义带电粒子在引力场和电磁场中受到的3-力（3-force），并在一股时空分解（spacetime decomposition）框架下推导其显式形式。随后将力方程应用于稳态时空超导体中的自由电荷，分别在threading和slicing两种分解形式下，结合超导态稳态条件，推导广义Schiff-Barnhill效应与广义Meissner效应。

Result: 得到了广义Schiff-Barnhill效应和广义Meissner效应，与弱场低速极限相比，它们包含来自新引入的引力感应本构方程（gravitationally-induced constitutive equations）的额外项。此外，作者从第二伦敦方程（second London equation）推导出广义Meissner效应，验证了方法的自洽性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架无需弱场和低速近似，适用于稳态时空中超导体的电动力学描述，并明确体现了观测者角色在引力-电磁耦合效应中的重要性。

Abstract: Previous studies on conductors and superconductors in curved spacetimes, and the gravitationally-induced electric and magnetic fields in them, are mostly restricted to the limit of weak fields and low velocities. Also the observer-dependence of these phenomena are not discussed in the literature. In this article, following London's phenomenological approach to superconductivity, we consider the electrodynamics of superconductors in stationary spacetimes without employing any weak field or low velocity limit. To this end, first we define the 3-force acting on charged particles in gravitational and electromagnetic fields, and derive its explicit form in a general spacetime decomposition formalism. Then, to highlight the role of observers, we apply the force equation to the free charges in superconductors in stationary spacetimes in the two well known threading and slicing decomposition formalisms. Applying the stationary condition in the superconducting state, we arrive at the generalized Schiff-Barnhill and Meissner effects, which compared to the low velocity and weak field limit include extra terms originating from the recently introduced gravitationally-induced constitutive equations. To show the consistency of our approach, we also derive the generalized Meissner effect from the second London equation in stationary spacetimes.

</details>


### [116] [Constraining ModMax Black Holes with EHT and GRAVITY Observations: Optical Signatures and Accretion Disk Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10859)
*Tursunali Xamidov,Mirjavoxir Mirkhaydarov,Sanjar Shaymatov,Pankaj Sheoran,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究考察了ModMax黑洞周围薄吸积盘的光子传播与辐射性质，分析了电荷Q和非线性参数v对事件视界、光子球和阴影半径的影响，并利用M87*与Sgr A*的EHT阴影观测数据通过MCMC分析给出了Q<0.391和v<4.153（95%置信度）的观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索ModMax黑洞理论参数（电荷与非线参数）如何影响黑洞阴影和薄盘辐射，并利用现有EHT观测数据为这些参数提供观测约束。

Method: 通过解析计算确定事件视界、光子球与阴影半径；利用EHT对M87*和Sgr A*的阴影测量数据并结合已有质量和距离测量进行MCMC分析以约束ModMax参数；对Novikov-Thorne薄吸积盘使用反向光线追踪（backward ray tracing）生成模拟盘图像。

Result: 增大Q会使事件视界、光子球和阴影半径减小；增大v则使这些半径向Schwarzschild值偏移。MCMC分析给出最强的上界约束为Q<0.391和v<4.153（95%置信水平）。观测到的盘通量随Q增大而增加，而v增大会使盘亮度略微降低。

Conclusion: ModMax参数Q和v对黑洞阴影和薄吸积盘辐射均有显著影响，研究为这两个参数提供了基于EHT观测的定量约束。

Abstract: In this work, we study photon propagation and the radiative properties of a thin accretion disk around a ModMax black hole and examine the effects of the charge $Q$ (i.e. $Q^2 = Q^2_e +Q^2_m$ is the total dyonic charge, with $Q_e$ and $Q_m$ denoting the electric and magnetic charges, respectively) and the nonlinearity parameter $v$. We determine the event-horizon, photon-sphere, and shadow radii and find that increasing $Q$ decreases these radii, whereas increasing $v$ shifts them toward their Schwarzschild values. Using the EHT shadow measurements of M87$^\star$ and Sgr~A$^\star$, together with the available mass and distance measurements, we perform an MCMC analysis to constrain the ModMax parameters. The strongest upper limits are found to be $Q<0.391$ and $v<4.153$ at the 95\% credible level. We also investigate a Novikov-Thorne thin accretion disk and generate simulated disk images using backward ray tracing. The observed flux increases with $Q$, while increasing $v$ produces a slight decrease in the disk brightness. These results show how the ModMax parameters affect the black-hole shadow and thin-disk emission and provide observational constraints on $Q$ and $v$.

</details>


### [117] [Radiation-reaction driven dynamics at the third-and-a-half post-Newtonian order in different gauges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10965)
*Donato Bini,Andrea Geralico,Sara Rufrano Aliberti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文考虑了一般轨道（general orbits）下三阶半后牛顿（third-and-a-half post-Newtonian）阶辐射反作用力（radiation-reaction force）的坐标依赖定义，通过平衡方法（balance method）结合Schott项与无穷远通量来确定其表达式，并展示了如何通过坐标变换（harmonic坐标到ADM或EOB坐标）直接获得新坐标系下的辐射反作用力及其轨道修正，从而避免重新求解平衡方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射反作用力和Schott项在坐标变换下不是规范不变的（gauge-invariant），其定义依赖于坐标选择；现有工作需针对每种坐标系重新求解复杂的平衡方程，缺乏一种通用的坐标变换方法来直接推广已知的谐波坐标解。

Method: 采用平衡方法，结合系统损失的能量与角动量、Schott项以及无穷远处的能量与角动量通量来确定辐射反作用力的表达式；在哈密顿框架（Hamiltonian framework）下，利用相空间变量将谐波坐标中系统的机械能与角动量损失与新坐标系中辐射反作用力的径向和角向分量联系起来；只需坐标变换矩阵即可实现，无需重新求解平衡方程。对Arnowitt-Deser-Misner（ADM）坐标和Effective-One-Body（EOB）坐标分别推导了变换公式。

Result: 成功推导了从谐波坐标到ADM和EOB坐标的变换关系；在EOB情形下讨论了当前波形模型（waveform models）中采用的简化规范参数选择；并展示了在新坐标系中获得辐射反作用轨道修正（orbit correction）的途径——仅需对文献中已知的谐波坐标解做坐标变换即可。

Conclusion: 该方法的显著简化在于避免了重新求解辐射反作用动力学（radiation-reacted dynamics），仅通过坐标变换即可从谐波坐标解直接获得新坐标系下的辐射反作用轨道修正，为提高后牛顿理论在一般轨道情形下的应用效率提供了实用途径。

Abstract: We consider the coordinate-dependent definition of the radiation-reaction force at the third-and-a-half post-Newtonian order for general orbits. In order to (partially) determine its expression we refer to the balance method, involving the energy and angular momentum lost by the system, the Schott terms, and the energy and angular momentum fluxes at infinity. Only the latter are gauge-invariant quantities when passing from a coordinate system to another. The gauge dependence of both radiation-reaction force and Schott terms is encoded in a set of gauge parameters entering their definitions. We show how to relate the harmonic-coordinate losses of mechanical energy and angular momentum by the system with the radial and azimuthal components of the radiation-reaction force in a different coordinate system expressed in terms of phase-space variables in a Hamiltonian framework. The advantage of this approach is that only the coordinate transformation between harmonic coordinates and coordinates and momenta in the new coordinate system is needed, without solving again the balance equations. We derive such a transformation for both Arnowitt-Deser-Misner and Effective-One-Body coordinates. In the latter case we also discuss some simplifying choices of the gauge parameters adopted in current waveform models. Finally, we show how to obtain the solution for the radiation-reaction correction to the orbit in the new coordinate system simply by transforming the harmonic-coordinate solution known in the literature. This is a remarkable simplification, since one can avoid to solve again for the radiation-reacted dynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Ultracompact Anisotropic Stars and Gravastars in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10991)
*Prajwal Hassan Puttasiddappa,Nora Bretón,Santiago Esteban Perez Bergliaffa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了广义相对论（General Relativity）中各向异性应力（anisotropic stress）下的静态、球对称超致密天体（ultracompact objects），通过协变各向异性状态方程和均匀密度假设，构建了一个允许超越Buchdahl极限（Buchdahl limit）且致密度可任意接近黑洞值的近解析模型，并进一步利用厚壳（thick shell）构造消除了压力发散，得到了规整的超致密引力真空星（gravastar）构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论框架下，传统各向同性理想流体模型受Buchdahl极限约束，无法达到接近黑洞的致密度。该研究旨在探索各向异性应力如何使超致密天体超越该极限，并解决由此产生的中心压力发散问题。

Method: 采用协变各向异性状态方程（covariant anisotropic equation of state），假设天体内部具有均匀密度（homogeneous density），构造了一个以解析表达为主的模型。通过在参数空间（致密度与各向异性）中分析临界曲线，区分不同解的行为；在奇异构型情况下引入厚壳构造来消除压力发散，并据此建立各向同性与各向异性的引力真空星模型。

Result: 模型存在由致密度和异性参数空间中一条临界曲线隔开的两个区域，分别对应中心压力为正的规整各向异性构型，以及中心压力为负的奇异构型（适用于任意各向异性）。在后一种情形中，引入厚壳可消除压力发散，得到规整的超致密引力真空星构型，并成功构造了各向异性和各向同性两种引力真空星模型。

Conclusion: 各向异性应力允许致密度超越Buchdahl极限并任意接近黑洞值，而厚壳构造为消除压力奇异性、形成规整引力真空星提供了有效途径。该工作扩展了超致密天体的理论建模范围。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric ultracompact objects in General Relativity with anisotropic stress. Using the covariant anisotropic equation of state and assuming homogeneous density, we construct a mostly analytical model admitting configurations beyond the Buchdahl limit and with compactness arbitrarily close to the black hole value. The solutions exhibit two regimes separated by a critical curve in the parameter space of compactness and anisotropy, associated with the divergence of the central pressure: regular anisotropic configurations with positive central pressure, and singular configurations with negative central pressure for arbitrary anisotropy. In the latter case, we show that introducing a thick shell removes the pressure divergence, yielding regular ultracompact gravastar configurations. We further construct both anisotropic and isotropic gravastar models using this thick-shell construction.

</details>


### [119] [Periodic orbits and gravitational wave signatures from magnetic dipoles around magnetized Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11048)
*Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Chengxun Yuan,Satimbay Palvanov,Khurshida Khaknazarova,Faisal Javed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究磁化（未带电）测试粒子在旋转磁化Kerr黑洞赤道面上的周期轨道及其引力辐射。粒子携带磁偶极矩（耦合常数β），系统由外部渐近均匀磁场沉浸。利用Levin和Perez-Giz的拓扑zoom-whirl方案分类周期轨道，并在数值-克鲁奇（numerical-kludge）、受限四极近似下计算极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统的引力波波形，与LISA、Taiji和TianQin的灵敏度曲线比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁化Kerr黑洞周围携带磁偶极矩的测试粒子的轨道动力学和引力波辐射，为未来空间引力波探测器（LISA、Taiji、TianQin）提供极端质量比旋进系统的理论预测。

Method: 基于有效势推导磁化测试粒子的运动方程；计算边缘束缚轨道（MBO）和最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）作为自旋参数a和磁耦合β的函数；使用三个整数(z,w,v)及旋转数q=w+v/z的拓扑zoom-whirl方案对周期轨道分类；在数值-克鲁奇、受限四极近似下计算时域引力波波形和频域特征应变。

Result: 磁耦合β使MBO和ISCO系统性地外移，并降低其轨道能量和角动量；周期轨道的zoom-whirl结构直接印刻在波形爆发状形态上；对于足够近且大质量的源，所产生的引力波信号落入未来空间探测器（LISA、Taiji、TianQin）的灵敏度频带内。

Conclusion: 磁化Kerr黑洞周围磁化粒子的周期轨道可在(能量-角动量, L-E)平面映射束缚运动允许区域，其引力波信号有望被即将上天的空间探测器探测到，为EMRI系统观测提供了重要理论支撑。

Abstract: We study periodic orbits and the associated gravitational radiation of a magnetized (uncharged) test particle carrying a magnetic dipole moment with coupling constant beta, moving in the equatorial plane of a rotating, magnetized Kerr black hole immersed in an external asymptotically uniform magnetic field, starting from the effective potential derived for such particles. We compute the marginally bound orbit (MBO) and the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) as functions of the black hole spin a and the magnetic coupling beta, and map out the allowed region of the orbital energy-angular momentum (L, E) plane for bound motion. We then classify periodic orbits using the topological zoom-whirl scheme of Levin and Perez-Giz, characterized by three integers (z,w,v) through the rational rotation number q=w+v/z, and construct a family of closed rosette orbits at fixed angular momentum. Using the numerical-kludge, restricted-quadrupole approximation for an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral consisting of a stellar-mass magnetized secondary orbiting a supermassive magnetized Kerr black hole, we compute the time-domain gravitational waveforms h_+(t), h_\times(t) produced by these periodic orbits and their frequency-domain characteristic strain, and compare the latter with the anticipated instrumental sensitivity curves of LISA, Taiji and TianQin. We find that the magnetic coupling βsystematically shifts the MBO and ISCO outward and lowers their orbital energy and angular momentum, that the zoom-whirl structure of the periodic orbits is imprinted directly on the burst-like morphology of the emitted waveform, and that the resulting gravitational-wave signals fall within the sensitivity band of upcoming space-based detectors for suitably close and massive sources.

</details>


### [120] [Quasinormal modes in effective loop quantum gravity and consequences for isospectrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11088)
*Alejandro García-Quismondo,Guillermo A. Mena Marugán,Alvaro Torres-Caballeros*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了圈量子引力（loop quantum gravity）混合方法下有效Ashtekar–Olmedo–Singh黑洞几何中轴性和极性微扰的准正则模（quasinormal mode）谱，并与dressed metric方法的先前结果对比，发现等谱性（isospectrality）破缺在两种方法中均存在且量级相近，同时评估了WKB近似在该有效分析中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在圈量子引力的不同量子化方案（混合与dressed metric方法）下，黑洞微扰准正则模谱是否一致以及轴性–极性等谱性破缺是否普遍存在，是检验量子引力修正效应的重要问题。

Method: 从经典方程的有效对应形式出发，采用带Padé重求和的高阶WKB方法计算有效Ashtekar–Olmedo–Singh黑洞几何中轴性和极性的准正则模谱，并与dressed metric方法的已有结果进行比较。

Result: 等谱性破缺在混合量子化方案中显著存在，其量级与dressed metric有效情况同阶但略大；两种情形下破缺均随黑洞质量的三次方根衰减，最终恢复经典Schwarzschild极限；同时确认了WKB方法在特定参数区域可靠，并识别出近似失效的具体模式。

Conclusion: 等谱性破缺是圈量子引力有效黑洞描述中两种量子化方案共同的特征性结果，其随质量坍缩至经典极限的行为支持量子修正的一致性；WKB方法的适用性边界也需在实际分析中加以明确界定。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal mode spectra of axial and polar perturbations about the effective Ashtekar, Olmedo, and Singh black hole geometry within the hybrid approach to loop quantum gravity. We also compare our results with those previously obtained by del-Corral and Olmedo for the dressed metric approach. Starting from mode equations that are straightforward effective counterparts of the classical equations, we compute these spectra by means of a high-order WKB method with Padé resummation. The violation of isospectrality between axial and polar perturbations that was claimed to exist in the dressed metric approach persists with the hybrid quantization, with deviations of the same order of magnitude as in the former effective case, although slightly larger for the hybrid prescription. In both situations, the isospectrality breaking decreases with the cubic root of the squared black hole mass in Planck units, consistently recovering the classical Schwarzschild limit. In addition, we perform a careful assessment of the applicability of the WKB approximation to the present effective analysis, confirming the parameter regions where the method remains reliable and highlighting specific mode cases for which the approximation breaks down.

</details>


### [121] [All You Need is not $Ω_\mathrm{gw}$: Beyond the Mean of the Cross-Correlation Estimator when Searching for an Astrophysical Gravitational-Wave Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11119)
*Haowen Zhong,Francesco Iacovelli,Vuk Mandic,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了地面引力波探测器随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background, SGWB）互相关估计量C̅f的高斯性假设，将其分解为平均强度、几何散粒噪声和偏振泄漏三个物理分量，并基于局域并合率不确定性传播，计算了二元黑洞、二元中子星和中子星-黑洞三种天体物理源群体的四阶累积量90%可信区间。结果表明，在LIGO O5灵敏度下C̅f对三种源群体均保持高斯性，但在Cosmic Explorer（CE）灵敏度下，二元黑洞产生的C̅f呈现显著非高斯性，偏度与超额峰度分别达0.31和1.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于交叉相关估计量的引力波背景推断框架普遍假设该估计量满足高斯性（Gaussianity），但这一假设对于致密双星并合产生的天体物理背景尚未经过系统性检验，在下一代探测器灵敏度下其有效性存疑。

Method: 将交叉相关估计量C̅f分解为三个具有物理含义的分量——平均强度（mean intensity）、几何散粒噪声（geometrical shot noise）和偏振泄漏（polarization leakage），并通过传播局域并合率不确定性（local merger rate uncertainties），为三种并合源群体计算该估计量直至四阶的累积量（cumulants）的90%可信区间。

Result: 在LIGO O5灵敏度下，C̅f对三种源群体均保持有效高斯性。在Cosmic Explorer（CE）灵敏度下（一年观测期、最灵敏频带），二元黑洞产生的C̅f呈现非高斯性，偏度（skewness）为0.31（+0.04/−0.03），超额峰度（excess kurtosis）为1.3（+0.4/−0.3）；而二元中子星和中子星-黑洞群体的C̅f在CE下仍基本保持高斯性。

Conclusion: Edgeworth展开（Edgeworth expansion）能为下一代探测时代预期出现的非高斯性提供充分的领头阶描述，同时本文结果也激励了开发更精细统计方法以进行更精确处理的必要性。

Abstract: Searches for the stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) using ground-based detectors rely heavily on the cross-correlation estimator $\bar C_f$, whose statistical properties determine the theoretical foundation for inference frameworks built on it. Past analyses usually assume Gaussianity of $\bar C_f$, but this assumption has not been systematically tested for a background of astrophysical origin like the one produced by compact binary coalescences. In this work, we decompose $\bar C_f$ into three physically motivated components: (1) the mean intensity, (2) the geometrical shot noise, and (3) the polarization leakage, and we compute 90\% credible intervals for its cumulants up to fourth order for the binary black hole, binary neutron star, and neutron star-black hole populations by propagating local merger rate uncertainties. We find that for the upcoming LIGO O5 sensitivity, $\bar C_f$ remains effectively Gaussian for all three populations, validating current Gaussian likelihood frameworks. For Cosmic Explorer (CE), however, the $\bar C_f$ produced by binary black holes becomes non-Gaussian, with skewness and excess kurtosis of $0.31^{+0.04}_{-0.03}$ and $1.3^{+0.4}_{-0.3}$, respectively, in the most sensitive band over a one-year observation period. For binary neutron star and neutron star - black hole mergers, $\bar C_f$ remains largely Gaussian even at CE. These results indicate that the Edgeworth expansion provides an adequate leading-order description of the non-Gaussianity expected in the next-generation era, while also motivating the development of more sophisticated statistical methods for a more accurate treatment.

</details>
