### [122] [Complete kinematic null for local kinetic dissipation in a sixfold-driven electron fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10031)
*P. Shubham Parashar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在一维二维电子流体中，通过六重边界图案驱动，识别出一个完整的运动学零点（kinematic null）。在D12点群的U3±通道中，设备对称性排除矢量表示和完整一阶梯度张量，使得所有局部二次耗散形式在对称固定中心消失，而在O(2)各向同性角谐运动学模型中，第一个允许的局部扇区为m=3，其加热系数为κ4=v_F^4/(16γ2²γ3)。通过不可压缩Stokes圆盘实现非零中心信号，并在局部固定电流动量守恒Stokes机制下拟合有效γ3和γ2，实现无需局部耗散的六重驱动测量点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图在二维电子流体中通过六重边界对称性构造一个特殊测量点，在该点处平凡的局部耗散（local dissipation）被完全排除，从而暴露出仅由动力学（kinetic）模式主导的信号，为区分局部流体力学描述与更基本的运动学模型提供实验判据。

Method: 采用群论分析确定D12对称性下的不可约表示（irreducible representation）约束，排除矢量表示R1和一阶梯度张量R1⊗R1；随后利用O(2)各向同性角谐运动学模型分析局部扇区，并通过不可压缩Stokes圆盘的解析解和有限动量的运动学边界值问题数值解验证对称性预测。

Result: 对称性分析表明在U3±通道中j(0)=0且∂i jj(0)=0，所有局部二次耗散形式在第一梯度阶上独立于本构系数消失；运动学模型中第一个允许的局部扇区为m=3，其热化系数为κ4=v_F^4/(16γ2²γ3)；Stokes圆盘解给出了非零的中心信号，与有限动量运动学解一致，并可通过磁响应拟合有效γ3和γ2。

Conclusion: 六重驱动力创造出一种测量机制，在六重驱动下普通局部电荷/动量流体力学耗散被消除，而动力学模式保持有限，从而为探测超出局部流体力学框架的动力学效应提供了独特的实验途径。

Abstract: We identify a complete kinematic null in a two-dimensional electron fluid driven by a sixfold boundary pattern. In the $U_3^\pm$ channel of $D_{12}$, device symmetry excludes both the vector representation $R_1$ and the full first-gradient tensor $R_1\otimes R_1$. Hence at the symmetry-fixed center $\mathbf{j}(0)=0$ and $\partial_i j_j(0)=0$, so every local quadratic dissipative form built from the modeled charge/momentum field through first gradient order vanishes independently of the constitutive coefficients. In an $O(2)$-isotropic angular-harmonic kinetic model the first allowed local sector is $m=3$. For $νq^2/γ_3\ll1$, its heating is set by the previously derived coefficient $κ_4=v_F^4/(16γ_2^2γ_3)$. An explicit incompressible Stokes disk realizes a nonzero center signal, and a finite-moment kinetic boundary-value solution approaches the corresponding local benchmark. Within the local, fixed-current momentum-conserving Stokes regime, the normalized magnetic response can then be fitted for effective $γ_3$ and $γ_2$. Thus the sixfold drive creates a measurement point where ordinary local charge/momentum hydrodynamic dissipation is absent while a kinetic mode remains finite.

</details>


### [123] [Nanoscale imaging of ferromagnetic vortex dynamics with scanning NV magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10310)
*Jeffrey Rable,Jyotirmay Dwivedi,Nitin Samarth,Paul Stevenson,Arun Bansil,Swastik Kar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用扫描氮空位磁测量（SNVM）技术，在介观坡莫合金（permalloy）结构中实现了对磁性涡旋（vortex）产生的静态磁场和微波场的成像，空间分辨率约50纳米，与微磁模拟高度一致，并在涡旋核心附近观测到40倍的微波场增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波（spin wave）在纳米尺度上的产生与操控对磁性（magnonic）和量子技术具有重要应用价值，但此前只有静态涡旋动力学的局域表征实验，缺乏对涡旋动态微波场的直接成像研究。

Method: 利用扫描氮空位磁测量（SNVM）技术，对介观坡莫合金结构中的磁性涡旋进行静态磁场和微波场的同步成像，并结合微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）对比验证，分析无序效应对微波场分布的影响。

Result: 实现了约50纳米空间分辨率的涡旋静态场与微波场成像，与微磁模拟高度吻合；在涡旋核心附近观察到40倍的微波场增强，并成像了依赖无序分布的、空间变化的倏逝波（evanescent decay）衰减特性。该技术在环境条件下运行，分辨率超过衍射极限技术至少5倍，且比基于同步辐射的技术具有更高的可及性和通量。

Conclusion: 该环境（ambient）、台面式（tabletop）SNVM成像技术为研究和发展磁性器件提供了一种高分辨率、高可及性的新工具，为磁性涡旋与微波场相互作用的理解及磁振子器件（magnonic devices）的开发开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: The generation and manipulation of spin waves at the nanoscale via magnetic vortices are of considerable importance because of their broad applications across magnonic and quantum technologies. Previously, fixed nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond have been used to locally characterize vortex dynamics, and scanning NV magnetometry (SNVM) has been used to image vortices' static stray fields. Here, we demonstrate SNVM imaging of both the static and microwave fields generated by vortices in mesoscopic permalloy structures with $\sim$50 nm spatial resolution, achieving excellent agreement with micromagnetic simulations, while revealing the effects of disorder. We further demonstrate a 40$\times$ microwave field enhancement near a vortex core and image the disorder-dependent, spatially varying, evanescent decay of these microwaves. Our ambient, tabletop technique surpasses diffraction-limited techniques' resolutions by at least 5$\times$, with far greater accessibility and throughput than synchrotron radiation-based techniques, offering new opportunities in the study and development of magnonic devices.

</details>


### [124] [Real-Time FPGA-Based Multi-Parameter Feedback Stabilization of a Silicon Double Quantum Dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10325)
*Johnathan Bryan,Tim J. Wilson,Hong-Wen Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在硅双量子点器件中，利用基于现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）的OPX控制器扩展了梯度下降反馈（GDFB）方法，通过射频反射计读数和数字信号处理实现高速稳定化，在最高5 kHz带宽内实现-6 dB噪声抑制，有效压制低频1/f噪声并维持器件长期稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体自旋量子比特（semiconductor spin qubit）的长期运行需要主动稳定量子点势能，以对抗低频电荷噪声。此前基于输运电流测量的梯度下降反馈方法反馈带宽有限，亟需提升反馈速度以满足大规模量子点阵列的实时控制需求。

Method: 在配备高带宽射频反射计（rf-reflectometry）读数的硅双量子点器件中，利用Quantum Machines公司的OPX控制器（基于现场可编程门阵列实现数字信号处理）扩展梯度下降反馈方法。OPX支持连续多参数梯度计算和快速栅极电压更新，每个反馈周期执行8步操作，积分时间设为25.6微秒。

Result: 该高速稳定化方案在最高5 kHz带宽内实现-6 dB的噪声抑制，有效压制低频1/f噪声，并能在更长时间段内维持器件稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方案显著提高了有效反馈带宽，相比此前工作大幅增强噪声抑制能力，有望为大规模量子点阵列实现实时控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: Long term operation of semiconductor spin qubits requires active stabilization of quantum dot potentials against low-frequency charge noise. Previous work demonstrated a gradient-descent feedback (GDFB) approach on a silicon double quantum dot utilizing transport current measurements. We extend such a GDFB approach in a silicon double quantum dot device with high-bandwidth rf-reflectometry readout by utilizing a field-programmable gate array, the OPX by Quantum Machines, for digital signal processing. The OPX enables continuous multi-parameter gradient calculation and quick gate voltage updates, significantly increasing the effective feedback bandwidth compared to previous work. By operating with 8 steps per feedback cycle and an integration time of 25.6 $μ$s, this high speed stabilization scheme achieves a -6 dB noise suppression up to a bandwidth of 5 kHz. This effectively suppresses low frequency 1/f noise, maintaining device stability over longer time periods, and potentially enables real-time control in large-scale quantum dot arrays.

</details>


### [125] [Giant mode splitting of azimuthal spin waves in radial vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10340)
*Zhenyu Wang,Liangrui Li,Xuejuan Liu,Xiansi Wang,Ruifang Wang,H. Y. Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了径向涡旋（radial vortex）中由界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）驱动的方位角自旋波模式分裂，其幅度可达传统涡核诱导分裂的一个数量级以上，为手性磁织构中的模式分裂提供了全新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统圆形涡旋中方位角自旋波的双重分裂源于涡核（vortex core, VC）与自旋波的耦合，且仅存在于m=±1的低阶模式。研究者希望探索径向涡旋中是否以及如何实现更显著、更普遍的模式分裂现象。

Method: 以铁磁圆盘中径向涡旋为研究对象，系统分析界面DMI对方位角自旋波模式分裂的影响，对比DMI诱导的分裂与传统涡核诱导的分裂，并考察分裂幅度随DMI常数和模式指数的变化关系。

Result: 径向涡旋中即使没有涡核也能产生巨大的方位角模式分裂，该分裂源于DMI的作用，幅度可比涡核诱导的分裂大一个数量级；频率分裂随DMI常数和模式指数的增大而增大，高阶方位角模式可达数十GHz。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了手性磁织构中模式分裂的稳健机制，深化了对受限磁性体中DMI对自旋波动力学影响的基础理解。

Abstract: Radial vortex is a topological spin texture stabilized by the interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) in ferromagnetic disks. Previous investigations have shown that the doublet splitting of azimuthal modes in traditional circular vortices arises from the coupling between azimuthal spin waves and vortex core (VC), an effect occurs only for azimuthal indices $m=\pm1$ and is absent for higher-order modes. Here, we present a giant mode splitting of azimuthal spin waves in radial vortices, even in the absence of the VC. This mode splitting arises from the DMI, which can be an order of magnitude larger than that induced by the VC. Moreover, the DMI-induced frequency splitting increases with both the DMI constant and mode index, reaching tens of GHz for higher-order azimuthal modes. Our results reveal a robust mechanism for mode splitting in chiral magnetic textures and deepen the fundamental understanding of the DMI effect on the spin-wave dynamics in confined magnets.

</details>


### [126] [Moving Analogue Horizons in Stationary Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10371)
*David Galvez-Poblete,Rubén M. Otxoa,Alvaro S. Nunez,Sebastian Allende*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明铁电材料（ferroelectric material）中极化梯度刚度的行波调制会在集体极化动力学中诱发有效流动，小极化涨落（ferron）服从含流的巨质量Klein-Gordon方程，从而为实现具有巨质量标量激发的模拟引力（analogue gravity）平台提供了实验可控的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模拟引力平台依赖介质的物理输运来产生有效流，而本文旨在展示一种无需介质宏观运动、仅通过材料参数调制即可产生有效流的新机制，为模拟引力研究提供更灵活可控的实验载体。

Method: 作者在受控的局域近似（local approximation）下推导出小极化涨落（ferron）的动力学方程，表明其满足含流的巨质量Klein-Gordon方程（massive Klein-Gordon equation with flow），并分析由材料参数调制所诱发的有效流如何形成移动的模拟视界（analogue horizon），进而考察负模反铁子（antiferron）与正模铁子（ferron）之间的耦合所导致的超辐射式放大（superradiant-like amplification）。

Result: 该机制能够在铁电材料中形成移动的模拟视界，将区域划分为亚铁子（sub-ferronic）与超铁子（super-ferronic）两个区域，后者支持具有负范数的反铁子模式；正负模耦合产生超辐射式放大效应，且有效质量间隙可通过外加电场独立调节。

Conclusion: 铁电系统为具有巨质量标量激发（massive scalar excitations）的模拟引力提供了一个新颖且实验可控的平台，其有效流动来源于材料参数的空间调制而非介质的物理输运。

Abstract: We show that a traveling modulation of the polarization-gradient stiffness in a ferroelectric material induces an effective flow in its collective polarization dynamics. Within a controlled local approximation, small polarization fluctuations, or ferrons, obey a massive Klein-Gordon equation with flow. Unlike conventional analogue-gravity platforms, the effective flow originates from the modulation of the material parameters rather than from the physical transport of the medium. This mechanism enables mobile analogue horizons separating sub-ferronic and super-ferronic regions, the latter supporting negative-norm antiferron modes. Their coupling to positive-norm ferrons gives rise to superradiant-like amplification, while the effective mass gap can be tuned independently through an external electric field. Ferroelectric systems therefore provide a novel and experimentally controllable platform for analogue gravity with massive scalar excitations.

</details>


### [127] [Dynamics of amorphous membranes in the two-dimensional limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10486)
*Liga Jasulaneca,Alberto Martín-Pérez,Hongji Zhang,Prertahn Munireternam,Natalia A. Mamchik,Chee-Tat Toh,Artem K. Grebenko,Barbaros Özyilmaz,Makars Šiškins,Farbod Alijani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究首次在单层非晶碳（monolayer amorphous carbon, MAC）悬浮薄膜中实现了光热驱动和干涉测量，在纳米鼓中观测到热机械运动、驱动共振和多模谱，并揭示了由低预张力引发的强非线性动力学行为，确立了MAC作为无序控制纳米力学平台的地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄机械谐振器此前主要在晶体二维材料（如石墨烯）中实现，其弹性性质和非线性共振行为由长程晶体序决定。将这一概念拓展到非晶二维极限在实验上仍是空白，该研究旨在探索非晶二维材料中的纳米力学行为。

Method: 制备单层非晶碳（MAC）悬浮膜，利用光热驱动（optothermal actuation）机制激发共振运动，并通过灵敏的干涉测量（interferometric readout）系统在线性和非线性谐振区间进行信号读取，同时解析热机械运动、驱动共振及多模谱。

Result: MAC纳米鼓的基模频率对应异常低的预张力，使其在较低驱动功率下即进入几何非线性、应力不均匀性（stress heterogeneity）和模式耦合占主导的区间。实验观测到显著的硬化、软化和混合Duffing响应、非线性阻尼、参数激励模式及模态间耦合特征。

Conclusion: 研究建立了MAC作为稳健的纳米机电（nanoelectromechanical）平台，开辟了二维非晶极限下由无序支配的纳米力学（disorder-governed nanomechanics）实验研究途径。

Abstract: Atomically thin mechanical resonators have been realized predominantly in crystalline two-dimensional (2D) materials, such as graphene, where long-range crystalline order sets their elastic properties and defines their nonlinear resonant behavior. Extending these concepts to the amorphous 2D limit has remained largely unexplored. Here, we demonstrate that monolayer amorphous carbon (MAC) forms suspended membranes that support optothermal actuation and sensitive interferometric readout across both linear and nonlinear regimes of its resonant motion. We resolve thermomechanical motion, driven resonances, and multimode spectra in MAC nanodrums. The frequencies of fundamental vibration modes correspond to unusually low pretensions, placing monolayer MAC nanodrums in a regime where geometric nonlinearities, stress heterogeneity, and mode coupling emerge at comparatively low drive powers. Consistently, we observe pronounced nonlinear dynamics, including hardening, softening, and mixed Duffing responses, nonlinear damping, parametrically excited modes, and signatures of intermodal coupling. These results establish MAC as a robust nanoelectromechanical platform and open an experimental route to disorder-governed nanomechanics in the 2D amorphous limit.

</details>


### [128] [Higher-Order Topological States with Cleavage-Dependent Dirac Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10520)
*Hongyu Chen,Min Long,Chuang Chen,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过切割受阻原子绝缘体（obstructed atomic insulator）发现了一类高阶拓扑绝缘体（higher-order topological insulators, HOTIs）中的拓扑相，其关键特征是e/2分数电荷（fractional charge）局域于恰好一半的角上，并通过C2旋转对称性实现空间平衡。研究发现角上的零模出现要求特定悬挂键（dangling bonds）在角落区域显式暴露并朝角落倾斜，且拓扑角的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）低于体态，与实空间能量分布行为相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于局域电荷轮廓的拓扑量子化学（topological quantum chemistry）方法无法预测高阶拓扑绝缘体的晶体解理行为，因此需要探索新的拓扑相表征方式来理解HOTI中拓扑角的物理起源和切割规律。

Method: 通过对受阻原子绝缘体进行晶体切割（cleaving）来诱导拓扑相变，分析e/2分数电荷在角落的分布模式、悬挂键的方向性约束，并在(2+1)维参数空间中追踪质量项（mass term）的各向异性演化，同时计算拓扑角与体态的纠缠熵。

Result: 发现一半角落携带e/2分数电荷，另一半角落携带间隙电荷（interstice charge）的互补空位，两者由C2旋转对称性分别约束形成空间平衡几何。角零模的出现要求特定悬挂键作为狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermion）的质量项显式暴露且朝角落倾斜，这一严格方向性在(2+1)维参数空间中得到验证。拓扑角的纠缠熵低于体态，与实空间能量分布梯度方向相反。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了HOTI中拓扑角的晶体切割机制受悬挂键方向性约束，并提出纠缠熵可作为区分拓扑角与体态的新的判据（反之于能量分布），为超越局域电荷轮廓的拓扑相预测提供了新视角。

Abstract: Topological quantum chemistry based on local charge profiles lacks predictive power for the crystalline cleavage of higher-order topological insulators (HOTIs). By cleaving an obstructed atomic insulator, we discover a topological phase characterized by e/2 fractional charges localized at precisely half of the corners, while the remaining empty corners host complementary vacancies of interstice charge. These zero-energy charge-vacancies and topological corners form a spatially balanced geometry, confined separately by C2 rotation symmetry. Crucially, we demonstrate that the emergence of corner zero modes dictates that specific dangling bonds-acting as the mass of a Dirac fermion-must explicitly expose in, and subtly slope toward, the corner regions. This strict directionality is verified by the anisotropic evolution of the mass term within a (2+1)-dimensional parameter space. Moreover, we find that the topological corners acquire lower entanglement entropy compared to the bulk, a behavior opposite to that of the real-space energy distribution.

</details>


### [129] [Topological states of generalized dissipative Majorana wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10556)
*Farokhnaz Hosseinifar,Ali G. Moghaddam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维广义量子耗散模型（对应可在一端拥有多个马约拉纳束缚态的Majorana线），利用伴随Lindblad方程分析费米子两点关联，并基于对称性分类定义了拓扑不变量——绕数（winding number），用于区分不同稳态。通过引入次近邻量子跳变项可获得更高绕数和更多马约拉纳束缚态，同时通过非平衡耦合可在正负绕数状态间切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于理解开放量子系统中拓扑相的性质，特别是当一维Majorana线的一端可以承载多个马约拉纳束缚态（Majorana bound states）时，如何系统地描述其耗散动力学和拓扑分类。

Method: 方法基于二次Lindblad方程（quadratic Lindblad equation）描述的一维费米子开放量子系统，利用伴随Lindblad方程（adjoint Lindblad equation）求解费米子两点关联函数，从而得到阻尼谱（damping spectrum）和纯度谱（purity spectrum）的能隙。随后采用基于对称性的分类，定义绕数作为拓扑不变量，并针对不同Lindblad量子跳变项的模型计算能隙和绕数以绘制相图。

Result: 结果表明，在阻尼能隙和纯度能隙存在时，绕数可区分系统不同稳态。通过加入次近邻（next-nearest-neighbor）位点间的量子跳变项，可实现更高的绕数值，等价于产生更多马约拉纳束缚态；引入非平衡耦合（imbalanced couplings）则可在正负绕数状态之间进行切换。

Conclusion: 由于该理论框架基于费米子关联而非马约拉纳算符（Majorana operators），其可方便地推广到其他对称性类（symmetry classes）的耗散拓扑相（dissipative topological phases）研究。

Abstract: We study the generalized one-dimensional (1D) quantum dissipative models corresponding to a Majorana wire which can possess more than one Majorana bound state at each end. The system consists of a 1D fermionic open quantum system whose dynamics is governed by a quadratic Lindblad equation. Using the adjoint Lindblad equation for the fermionic two-point correlations, we find the gaps in the damping and purity spectra of a generic 1D model. Then, using the symmetry-based classification, we show that a winding number as the topological invariant can be defined which distinguishes different steady states of the system in the presence of damping and purity gaps. Then we focus on certain models with different Lindblad quantum jump terms and explore their phase diagrams by calculating the damping and the purity gaps as well as the winding number. In particular, we show that by inclusion of quantum jumps between next-nearest-neighbor sites, higher winding numbers and, equivalently, more Majorana bound states can be achieved. Also, by introducing imbalanced couplings, we can switch between states with negative and positive winding numbers. Finally, we should mention that since our formulation is based on the fermionic correlations rather than the Majorana operators, it can be easily extended to the dissipative topological phases belonging to other symmetry classes.

</details>


### [130] [Geometric Phonon Energy Pumping in a Layer-Hybridized Moiré Exciton Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10631)
*Bitap Raj Thakuria,Himangshu Prabal Goswami*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个以实验为锚定的四态开放量子系统模型，用于研究WSe2/WS2莫尔激子系统中慢循环调制产生的几何贡献对能量转移统计量的影响，并通过反转回路方向分离几何贡献与动态背景。研究发现缩短循环周期并采用非均匀遍历驱动回路可显著提升几何响应的可探测性，结合增强的声子弛豫和降低的辐射损耗，固定时间信噪比相比均匀回路提升约14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的慢循环调制可以产生可分离于动态背景的能量转移统计量几何贡献（geometric contribution），但如何在实际系统（如门可调的莫尔激子体系）中利用这一效应并提升其可探测性尚不清楚。作者旨在构建符合实验条件的模型，系统评估该几何响应在不同条件下的稳健性，并寻求有效提升其可探测性的控制策略。

Method: 在种群水平全计数统计（population level full counting statistics）框架下，构建一个以实验数据为锚定的四态开放系统模型，用于描述门可调WSe2/WS2莫尔激子流形。首先沿闭合的门控与泵浦回路对系统进行均匀驱动，并通过反转回路方向将几何贡献从动态背景中分离出来；随后系统考察该响应随环境速率、激子耦合、控制条件及静态谱展宽的变化，并测试非均匀遍历与频率调制驱动等策略对探测性的影响。

Result: 较短的循环周期配合对驱动回路的非均匀遍历可显著提升几何响应的可探测性，同时保持几何贡献不变。将这一策略与增强的声子弛豫（phonon relaxation）以及降低的辐射损耗（radiative loss）相结合，固定时间信噪比相对均匀回路获得约14倍的增益。此外，几何声子响应在系统和环境的真实参数变化下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 几何声子响应（geometric phonon response）对系统及其环境的现实变化具有鲁棒性；相比进一步放大几何信号本身，通过频率调制驱动抑制动态噪声是提升该响应可探测性的更有效途径。

Abstract: Slow cyclic modulation of an open quantum system can generate a geometric contribution to its energy transfer statistics, separable from the dynamic background. In this work we construct an experimentally anchored four state open system model for a gate-tunable WSe2/WS2 moiré exciton manifold within a population level full counting statistics framework. We first drive the system uniformly through a closed gate and pump loop and separate the geometric contribution from the dynamic background by reversing the loop direction. We then examine how this response changes with environmental rates, exciton coupling, control conditions, and static spectral broadening. A shorter cycle period followed by nonuniform traversal of the driving loop substantially improves detectability while preserving the geometric response. Combined with enhanced phonon relaxation and reduced radiative loss, this gives about a fourteenfold fixed time signal to noise gain relative to the uniform loop. Our results show that the geometric phonon response remains robust against realistic variations in the system and its environment, and that we can improve its detectability more effectively by suppressing dynamical noise through frequency modulated driving.

</details>


### [131] [From Zero to Mega-Gauss Fields: Comprehensive Magnetophotonic Spectroscopy of Graphene Dirac Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10638)
*Shojiro Takeyama,Hiroaki Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用单匝线圈与电磁通量压缩技术在高达560 T的超强磁场下对4H-SiC上n型外延石墨烯的红外磁光响应进行了测量，发现吸收谱异常宽展，偏离常规回旋共振。通过广义双层石墨烯模型提取能带参数并构建朗道能级（Landau level）扇形图，揭示了N=0⁺与N=0⁻态在约160–200 T处的临界能级交叉，并利用集体阿尔文波（Alfvén wave）模型在极端磁场下完美复现了吸收谱，证明了二维石墨烯可作为天体物理极端相对论电子-正电子等离子体的桌面模拟系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索超强磁场（高达560 T）下二维石墨烯系统的极端磁光响应行为，这些行为挑战了传统回旋共振的预期，可能揭示石墨烯中电子-空穴集体激发的全新物理机制，并为模拟天体物理中相对论电子-正电子等离子体提供可行平台。

Method: 采用单匝线圈（single-turn coil）与电磁通量压缩（electromagnetic flux compression）两种极端磁场技术对n型掺杂外延石墨烯进行红外磁光吸收测量；结合角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）表征畸变的狄拉克色散及其“驼峰”结构；利用广义双层石墨烯模型提取能带参数，构建朗道能级扇形图，并使用集体阿尔文波模型解析极端磁场下的吸收谱。

Result: 测量发现吸收谱异常宽展，ARPES揭示出能隙约0.2 eV的“驼峰”状畸变狄拉克色散；朗道能级扇形图显示N=0⁺与N=0⁻态在160–200 T附近发生临界能级交叉，光学跃迁机制从电子主导的集体模式转变为电子-空穴协同集体激发；在μ激光激发下，约400 T处的主共振及200 T处的肩峰被阿尔文波模型精确复现，同时观察到亚晶格势不对称参数从0.10 eV增强至0.12 eV，表明磁场进一步放大了宏观电子-空穴能带不对称性。

Conclusion: 磁场诱导的能级反转产生了强相互作用的完全补偿电子-空穴等离子体，在此区域内阿尔文波的共振激发表明，一种与天体物理极端条件下相对论性电子-正电子等离子体对应的、清洁的桌面模拟可在二维石墨烯系统中优雅实现。

Abstract: We investigate the infrared magneto-optical response of n-doped epitaxial graphene on 4H-SiC in ultrahigh magnetic fields up to 560~T, utilizing single-turn coil and electromagnetic flux compression techniques. The measured absorption spectra are anomalously broad, strongly deviating from conventional cyclotron resonance. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) reveals a distorted Dirac dispersion featuring a ``camel-back" structure with an energy gap of $E_g \sim 0.2$~eV. Using the band parameters extracted via a generalized bilayer graphene model, we construct a Landau level (LL) fan chart that dictates a critical level crossing between the $N=0^+$ and $N=0^-$ states near 160--200~T. At this threshold, the optical transition mechanism undergoes a dramatic shift from an electron-dominated collective mode to a cooperative electron-hole collective excitation. Furthermore, the extreme-field absorption spectra under thulium fiber laser excitation ($\hbarω_0 = 0.636$~eV)---culminating in a massive resonance near 400~T with a shoulder at 200~T---are excellently reproduced by a collective Alfvén wave model. This analysis also evidences a magnetic-field-induced enhancement of the sublattice potential asymmetry parameter (from 0.10~eV to 0.12~eV). This directly signifies that the macroscopic electron-hole band asymmetry is further amplified by the applied magnetic field. Ultimately, the field-induced energy inversion generates a strongly interacting, fully compensated electron-hole plasma. The resonant excitation of Alfvén waves in this regime demonstrates that a pristine tabletop analog to the relativistic electron-positron plasmas found in astrophysical extremes can be elegantly realized within a 2D graphene system.

</details>


### [132] [Revealing time characteristics of optical excitations in dielectric and plasmonic structures through cathodoluminescence interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10721)
*Evelijn Akerboom,Hirohsi Sugimoto,Minoru Fujii,Nicolas Pazos-Perez,Ramon A. Álvarez Puebla,A. Femius Koenderink,F. Javier García de Abajo,Albert Polman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了阴‑极荧光干涉测量法（CL interferometry）能够在无需超快泵浦-探测方案的情况下，获取纳米尺度共振散射体的时间响应、相位行为和模态谱结构信息，并在金纳米颗粒、宽带等离激元发射体、金纳米星及硅纳米球等体系中验证了理论预测，测得1–10飞秒（fs）级别的衰减时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阴‑极荧光光谱法（cathodoluminescence spectroscopy）能以纳米级空间分辨率访问光学激发，但直接对光学共振进行时间分辨测量仍然具有挑战性，需要发展无需复杂超快泵浦-探测装置的新方法。

Method: 建立了一个解析框架，通过对角度分辨和频率分辨的CL干涉图进行傅里叶变换（Fourier transformation），获得由线性光学响应（linear optical response）决定的共振衰减时间。利用邻近金属表面发射的渡越辐射（transition radiation）作为宽带参考，实现相位恢复以及瞬时与共振发射过程之间的交叉关联测量。

Result: 多模谐振腔展现出与光谱模式劈裂（spectral mode splitting）相关的特征性时间域CL拍频信号。在金纳米颗粒、宽带等离激元发射体、金纳米星以及支持多极米氏共振（Mie resonances）的硅纳米球上的实验测量均证实了理论预测，每个体系获得了1–10 fs范围内的衰减时间。

Conclusion: CL干涉测量法被确立为一种强大的方法，可在单一纳米尺度测量中同时获取光谱、空间和相位信息，且具备飞秒级分辨率。

Abstract: Cathodoluminescence (CL) spectroscopy provides access to optical excitations with nanometer spatial resolution, but direct time-resolved measurements of optical resonances remain challenging. Here, we demonstrate that CL interferometry provides access to the temporal response, phase behavior, and modal spectral structure of resonant nanoscale scatterers without requiring ultrafast pump-probe schemes. We develop an analytical framework in which Fourier transformation angle- and frequency-resolved CL interferograms yields the decay time of optical resonances governed by the linear optical response. Multimode resonators exhibit characteristic temporal CL beating signatures associated with spectral mode splitting. By exploiting transition radiation emitted from a nearby metallic surface as a broadband reference, we further demonstrate phase retrieval and cross-correlation measurements between instantaneous and resonant emission processes. Experimental measurements on Au nanoparticles, broadband plasmonic emitters, Au nanostars, and Si nanospheres supporting multipolar Mie resonances confirm the theoretical predictions, and decay times in the range 1-10 fs are derived for each system. Our results establish CL interferometry as a powerful approach for accessing spectral, spatial, and phase information within a single nanoscale measurement with fs resolution.

</details>


### [133] [Anisotropic magnon spin transport in CrPS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10802)
*Krishnaraajan Sundararajan,Muhammad Zohaib,Yulia Kreminska,Sytze H. Tirion,Bart J. van Wees*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在非局域几何结构中，利用单斜范德华反铁磁体CrPS₄，观察到电激发和热激发磁振子自旋输运均呈现显著的晶格各向异性：沿晶体b轴输运时，磁振子自旋电导率至少为a轴的2.2倍，自旋扩散长度至少为a轴的2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管晶体各向异性对电子输运的影响已被充分研究，但各向异性在范德华磁体磁振子自旋输运中的作用在很大程度上尚未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究晶格各向异性是否可作为调控磁振子输运的有效参数。

Method: 作者在非局域几何结构中，利用单斜范德华反铁磁体CrPS₄，分别测量了电激发和热激发（非局域二次谐波电阻）的磁振子自旋输运，并沿晶体a轴和b轴方向进行对比实验，同时辅以8T磁场和25K温度的特定条件。

Result: 电激发磁振子沿b轴的磁振子自旋电导率至少为a轴的2.2倍，自旋扩散长度至少为a轴的2.7倍（λ_m^a ~ 211 nm，λ_m^b ≥ 575 nm）。在8T、25K条件下，热激发磁振子对应的非局域二次谐波电阻沿b轴约为a轴的7倍。此外，作者发现仅凭非局域二次谐波电阻无法可靠提取磁振子自旋扩散长度，也无法可靠估计自旋Seebeck系数的各向异性。

Conclusion: 电激发磁振子自旋输运表明，固有晶体各向异性（crystal anisotropy）可作为有效控制参数来调控磁振子自旋输运，为磁振子器件工程开辟了新途径。热激发路径则受延展的温度分布和各向异性热导率的干扰，不适合定量提取输运参数。

Abstract: Crystal anisotropy provides a powerful route for realizing direction-dependent transport in solid-state systems. While its influence on electronic transport is well established, the role of anisotropy in magnon spin transport in van der Waals magnets is largely unexplored. Here, in a nonlocal geometry, utilizing the monoclinic van der Waals antiferromagnet CrPS$_4$, we observe pronounced anisotropy in both electrically and in thermally excited magnon spin transport. Electrically generated magnons exhibit a magnon spin conductivity at least 2.2 times larger and a spin diffusion length at least 2.7 times longer for transport along the crystallographic-b axis compared to the crystallographic-a axis, where $λ_m^{a} \sim$ 211 nm and $λ_m^{b} \geq$ 575 nm. In comparison, at 8T, we find the nonlocal second-harmonic resistance associated with thermally excited magnons to be $\sim$7 times larger along the crystallographic-b axis at 25K. We further show that a magnon spin diffusion length cannot be reliably extracted from the nonlocal second-harmonic resistance, owing to the extended temperature profile within CrPS$_4$. Likewise, we show that the anisotropy in the spin Seebeck coefficients cannot be reliably estimated from the thermally excited magnon spin transport alone, as it is intertwined with the anisotropic heat conductivity of CrPS$_4$. Utilizing the electrically generated magnon spin transport, we demonstrate that intrinsic crystalline anisotropy serves as an effective control parameter for tuning magnon spin transport, opening new avenues for magnonic device engineering.

</details>


### [134] [Strong coupling between antiferromagnetic magnons and spoof surface plasmons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10822)
*Yamin Sun,Ka Shen,H. Y. Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在平面异质结构中研究了反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AFM）磁子与赝表面等离激元（spoof surface plasmons）的杂化，通过解析求解耦合的麦克斯韦方程与磁化动力学方程，预言了太赫兹频段的强磁子-等离激元耦合，表现为色散中的显著回避交叉（avoided crossings）。该系统可在强耦合区域工作，实现磁子与等离激元之间的相干信息传递，为片上操控与集成提供了平面平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于铁磁球和局域微波谐振器已观测到磁子与赝等离激元的强耦合，但平面磁结构中能否实现相干磁子-等离激元耦合仍属未探索领域，而平面结构更有利于芯片上的操控与集成。

Method: 研究结构为平面异质结，包含AFM薄膜、介电间隔层（dielectric spacer）和结构化金属表面。方法为解析求解耦合的麦克斯韦方程与磁化动力学方程，分析磁子与赝表面等离激元的杂化色散关系。

Result: 预言了太赫兹频段的强磁子-等离激元耦合，表现为色散中的显著回避交叉。耦合源于介电间隔层中磁子模与等离激元模的空间重叠，且可通过系统几何参数高效调节。计算的协同度（cooperativity）证实该系统可在强耦合区域运行，支持磁子与等离激元间的相干信息传递。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个用于磁子-等离激元杂化的平面平台，相比已有构型更易于片上操控与集成，为相干信息交换提供了新途径。

Abstract: Hybrid magnonic system provides a versatile platform for coherent information exchange between spin excitations and other physical degrees of freedom. While strong coupling between magnons and spoof plasmons has been observed based on ferrimagnetic spheres and localized microwave resonators, it remains unexplored whether coherent magnon-plasmon coupling can be achieved in planar magnetic structures. Here we study the hybridization of antiferromagnetic (AFM) magnons and spoof surface plasmons in a planar heterostructure consisting of an AFM thin film, a dielectric spacer, and a structured metal surface. By analytically solving the coupled Maxwell and magnetization dynamics equation, we predict strong magnon-plasmon coupling in the terahertz regime, manifested by pronounced avoided crossings in the dispersion. The coupling originates from the spatial overlap between magnonic and plasmonic modes in the dielectric spacer, and can be efficiently tuned through geometric parameters of the system. The calculated cooperativity confirms that the hybrid system can operate in the strong coupling regime, enabling coherent information transfer between magnons and plasmons. Our results establish a planar platform for plasmon-magnon hybridization, which is more amenable to on-chip manipulation and integration.

</details>


### [135] [Studying electron beam coherence using plasmon interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10842)
*Evelijn Akerboom,F. Javier García de Abajo,Albert Polman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明了阴极发光（cathodoluminescence, CL）相干性的反直觉行为：扩展电子波不同横向区域激发的CL信号互不相干，而窄电子束电磁场中不同结构激发的CL信号彼此相干，并提出了通过电子态后选择恢复相干性的测量几何方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能电子与介质及等离激元纳米结构相互作用产生的阴极发光角分布携带激发路径相干性的关键信息，但激发路径的相干性与电子波空间分布之间的关系尚存反直觉预测，需要实验验证。

Method: 实验采用欠焦电子束（defocused electron beam）激发薄氮化硅膜，测量其角分辨CL发射剖面；同时观察分散等离激元散射体在单电子倏逝场（evanescent field）激发下的CL干涉现象；并提出一种去除电子轨迹路径信息（which-path information）的测量几何用于恢复相干性。

Result: 实验证实薄膜CL发射剖面遵循电子束光斑内CL激发的不相干叠加；而分离的等离激元散射体被单电子倏逝场激发时观测到CL干涉效应，验证了相干性的空间非局域性质。

Conclusion: 电子激发CL的相干性取决于激发路径的空间关系而非电子束宽度本身，且可通过发射光与后选择电子态（post-selected electron states）之间的关联恢复相干性，为量子相干探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Energetic electrons create distinct cathodoluminescence (CL) angular distributions upon interaction with dielectric and plasmonic nanostructures, providing valuable information on coherences in the excitation pathways. A counterintuitive prediction is that the CL signals arising from the excitation associated with different lateral regions of an extended electron wave are mutually incoherent and do not interfere, while the signals originating from different structures within the electromagnetic field of a narrow electron beam are mutually coherent. We present conclusive experimental evidence of these effects by examining the angular CL emission profile from defocused electron-beam excitation of a thin silicon nitride film, which is shown to follow an incoherent sum of CL excitations within the electron beam spot. In contrast, CL interferences are observed for separated plasmonic scatterers excited within the evanescent field of a single electron. Coherence may be recovered through correlations between emitted light and post-selected electron states, for which we propose a measurement geometry that erases which-path information of the electron trajectory.

</details>


### [136] [Assessing fidelity-limiting factors and achieving single-qubit gate fidelity beyond 99.999% in driven silicon spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11072)
*Kenta Takeda,Akito Noiri,Takashi Nakajima,Leon C. Camenzind,Takashi Kobayashi,Giordano Scappucci,Seigo Tarucha*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了半导体单自旋量子比特中单量子比特门保真度超过99.999%的实验成果，通过大幅延长驱动自旋相干时间（driven-spin coherence时间，即自旋锁定相干时间T₁ρ）并抑制离共振驱动效应实现，同时揭示了奇偶读出和矩形脉冲下邻近量子比特离共振激发对保真度标定造成的显著伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管半导体单自旋量子比特已实现高保真量子门，但由于驱动量子比特相干性的变化（相比自由演化相干性如T₂*研究较少），一致的性能表现仍具挑战性。作者旨在探究限制高保真度标定的机制并提供实用指南。

Method: 通过移除邻近储库（proximal reservoirs）以显著增强自旋锁定相干时间T₁ρ；优化器件条件以缓解微波诱导退化；采用光谱定制（spectrally tailored）的脉冲整形方案；结合奇偶读出（parity readout）与矩形脉冲设置进行系统的保真度标定伪影分析。

Result: 实现了π/2门保真度达到99.99920(2)%，剩余误差主要受非相干噪声限制。去除邻近储库显著增强T₁ρ，并揭示了典型自旋量子比特设置中奇偶读出和矩形脉冲导致的离共振激发会造成实质性标定伪影。

Conclusion: 研究展示了限制最先进硅自旋量子比特保真度标定的机制，并为在这些系统中实现和验证高保真度提供了实用指南。

Abstract: In semiconductor single-spin qubits, high-fidelity quantum gates have been demonstrated; however, achieving consistent performance remains challenging due to variations in driven qubit coherence, which is less explored than free-evolution coherence such as $T_2^*$. Here, we report single-qubit gate fidelities above 99.999%, achieved by dramatically extending the driven-spin coherence time and suppressing off-resonant driving effects that are detrimental to accurate fidelity benchmarking. We demonstrate that removing proximal reservoirs significantly enhances the spin-locking coherence time ($T_{1ρ}$), a critical metric for qubits under microwave driving. Furthermore, we reveal that in typical spin qubit setups using parity readout and rectangular pulses, off-resonant excitation of neighboring qubits causes substantial benchmarking artifacts. By optimizing device conditions to mitigate microwave-induced degradation and implementing spectrally tailored pulse shaping, we achieve a $π/2$ gate fidelity of 99.99920(2)%, with remaining errors primarily limited by incoherent noise. These results showcase the mechanisms that bound fidelity benchmarking in state-of-the-art silicon spin qubits and provide practical guidelines for achieving and verifying high fidelities in these systems.

</details>


### [137] [Inferring stealthy hyperuniform correlations from quantum transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11188)
*Natanael C. Costa,Mauro S. Ferreira,Caio Lewenkopf,Felipe A. Pinheiro,Paul J. Steinhardt,Salvatore Torquato,Carlo Vanoni*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于电导（conductance）的逆散射协议，通过一维紧束缚链中无自旋费米子的透射谱来恢复隐匿超均匀（stealthy hyperuniform）无序系统的关联参数χ。数值实验表明透射谱的陡降位置由χ主导，失配函数在目标χ附近呈现清晰最小值，证明透射谱可作为隐匿超均匀无序的"指纹"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐匿超均匀无序体系具有抑制长波长涨落的特点，其核心特征参数χ控制了无序谱中排除的傅里叶分量范围；然而在现实场景中微观无序构型难以直接观测，因此需要从可测量的输运数据中间接推断χ。

Method: 以无自旋费米子在一维紧束缚（tight-binding）链中为模型，施加具有隐匿谱S(k)=Θ(|k|-K)（其中K=2πχ）的格点无序，链条两端连接干净半无限导线；使用递归格林函数（recursive Green's function）方法计算能量依赖的透射率，并通过定义在能量窗口上的失配函数（misfit function）将其与目标谱进行比较。

Result: 高、低透射区之间陡降位置对χ有强敏感性，而无序强度W主要影响透射率的绝对大小；失配函数在目标隐匿参数附近呈现清晰的最小值，表明该方法可准确恢复χ。

Conclusion: 透射谱可作为隐匿超均匀无序的指纹，为从输运测量中推断相关无序参数提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Stealthy hyperuniform disordered systems exhibit strongly suppressed long-wavelength fluctuations, producing correlated disorder with unusual consequences for wave propagation. A central quantity characterizing these systems is the stealthiness parameter $χ$, which controls the range of excluded Fourier components in the disorder spectrum. However, in realistic settings, the microscopic disorder configuration may not be directly accessible, making it challenging to determine $χ$ from structural information alone. Here, we propose a conductance-based inverse protocol to recover stealthy hyperuniform correlations from transport data. As a proof of concept, we study spinless fermions in a one-dimensional tight-binding chain connected to clean semi-infinite leads, with on-site disorder generated by imposing a stealthy spectrum $S(k)=Θ(|k|-K)$, where $K=2πχ$. The energy-dependent transmittance is computed using a recursive Green's function method and compared with target spectra through a misfit function defined over an energy window. We show that the position of the sharp drop separating high- and low-transmittance regions is strongly controlled by $χ$, while the disorder strength $W$ mainly affects the absolute magnitude of the transmittance. As a result, the misfit function displays a clear minimum close to the target stealthy parameter. Our results demonstrate that transmittance spectra can serve as fingerprints of stealthy hyperuniform disorder, providing a practical route to infer correlated-disorder parameters from transport measurements.

</details>


### [138] [Floquet Engineering of Topological Phases and Magneto-Optical Response in a Driven $d$-wave Altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11192)
*Muzamil Shah*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究线偏振光驱动的二维d波交错磁体（altermagnet）中拓扑态与磁光响应的调控机制，揭示了偏振依赖的Peierls相位破坏自旋扇区间的对称性，产生中间陈绝缘体相，并通过Kubo形式计算了朗道尔（Faraday）和克尔（Kerr）旋转角，提出线偏振光作为自旋分辨带反转和数字切换的对接触手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用线偏振光Floquet驱动控制二维d波交错磁体的拓扑性质与磁光响应，为交错磁体中自旋分辨拓扑态的光学调控提供新的物理机制。

Method: 采用Floquet驱动框架分析线偏振光对d波交错磁体的作用，通过偏振依赖的Peierls相位导致各向异性跳跃重整化，破坏C4zT对称性，并通过Kubo形式计算频率相关的纵向和霍尔电导，进而推导自由孤立导电薄层的Faraday和Kerr旋转。

Result: 研究发现无光场时系统具有自旋陈数（spin-Chern）相位；光场破坏对称性后产生C=±1的中间陈绝缘体相；驱动幅度A0决定反转阈值，偏振旋转π/2会交换自旋扇区并使陈数变号；纵向响应反映Floquet重整化的带间阈值，而霍尔响应和磁光旋转符号可区分两种相反的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）手性；显著的Kerr角仅出现在窄共振窗口内。

Conclusion: 线偏振光可作为对称性选择工具，实现对d波交错磁体中自旋分辨带反转、陈数切换及非接触式光学探测的调控，为相关拓扑光电器件设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study how Floquet driving with linearly polarized light controls the topology and magneto-optical response of a two-dimensional (2D) $d$-wave altermagnet. In the absence of linearly polarized optical field and under spin conservation, we find that the system hosts a spin-Chern (a quantum-spin-Hall analog) phase with Chern numbers of opposite sign in the two spin sectors. The irradiated optical field breaks the $C_{4z}\mathcal{T}$ crystalline antiunitary symmetry between the spin sectors. Symmetry breaking originates from polarization-dependent Peierls phases, which renormalize hopping anisotropically along the two axes. The resulting spin-selective gap closures produce intermediate Chern-insulating phases with $C=\pm1$. The drive amplitude $A_0$ determines the inversion thresholds, while rotating the polarization by $π/2$ swaps the spin sectors and reverses the Chern number. Using the Kubo formalism, we compute the frequency-dependent longitudinal and Hall conductivities and derive the corresponding Faraday and Kerr rotations for a free-standing conducting sheet. The longitudinal response tracks the Floquet-renormalized interband thresholds, whereas the optical Hall response, together with the sign of the magneto-optical rotations, distinguishes the two opposite Berry-curvature chiralities. Sizable Kerr angles occur only within narrow resonant windows and should be interpreted together with the reflected intensity and Kerr ellipticity. These results identify linearly polarized light as a symmetry-selective handle for spin-resolved band inversion, Chern-number switching, and contact-free optical detection in $d$-wave altermagnets.

</details>

### [139] [Arithmetic selection rules in dispersionless Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11179)
*Fatma Aydogmus,Mustafa Mullahasanoglu*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文推导了任意幂次单项式电荷密度在刘维尔可积性条件下的选择规则，并将其与1+1维可积场论及组合多项式序列建立了对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索刘维尔可积（Liouville integrable）哈密顿系统与组合数学中多项式序列之间的深层联系，建立更一般的选择规则框架。

Method: 从刘维尔可积条件出发，推导任意幂次单项式电荷密度的选择规则；通过将上述规则转化为负Pell方程求其整数解，以此构造无穷多个相互对合（involution）的运动积分；随后将组合多项式序列（如Motzkin序列、二项式序列）与1+1维可积场论建立对应。

Result: Motzkin系统与Levi系统的无色散极限重合；二项式系统等价于无色散导数非线性薛定谔方程（derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation）；二项式哈密顿模型可约化为无黏伯格斯方程，其高阶电荷生成广义伯格斯型守恒律。

Conclusion: 刘维尔可积性的选择规则与Pell方程解之间存在系统联系，且组合多项式序列为构建可积场论提供了自然框架。

Abstract: In this work, we derive selection rules imposed by Liouville integrability conditions for monomial charge densities with arbitrary powers. For a certain monomial Hamiltonian system, the selection rules reduce to a negative Pell equation, and its solutions generate an infinite set of integrals of motion that are mutually in involution.
  Furthermore, we study the correspondence between combinatorial polynomial sequences and Liouville integrable Hamiltonian field theories in 1+1 dimensions. We show that the Motzkin system coincides with the dispersionless limit of the Levi system, while the binomial system is equivalent to the dispersionless derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation. Additionally, we show that the binomial Hamiltonian model admits a reduction to the inviscid Burgers equation and its higher-order charges generate generalized Burgers-type conservation laws.

</details>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Asymptotic stability of the degree-one vortex in the abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model: Spectral Theory and Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10608)
*Jonas Luhrmann,José M. Palacios,Fabio Pusateri,Wilhelm Schlag,Sohrab Shahshahani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是三篇系列论文中的第一篇，在自对偶耦合的$(1+2)$维阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯模型中，证明了度一涡旋在等变微扰下的渐近稳定性。通过将线性化动力学约化为强奇异径向薛定谔算子谱问题的分析，结合严格区间算术，证明了不存在阈值共振、离散谱仅含一个正间隙特征值（内模），并以有效非线性阻尼形式确认了非线性Fermi Golden Rule系数构成正定二次型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于证明自对偶耦合条件下$(1+2)$维阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯（Yang-Mills-Higgs）模型中度一涡旋（degree-one vortex）在等变微扰（equivariant perturbations）下的渐近稳定性（asymptotic stability），这是理解涡旋长期动力学行为的关键步骤。

Method: 在正交规范（orthogonal gauge）下，线性化动力学由自伴矩阵薛定谔算子$\mathbf{M}$描述；其超对称伙伴算子为对角矩阵，对角线元为$\mathbb{R}^2$上强奇异径向薛定谔算子。经共轭处理后，谱问题归结为两个强奇异标量半线算子的分析，并结合解析方法与严格区间算术（rigorous interval arithmetic）进行判定。

Result: 证明了不存在阈值共振（threshold resonances），并确立了算子$\mathbf{M}$的离散谱恰好包含一个正间隙特征值（internal mode），其对应二维特征空间；同时验证了相关非线性Fermi Golden Rule系数构成正定二次型（definite quadratic form），从而保证内模的有效非线性阻尼（effective nonlinear damping）。

Conclusion: 上述谱分析结果为后续两篇论文中的度一涡旋渐近稳定性证明提供了必要的基础框架，特别是内模的阻尼机制与谱性质为非线性稳定性分析铺平了道路。

Abstract: This is the first of three papers proving asymptotic stability of the degree-one vortex under equivariant perturbations in the $(1+2)$-dimensional abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model at self-dual coupling. In the orthogonal gauge, the linearized dynamics are governed by a selfadjoint matrix Schrödinger operator $\mathbf{M}$. The super-symmetric partner operator is a diagonal matrix whose diagonal entries are strongly singular radial Schrödinger operators on $\mathbb{R}^2$. After a conjugation, this reduces the spectral problem to the analysis of two strongly singular scalar half-line operators. Combining analysis with rigorous interval arithmetic, we prove absence of threshold resonances and show that the discrete spectrum of $\mathbf{M}$ consists of exactly one positive gap eigenvalue (internal mode) with a two-dimensional eigenspace. We also certify that the relevant nonlinear Fermi Golden Rule coefficients form a definite quadratic form, yielding effective nonlinear damping of the internal mode. These spectral inputs form the basis of the stability analysis in the subsequent papers.

</details>


### [141] [Asymptotic stability of the degree-one vortex in the abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model: Linear Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10609)
*Jonas Luhrmann,José M. Palacios,Fabio Pusateri,Wilhelm Schlag,Sohrab Shahshahani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是该系列论文三篇中的第二部分，研究阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯模型中自对偶耦合下度一涡旋附近的线性化动力学，通过构造畸变傅里叶变换（distorted Fourier transform）并利用超对称分解，证明了线性算子$\mathbf{M}$的色散估计、局部能量衰减估计及传递关系，为第三部分的空间共振方法奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系列论文旨在证明基态涡旋的渐近稳定性（asymptotic stability），本文需要为其第三部分提供线性估计工具。为此需对自对偶耦合下阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯模型的线性化算子$\mathbf{M}$建立严格的色散和能量衰减估计，这是后续非线性分析的前提。

Method: 方法的核心是构造与算子$\mathbf{M}$相关联的畸变傅里叶变换。论文利用$\mathbf{M}$的超对称分解（super-symmetric factorization）及其伴生算子的对角结构，将问题归结为两个强奇异标量半线薛定谔算子的Weyl-Titchmarsh理论，从而完成广义本征函数的详细分析和变换的构造。

Result: 论文证明了色散估计（dispersive estimates）和局部能量衰减估计（local-energy decay estimates），并建立了允许将非线性分析中空间共振方法（space-time resonance method）对平坦Klein-Gordon算子实施的传递关系（transference relation）。

Conclusion: 本文为第三部分非线性稳定性分析提供了所需的全部线性估计框架，通过畸变傅里叶变换技术建立了与平坦Klein-Gordon算子的对应关系，证明了该技术路线的可行性。

Abstract: We study the linearized dynamics near the degree-one vortex of the $(1+2)$-dimensional abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model at the self-dual coupling, restricted to equivariant perturbations in the orthogonal gauge. The linearized operator is a selfadjoint matrix Schrödinger operator $\mathbf{M}$ on radial $L^2_{\mathrm{rad}}(\mathbb{R}^2;\mathbb{R}^4)$ with continuous spectrum $[1,\infty)$ and a two-dimensional internal mode at a unique gap eigenvalue $λ^2 \in (0,1)$, as established in Part I of our three-paper series on asymptotic stability of the ground state vortex. In this second part of the series, we prove linear estimates for $\mathbf{M}$ for applications in Part III. Specifically, we prove dispersive and local-energy decay estimates, as well as a transference relation which allows us to implement the space-time resonance method with respect to the flat Klein-Gordon operator in the nonlinear analysis in Part III.
  The engine for proving linear estimates for $\mathbf{M}$ in our approach is the distorted Fourier transform associated with $\mathbf{M}$. The construction of the distorted Fourier transform together with a detailed analysis of the underlying generalized eigenfunctions occupy the first half of this paper. For this we exploit the super-symmetric factorization of $\mathbf{M}$, and the diagonal structure of the super-symmetric partner operator, to relate the problem to the Weyl-Titchmarsh theory of two strongly singular scalar half-line Schrödinger operators.

</details>


### [142] [Asymptotic stability of the degree-one vortex in the abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10610)
*Jonas Luhrmann,José M. Palacios,Fabio Pusateri,Wilhelm Schlag,Sohrab Shahshahani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在自对偶耦合下，（1+2）维阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯模型中一阶涡旋（degree-one vortex）在加权Sobolev空间中关于小等变扰动（equivariant perturbations）的渐近稳定性（asymptotic stability）。作为三篇论文系列的最终篇，主要难点在于耦合的辐射-内部模式（radiation–internal-mode）的长时行为分析，通过好-坏分解、平坦-尖锐分解及空间-时间共振（space-time resonance）分析等方法完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于证明经典相对论场论——阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯模型中的拓扑孤子涡旋在受扰动后能否渐近稳定。此前，涡旋的线性化算子的谱性质、扭曲傅里叶理论（distorted Fourier theory）及线性衰减估计已由两篇姊妹论文完成，本文作为系列最终篇，旨在处理动力学非线性部分的长期稳定性问题。

Method: 方法包括：在正交规范（orthogonal gauge）条件下将扰动方程化为非线性Klein-Gordon方程组（耦合一个关于电磁势时间分量的椭圆方程）；将好-坏分解（good-bad decomposition）与平坦-尖锐分解（flat-sharp decomposition）相结合，并相对平坦Klein-Gordon流进行空间-时间共振分析来证明辐射场的衰减；通过对线性化算子内部模（internal mode）的谱性质及扭曲傅里叶理论导出的ILED与迁移估计（transference estimates）实现从平坦分析到含势Klein-Gordon流的过渡。

Result: 本文成功证明了一阶涡旋在自对偶耦合下对加权Sobolev空间中小等变扰动的渐近稳定性。证明的关键进展在于：在二维空间中Klein-Gordon辐射的色散衰减相对较弱且辐射方程含有非空间局域的变系数二次相互作用的情况下，通过上述分解与共振分析技巧克服了长时分析的困难，并利用ILED和迁移估计弥合了平坦分析与含势流之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该论文完成了三篇系列中对（1+2）维阿贝尔杨-米尔斯-希格斯模型涡旋渐近稳定性的完整证明，为经典场论中拓扑孤子的非线性稳定性分析提供了新的技术框架，特别是在含内部模且辐射衰减较弱情形下的长时动力学分析。

Abstract: We prove asymptotic stability of the degree-one vortex in the $(1+2)$-dimensional abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model at the self-dual coupling, for small equivariant perturbations in weighted Sobolev spaces. The abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs model is a classical relativistic field theory on $(1+2)$-dimensional Minkowski space, describing a complex-valued field coupled to an electromagnetic potential and admitting topological solitons known as vortices.
  This paper is the final and main part of a three-paper series. Under the orthogonal gauge condition used here, perturbations of the vortex are governed by a system of nonlinear Klein-Gordon equations for the dynamical variables, coupled to an elliptic equation for the temporal component of the electromagnetic potential. The linearized operator has continuous spectrum $[1,\infty)$ and a single positive gap eigenvalue (internal mode) of multiplicity two, whose spectral properties, associated distorted Fourier theory, and linear decay estimates are developed in the two companion papers.
  The main difficulty is the long-time analysis of the coupled radiation--internal-mode dynamics. In two space dimensions the dispersive decay of the Klein-Gordon radiation is relatively weak, while the internal mode decays only on the long time scale dictated by nonlinear radiation damping. At the same time, the Klein-Gordon equations for the radiation contain non-spatially localized variable coefficient quadratic interactions, which cannot be treated perturbatively and require a normal form analysis. We prove decay of the radiation by combining a good-bad decomposition, a flat-sharp decomposition, and a space-time resonance analysis carried out relative to the flat Klein-Gordon flow. The passage between the flat analysis and the Klein-Gordon flow with potential is achieved through ILED and transference estimates derived from the distorted Fourier theory.

</details>

### [143] [Geometrize the nongeometric space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09958)
*Tran Chi Quy,Sonnet Nguyen Quang Hung*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在下降理论（descent theory）框架下提出了非几何R-空间（nongeometric $R$-space）的一套严格且系统的数学形式化定义。核心思想是将非几何R-空间视为在底流形（underlying manifold）的每个局部坐标域上仅携带拟三角拟Hopf代数（quasitriangular quasi-Hopf algebra）的局部数据，并通过黏合（gluing）构造全局几何对象，最终表明其恰当的全局结构是一个双范畴值叠（bicategory-valued stack），从而在保留局部非几何本质的同时给出全局几何定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非交换非结合几何在数学和物理学界受到广泛关注，其中弦论中的R-空间虽同时具有非交换与非结合结构并被认定为局部非几何，但以往研究主要通过局部弦位置坐标的代数关系来描述R-空间，缺乏精确的全局几何定义。本文旨在弥补这一空白，为R-空间提供一个严密而系统的数学全局表述。

Method: 本文在下降理论（descent theory）框架下展开，将非几何R-空间视为仅携带底流形（underlying manifold）各局部坐标域上拟三角拟Hopf代数（quasitriangular quasi-Hopf algebra）的局部数据，并利用相干黏合（coherent gluing）过程将这些局部数据组装为全局几何对象。文章论证了合适的全局结构应是双范畴值叠（bicategory-valued stack）而非普通空间或全局定义的代数。

Result: 研究表明，非几何R-空间的恰当全局结构既不是普通空间（ordinary space）也不是全局定义的代数（globally defined algebra），而是一个双范畴值叠（bicategory-valued stack）。这一构造为R-空间提供了全局几何定义，同时保持了其局部非几何（locally nongeometric）的内在特征。

Conclusion: 本文通过下降理论（descent theory）为弦论中非几何R-空间建立了严格且系统的数学表述，给出了其全局几何定义，并在保持局部非几何本性的同时实现了全局几何结构的构建。该结果弥合了此前仅停留在代数关系描述层面的研究不足，为非交换非结合几何提供了更坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: Noncommutative and nonassociative geometry has attracted much attention in both mathematics and physics communities. A prominent example is a stringy $R$-space over an underlying manifold, which exhibits both noncommutative and nonassociative structures and is known to be locally nongeometric. However, previous studies have mainly described $R$-space through algebraic relations of local string position coordinates, instead of a precise global geometric definition. In this paper, we propose a rigorous and systematic mathematical formulation of nongeometric $R$-space within the framework of descent theory. The central idea is to regard nongeometric $R$-space as carrying only local data of a quasitriangular quasi-Hopf algebra on each local coordinate domain of the underlying manifold, which are then glued coherently together to produce a global geometric object. We show that the appropriate global structure is not an ordinary space or a globally defined algebra, but a bicategory-valued stack. This provides a global and geometric definition of $R$-space while still preserving its locally nongeometric intrinsic character.

</details>


### [144] [3-dimensional TQFTs from derived categories of quantum group representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10285)
*Cris Negron*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明任何有限模张量范畴（modular tensor category）A 的导出∞-范畴 D(A) 都能支撑一个三维拓扑量子场论（TQFT），该理论以对称幺半函子的形式从带标记曲面和装饰边缘的∞-范畴映射到 dg 向量空间的∞-范畴，并获得了二维射影映射类群作用与三维幂级数不变量的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为三维拓扑量子场论提供严格的数学形式化，特别是作为某些 N=4 超对称量子场论（supersymmetric QFT）拓扑 A-扭（topological A-twist）的数学模型，同时建立模张量范畴与低维拓扑不变量的深层联系。

Method: 利用有限模张量范畴的导出∞-范畴（derived ∞-category）构造对称幺半函子，从带标记曲面和适当装饰边缘的∞-范畴映射到 dg 向量空间的∞-范畴；通过同伦截断（homotopy truncation）去除标记，并讨论了沿 Langerlands 对偶群变形量子群表示的猜想性框架。

Result: 建立了三维 TQFT 状态空间与导出∞-范畴线性化映射空间的自然等同；通过同伦截断获得无标记 TQFT，在二维给出射影映射类群（projective mapping class group）在 lysis上同调上的作用，包括 SL_2(Z) 在 Hochschild 上同调上的射影作用；在三维获得幂级数值的扭结不变量、模张量范畴不变量和闭三维流形不变量。

Conclusion: 该导出场论为拓扑 A-扭的 N=4 超对称场论提供了数学形式化；作者期望所得映射类群作用能复现 Lentner 等人及 Schweigert-Woike 的结果，并猜想存在沿 Langerlands 对偶群的量子群表示形变，从而实现沿局部系统（local systems）的 TQFT 形变。

Abstract: For any finite modular tensor category A, we show that the associated derived $\infty$-category D(A) supports a topological quantum field theory in dimension 3. This TQFT takes the form of a symmetric monoidal functor from an $\infty$-category of surfaces with markings by objects in D(A), and appropriately decorated bordisms, to the $\infty$-category of dg vector spaces. We show that the state spaces in this theory are naturally identified with linearized mapping spaces for D(A).
  Though our TQFT requires markings from the derived $\infty$-category, we show that all markings can be removed after taking a homotopy truncation. The resulting unmarked TQFT produces projective mapping class group actions on cohomology in dimension 2, and in particular a projective SL_2(Z)-action on Hochschild cohomology. We expect these mapping class group actions to recover those of Lentner et al. arxiv:2003.06527 and Schweigert-Woike arxiv:2004.14343. In dimension 3 we obtain power-series valued knot invariants, and power-series valued invariants for modular tensor categories. We also obtain power-series invariants for closed 3-manifold, though these can already be calculated at the abelian level.
  Our derived field theories are proposed as mathematical formalizations for topological A-twists of certain N=4 supersymmetric QFTs, in dimension 3. Following physical principles, we discuss the possibility of deforming our TQFTs along local systems via an analogous (conjectural) deformation of quantum group representations along the Langlands dual group.

</details>


### [145] [Hochschild Cohomology, Modular Tensor Categories, and Mapping Class Groups II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11193)
*Simon Lentner,Svea Nora Mierach,Christoph Schweigert,Yorck Sommerhaeuser*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在第二部分中，针对正特征域上有限群的Drinfel'd double，显式计算了映射类群（mapping class group）在导出块空间（derived block spaces）上的作用，并将Lyubashenko方法与表示簇（representation varieties）理论建立联系，证明导出块空间上的映射类群表示与普通块空间上的表示在一般情况下不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者在第一部分中已将曲面映射类群在共形块空间上的作用推广到导出块空间，适用于不一定半单的模范畴（modular category）。第二部分旨在对这一推广在正特征域上的有限群Drinfel'd double情形给出显式计算，以检验导出块空间上的表示是否与普通情形一致。

Method: 本文将Lyubashenko关于映射类群表示的途径与表示簇理论相连接，通过这一桥梁计算正特征域上有限群的Drinfel'd double对应的映射类群在导出块空间上的作用。

Result: 计算结果表明，映射类群在导出块空间上的表示与在普通块空间（ordinary block spaces）上的表示在一般情况下是不同的。

Conclusion: 正特征域上的Drinfel'd double情形下，导出块空间给出了不同于传统共形块空间的映射类群表示，说明导出构造在此类模范畴中具有独立的数学意义。

Abstract: In the first part of this work, we have, for a not necessarily semisimple modular category, generalized the action of the mapping class groups of surfaces on the spaces of conformal blocks to the so-called derived block spaces. In the second part presented here, we compute this action explicitly in the case of Drinfel'd doubles of finite groups over fields of positive characteristic. To do that, we connect Lyubashenko's approach to mapping class group representations with the theory of representation varieties. In this way, we are able to show that the mapping class group representations on the derived block spaces are in general different from those on the ordinary block spaces.

</details>

### [146] [MEDA: Measurement-Efficient Disorder-Aware Majorana Zero Mode Detection in Realistic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26208)
*Nathan Jones,Binayyak Roy,Valentine Mohaugen,Ian Lewis,Toby Cox,Sumanta Tewari,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出 MEDA 框架（Measurement-Efficient, Disorder-Aware），一种面向真实器件的马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes, MZMs）检测方案，通过将稀疏可测观测量直接映射到周期无序不变量（periodic disorder invariant, PDI），在保持预测质量的同时将测量量降低 10 倍，并具备良好的物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错拓扑量子计算依赖对 MZMs 的可靠检测，但传统拓扑指标在有限且无序的系统中存在固有偏差，难以区分真实 MZM 与平庸态；同时，将拓扑指标通过机器学习映射到实际观测量需要密集且昂贵的电导测量，造成严重的规模化瓶颈。

Method: MEDA 框架将稀疏、实际可获得的观测量直接映射到稳健的周期无序不变量 PDI 上，采用一种新颖的稀疏参数区间，并结合与拓扑能隙协议（topological gap protocol）一致的输入特征排序策略。

Result: MEDA 在中等至强无序区间内将测量量减少 10 倍，同时保持了预测质量，且在传统方法受限的强无序条件下仍表现稳健。

Conclusion: MEDA 同时解决了拓扑指标偏差与测量效率两大问题，为真实器件中的 MZM 检测提供了一种高效、抗无序且物理可解释的实用方案。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant topological quantum computing relies on identifying Majorana zero modes (MZMs), but reliable detection in realistic devices remains challenging. Conventional topological indicators are inherently biased in finite, disordered systems, blurring the distinction between true MZMs and trivial states. Furthermore, attempts to map these indicators to real observables via machine learning require dense, expensive conductance measurements, creating a severe scaling bottleneck. To simultaneously address topological bias and measurement limitations, we present MEDA: a Measurement-Efficient, Disorder-Aware framework for MZM detection in realistic devices. MEDA maps sparse, practically obtainable observables directly to the robust periodic disorder invariant (PDI). Using a novel sparse parameter regime, MEDA reduces measurement volume by 10x while maintaining predictive quality, even in moderate to strong disorder regimes that limit conventional methods. Furthermore, MEDA naturally prioritizes input features consistent with the topological gap protocol, demonstrating strong physical interpretability.

</details>
