### [147] [Archimedean Seesaw: Small Neutrino Masses and Large Lepton-number Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10062)
*Tao Han,Alejandro Ibarra,Subhojit Roy,Martina Sabová*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出一类跷跷板模型（seesaw models），其中任意大的轻子数破坏（lepton-number violation, LNV）可以与亚电子伏特的中微子质量自然共存，推翻了大LNV必然被微小小中微子质量抑制的常见认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为可观测的轻子数破坏必然被极其微小的中微子质量所抑制，本文旨在挑战这一普遍认知，寻找大LNV与亚eV中微子质量可以共存的理论框架。

Method: 作者在中微子汤川耦合（Yukawa couplings）和重马约拉纳质量矩阵（Majorana mass matrix）中构造了一个对称性保护的纹理零（texture-zero）结构，该结构产生了所需的偶然对称性（accidental symmetry），从而即使重扇区存在任意大的LNV，轻中微子也不会获得质量；微小中微子质量则通过提升纹理零结构同时保持底层对称性自然产生。

Result: 该框架预言了大活性-惰性混合（active-sterile mixing）的重中性轻子（Heavy Neutral Leptons），其质量覆盖广泛的实验可及范围，并在对撞机和强度前沿（intensity-frontier）实验中产生可观测的LNV信号。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个在实验上丰富且可测的理论框架，表明大LNV不必然与亚eV中微子质量不相容，拓展了寻找LNV现象的可行实验途径。

Abstract: Contrary to the common lore that observable lepton-number violation (LNV) is inevitably suppressed by tiny neutrino masses, we identify a class of seesaw models in which arbitrarily large LNV can naturally coexist with sub-eV neutrino masses. We construct a symmetry-protected texture-zero structure in the neutrino Yukawa couplings and heavy Majorana mass matrix that gives rise to the required accidental symmetry, thereby protecting the light neutrinos from acquiring mass even in the presence of arbitrarily large LNV in the heavy sector. Small neutrino masses arise naturally from lifting the texture-zero structure while preserving the underlying symmetry. The resulting framework offers a rich and experimentally accessible phenomenology, predicting Heavy Neutral Leptons with sizeable active-sterile mixing over a broad range of experimentally accessible masses, giving rise to observable LNV signatures at collider and intensity-frontier experiments.

</details>


### [148] [A $U(1)_{(B-L)_3}$ model for dark matter and the $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ excess reported by Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10086)
*MohammadAli Abri,Yasaman Farzan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一个仅对第三代费米子带电的 $U(1)_{(B-L)_3}$ 规范对称性模型，其中由手性费米子（chiral fermion）$\chi_L$ 消除反常，该粒子可充当热暗物质（thermal dark matter）候选体。模型中的新规范玻色子会在夸克质量基下产生对 $b$ 和 $s$ 夸克的非对角耦合，为 $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ 过程带来新贡献，作者探讨了该模型解释 Belle II 实验中观察到的这一过程超出（excess）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索一种新的规范对称性结构，即仅对第三代费米子带电荷的 $U(1)_{(B-L)_3}$ 模型，同时考察其作为暗物质（dark matter）候选解释的可行性和对稀有味物理过程的影响。此外，Belle II 实验近期报道了 $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ 衰变过程的超出，该模型有望为此提供解释。

Method: 通过要求 $[U(1)_{(B-L)_3}]^3$ 反常消除引入规范群单态但带电的手性费米子 $\chi_L$，使其经冻结（freeze-out）机制通过湮灭为第三代费米子对来产生暗物质热残余丰度。作者系统分析来自 LHC 新规范玻色子搜索、暗物质直接探测、电弱精确数据（electroweak precision data）、$\Upsilon$ 衰变、CKM 矩阵以及 $B_s^0$-$\bar{B}_s^0$ 混合等各方面的约束，并在此框架下检验 Belle II 的 $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ 超出解释。

Result: 该模型在同时满足 LHC、暗物质和电弱精确数据等多项约束的前提下，仍保留了解释 Belle II 报道的 $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ 超出事件的参数空间。

Conclusion: $U(1)_{(B-L)_3}$ 模型为热暗物质和第三代费米子味物理异常现象提供了一个统一的框架，其可行性需由未来实验数据的进一步检验来确定。

Abstract: We explore the $U(1)_{(B-L)_3}$ gauge symmetry under which only third generation fermions are charged. The cancelation of the $[U(1)_{(B-L)_3}]^3$ anomaly requires a chiral fermion, $χ_L$, singlet under the gauge group but charged under $U(1)_{(B-L)_3}$. This chiral fermion can play the role of thermal dark matter with a relic abundance set via the freeze-out scenario through the annihilation to pairs of the third generation fermions. In the quark mass basis, the new gauge boson will have off-diagonal couplings to the $b$ and $s$ quarks which can lead to a new contribution to $B^+\to K^+ν_τ\barν_τ$. We discuss the relevant bounds from the LHC searches for a new gauge boson, dark matter searches, electroweak precision data, $Υ$ decay, the CKM matrix and the $B_s^0-\bar{B}_s^0$ mixing. We entertain the possibility of explaining the $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ excess recently reported by Belle II within this model.

</details>


### [149] [Precision Tests of SM and new physics with the COHERENT Ge-mini and TEXONO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10104)
*Tousib Ahmed,Ayan Chattaraj,Anirban Majumdar,Newton Nath,Rahul Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对COHERENT Ge-mini和TEXONO实验的最新锗基相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（CEνNS）数据进行了全面数值分析，测试标准模型（SM）并探寻新物理。通过联合CEνNS与弹性中微子-电子散射（EνNS）信号并一致处理探测器效应和系统不确定性，获得了与SM预测一致的低能弱混合角测定；同时给出了中微子电磁性质（磁矩、毫电荷、电荷半径、环偶矩）的新约束，并研究了轻标量与矢量中介子以及通过跃迁偶极、标量和矢量门户产生的惰性中性轻子，结果显示当前锗基CEνNS实验为精确电弱测试和超越SM物理提供了强大低能平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用最新的锗基CEνNS实验数据，在低能标度下检验标准模型（Standard Model, SM）并探索超越标准模型（beyond the SM, BSM）的新物理信号，尤其是中微子电磁性质和轻暗物质中介子等尚未被很好约束的领域。

Method: 对COHERENT Ge-mini和TEXONO实验的CEνNS与EνNS数据进行了综合数值分析，采用一致的探测器效应与系统不确定性处理方法；在此基础上对弱混合角（weak mixing angle）、中微子电磁性质、轻标量/矢量中介子以及惰性中性轻子的产生门户分别进行拟合与约束推导。

Result: 从COHERENT Ge-mini获得了与SM预测一致的低能弱混合角测定；对中微子磁矩、毫电荷、电荷半径和环偶矩给出了新的约束，其中TEXONO提供极具竞争力的上限；加入EνNS数据后，中微子毫电荷灵敏度提升了最多三个数量级；轻标量和矢量中介子的约束在反应堆与散裂中子源间呈显著互补性；惰性中性轻子质量探测范围覆盖亚MeV至数十MeV。

Conclusion: 锗基CEνNS实验在低能区域是一种强大的精密电弱测试工具，并为多种BSM物理提供互补性探测途径，展现出该类实验在粒子物理基础检验中的广阔前景。

Abstract: A comprehensive numerical analysis of the latest germanium based CE$ν$NS data from the COHERENT Ge-mini and TEXONO experiments has been conducted to test the Standard Model (SM) and search for new physics. By combining CE$ν$NS and E$ν$NS signals with a consistent treatment of detector effects and systematic uncertainties, we obtain a low energy determination of the weak mixing angle from COHERENT Ge-mini, in agreement with the SM prediction. We derive novel constraints on neutrino electromagnetic properties, including the magnetic moment, millicharge, charge radius, and anapole moment, with TEXONO providing particularly competitive bounds. Inclusion of E$ν$NS, significantly improves the sensitivity to the neutrino millicharge by up to three orders of magnitude. We also investigated light scalar and vector mediators, finding striking complementarity between reactor and stopped pion sources across different mediator mass regimes. Finally, we put bounds on sterile neutral leptons production through transition dipole, scalar, and vector portals, probing masses from the sub MeV to tens of MeV scale. Our results demonstrate that current germanium based CE$ν$NS experiments provide a powerful low energy laboratory for precision electroweak tests and complementary probes of a broad class of physics beyond the SM.

</details>


### [150] [Commensurate Structure of Quark and Lepton Mixing: Eighteenth Powers of One Parameter and a Digital-Clock Quantization of the Unitarity Triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10312)
*Vernon Barger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在夸克和轻子混合数据中识别出两种协同结构（乘法结构与角结构）及其精确关系，基于层级参数$\varepsilon\equiv|V_{ub}|^{3/10}$，发现Fritzsch–Xing角满足简单的幂律关系，相位为最大相位，并推衍出么正三角形（unitarity triangle）的$\pi/24$晶格量子化以及轻子PMNS矩阵中的CP相位预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克和轻子的混合矩阵看起来具有某种内在结构，但尚未有统一的参数化分析能同时揭示乘法与角结构及其相互关系。作者试图从实验数据本身出发，在参数化层面对混合结构进行系统识别，而不引入任何动力学假设。

Method: 以层级参数$\varepsilon\equiv|V_{ub}|^{3/10}=0.1869\pm0.0018$为基准，分析Fritzsch–Xing（FX）角的正弦值是否遵循$\varepsilon$的幂次规律；利用相位不变性将角结构映射到$\pi/24$晶格上量化的么正三角形；将该分析推广到轻子PMNS矩阵（仅角结构适用，且无Majorana相位参与）；并检验同一$\varepsilon$是否确定中微子质量比。

Result: 夸克FX角满足$\sin\theta_u=\varepsilon^{26/18}$、$\sin\theta_d=\varepsilon^{17/18}$、$\sin\theta=\varepsilon^{34/18}$且系数偏差在$2.5\%$以内，相位为最大相位$\phi_{\rm FX}=92.3^\circ\pm2.7^\circ$；由此得到Jarlskog不变量$J=\varepsilon^{111/18}\sin\phi_{\rm FX}$匹配实测值。么正三角形在$\pi/24$晶格上量子化为$(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=(12,3,9)\times7.5^\circ$。轻子方面预测$δ_{\rm CP}\simeq296^\circ$（上外卦限）或$244^\circ$（下外卦限）；夸克与轻子三角形共享$\beta=22.5^\circ$。中微子质量比$r\equiv m_2/m_3=\varepsilon^{19/18}$，PMNS第一行按$r$的幂次给出。

Conclusion: 夸克与轻子混合数据中存在精确的乘法与角结构及其联系，两者在$\pi/24$晶格上共同量化，且共享$\beta=22.5^\circ$。所有预言（包括$δ_{\rm CP}$、$\gamma$角和中微子质量比）均可被DUNE、Hyper-Kamiokande和JUNO等实验检验。该分析局限于参数化层面，不假设任何底层动力学。

Abstract: We identify two commensurate structures in the measured quark and lepton mixing data, one multiplicative and one angular, and their exact relation. With the hierarchy parameter $\varepsilon\equiv|V_{ub}|^{3/10}=0.1869\pm0.0018$, the measured Fritzsch--Xing (FX) angles satisfy $\sinθ_u=\varepsilon^{26/18}$, $\sinθ_d=\varepsilon^{17/18}$, and $\sinθ=\varepsilon^{34/18}$ with coefficients within $2.5\%$ of unity, and the phase is maximal, $φ_{\rm FX}=92.3^\circ\pm2.7^\circ$ against $π/2$. Unit coefficients reproduce the remaining moduli at the percent level and fix the derived Jarlskog invariant, $J=\varepsilon^{111/18}\sinφ_{\rm FX}$, matching the measured $3.08(14)\times10^{-5}$. The rephasing-invariant image of this structure is a unitarity triangle quantized on the $π/24$ lattice, a digital clock, $(α,β,γ)=(12,3,9)\times7.5^\circ$, consistent with data at $1σ$ through $α\simeqφ_{\rm FX}$ and the theorem $\tanβ\simeq\sqrt{\varepsilon}$; degree-level $γ$ confronts $67.5^\circ$--$68^\circ$. For the leptons, where only the angular structure can act, its PMNS analog, formed from columns $1$ and $3$, free of Majorana phases, on the same lattice requires $δ_{\rm CP}\simeq296^\circ$ in the upper octant or $244^\circ$ in the lower, testable at DUNE and Hyper-Kamiokande. The quark and lepton triangles share $β=22.5^\circ$, measured for quarks, predicted for leptons, and differ by a transfer of two lattice units, with corollary $δ_{\rm CP}\simeq-δ\simeq295^\circ$. The same $\varepsilon$ sets the neutrino mass ratio, $r\equiv m_2/m_3=\varepsilon^{19/18}$, and the PMNS first row follows in powers of $r$, $(r^{1/9},\,r^{1/3},\,\tfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}r)$, both relations at $0.2σ$ and testable at JUNO. The analysis is confined to the parameterization level; no dynamics is assumed.

</details>


### [151] [Probing Gluon Linear Polarization with Dihadron Fragmentation in $χ_b$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10570)
*Zhi-Guo He,Guanghui Li,Yang Liu,Yu-Jie Tian,Xin-Kai Wen,Bin Yan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本篇论文提出利用 P 波底偶素态 χb0 的颜色单态衰变通道中的两个高能胶子（gluon）作为探针，首次在领先阶下直接测量线性极化胶子的双强子碎裂函数（dihadron fragmentation function）。该过程产生 Artru–Collins 型角关联，可通过现有 Belle 数据或 Belle II 数据实现百分量级的不对称性测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性极化胶子的双强子碎裂函数尚未在实验上被获取，导致自旋依赖的胶子强子化（hadronization）中一个重要方面仍未得到探索。

Method: 在共线因子化（collinear factorization）框架下，分析 χb0 颜色单态衰变到两个高能胶子的领先阶过程；胶子随后碎裂为分离的双强子对，从而产生 Artru–Collins 型角关联。同时利用谱观模型（spectator model）进行基准估算。

Result: 谱观模型基准估算显示不对称性为百分量级，处于现有 Belle 数据的探测能力范围内；Belle II 的专门数据集可显著提升统计精度，从而对线性极化胶子双强子碎裂函数的运动学依赖施加更严格的约束。

Conclusion: 该衰变通道为线性极化胶子双强子碎裂函数提供了首个直接探针，同时其中半包含衰变率可约束非极化胶子双强子碎裂函数，补充了胶子强子化自旋结构实验探索的空白。

Abstract: The dihadron fragmentation function (DiFF) of a linearly polarized gluon has not yet been accessed experimentally, leaving an important aspect of spin-dependent gluon hadronization unexplored. We show that, at leading order, the color-singlet decay channel of the $P$-wave bottomonium state $χ_{b0}$ produces two energetic gluons with correlated linear polarizations. Within collinear factorization, their fragmentation into separate dihadron pairs generates an Artru--Collins-type angular correlation that provides the first direct probe of the linearly polarized gluon DiFF, while the corresponding semi-inclusive decay rate constrains the unpolarized gluon DiFF. A spectator-model benchmark indicates percent-level asymmetries, potentially within reach of existing Belle data. A dedicated Belle~II data set would substantially improve the statistical precision, enabling more stringent constraints on the kinematic dependence of the linearly polarized gluon DiFF.

</details>


### [152] [Evolution of the underlying event and non-extensive thermodynamics in pp collisions from RHIC to LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10717)
*Suraj Prasad,Aditya Nath Mishra,Guy Paić,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 使用PYTHIA8模拟研究了pp碰撞（质心能量从62.4 GeV到13 TeV）中粒子产生的几何与热力学特性，通过Tsallis-Pareto参数和事件形状分析揭示了底层事件（underlying event, UE）区域可扩展至传统横向区域之外，并区分了软硬粒子产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究质心能量从数十GeV到TeV量级的宽泛范围内，pp碰撞中粒子产生的几何与热力学特性，以更深入理解底层事件（underlying event, UE）的结构和软硬产生机制的区别。

Method: 利用PYTHIA8蒙特卡洛模拟，研究带电粒子产生相对于领头粒子的方位角（azimuthal）依赖性；在不同固定Δφ区间提取Tsallis-Pareto参数；结合带电粒子多重数（multiplicity）和事件拓扑分析，并在不同横向球状度（transverse spherocity）和带电粒子平直度（flattenicity）类别中开展研究。

Result: UE主导区域可扩展至40°≲|Δφ|≲140°的扩大的角区间；喷注型（jetty）事件比各向同性（isotropic）事件具有更大的q值和更低的Ts参数；带电粒子多重数和横向球状度的Δφ分布在不同碰撞能量下形状保持普适性，而Tsallis参数在近侧和远侧区域保留了与领头粒子相关的残余能量依赖性。

Conclusion: 研究表明UE区域在RHIC至LHC能区均可超越传统横向区域；事件形状选择可有效区分软硬产生机制；尽管整体事件活动性随能量显著增强，事件形状轮廓保持普适，而热力学参数显示出由领头粒子驱动的局部能量依赖性。

Abstract: We investigate the geometrical and thermodynamical aspects of particle production in pp collisions over a broad center-of-mass energy, ranging from $\sqrt{s}=62.4$ GeV to 13 TeV using PYTHIA8. We study the azimuthal dependence of charged-particle production relative to the leading particle. This includes the study of the Tsallis--Pareto parameters extracted from the charged-particle transverse-momentum spectra in fixed $Δφ$ intervals relative to the leading charged particle. The simultaneous analysis of charged-particle multiplicity and event topology demonstrates that the underlying event (UE) dominated region can be extended beyond the conventional transverse side region across RHIC and LHC energies. To further disentangle effects from soft and hard particle production mechanisms, the study is performed in different transverse spherocity and charged-particle flattenicity classes. We demonstrate that the UE region extends beyond the conventional transverse region, validating an enlarged angular interval of $40^\circ\lesssim|Δφ|\lesssim140^\circ$ from RHIC to LHC energies. Event-shape selections reveal that jetty events consistently exhibit larger $q$ and lower $T_{\rm s}$ than isotropic events. The $Δφ$ profiles of charged-particle multiplicity and transverse spherocity remain universal in shape across collision energies despite the strong increase in overall event activity, whereas the Tsallis parameters retain a residual, leading-particle-driven energy dependence in the near- and away-side regions.

</details>


### [153] [Superfluid He-4 as Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10761)
*Zizhou Huang,Mihaela Parvu,Dmitry Budker,Ariel Zhitnitsky,Konstantin Zioutas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的暗物质（dark matter, DM）探测方案：借助超流氦-4（superfluid helium-4）大型强子对撞机（LHC）冷却系统作为大规模地球探测装置，被动利用该系统探测轴子（反）夸克核团（Axion (Anti)Quark Nuggets, AQN）这类暗物质候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流氦-4是高灵敏度暗物质探测的理想介质，已有多个合作组构建并测试了超流氦探测器，尤其针对亚GeV（sub-GeV）暗物质质量范围。本文旨在探索对更高质量的暗物质候选体——AQN——的探测新途径。

Method: 方法的核心思路是寄生式（parasitically）利用超流氦-4 LHC冷却系统作为独特的大规模地球暗物质探测器来探测AQN。针对AQN穿越超流氦周围金属部件的问题，本文特别研究了AQN在金属结构中的传播行为。

Result: 分析表明，AQN在其传播轨迹周围会引发显著加热效应（significant heating），从而对包裹超流氦的密封容器（enclosure）造成损伤，这为该类暗物质探测提供了可利用的物理信号。

Conclusion: 该方案将现存的LHC超流氦冷却系统转用于暗物质探测，为AQN这类暗物质候选体提供了一种大规模、低成本的地球探测手段，具有潜在的应用前景。

Abstract: Superfluid helium-4 is an attractive medium for high-sensitivity detection of dark matter (DM) candidates (as well as other particles). There are several collaborations that have built and tested superfluid helium detectors, particularly focusing on the sub-GeV DM mass range. In this paper we discuss a novel idea for detecting Axion (Anti)Quark Nuggets (AQN) by using parasitically the superfluid helium-4 LHC cooling system as a unique large-scale earth bound DM detector. In the case of the AQN, we address the question of propagation in the metal parts surrounding the helium. An AQN induces significant heating around the propagation track that damages the enclosure.

</details>


### [154] [TauPolaris: reconstructing tau lepton polarimetric vectors with conditional normalizing flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10961)
*Daniel Winterbottom,Lucas Russell*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍TauPolaris，一种基于条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）重建τ轻子极化矢量的工具，通过估计不可探测的中微子动量来恢复τ自旋信息。该方法优于均方误差训练的神经网络，并在LHC模拟数据中展示了Higgs玻色子衰变中量子纠缠判别、CP破坏灵敏度提升及Z→ττ本底抑制等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ轻子衰变产物的运动学取决于τ自旋，从而提供产生该过程的自旋关联和CP结构信息。然而，最佳自旋可观测量——极化矢量（polarimetric vector）——的确定需要τ衰变中产生的中微子动量，而这些中微子逃逸检测，必须通过推断获得。

Method: 提出TauPolaris工具，利用条件归一化流对未检测到的中微子运动学完整条件密度进行建模，而非进行点回归。该方法既提供每个事件最可能的中微子构型，又给出其不确定性估计。

Result: 重建自旋可观测量的分辨率优于使用均方误差损失训练的神经网络。在模拟的LHC质子-质子碰撞（含探测器分辨率效应）中，三种应用得到验证：在HL-LHC下以至少4.3σ显著性区分Higgs衰变中量子纠缠的存在与缺失；H→ττ衰变中CP破坏灵敏度提升18%；引入抑制Higgs搜索中Z→ττ本底的新变量。

Conclusion: TauPolaris通过概率性建模中微子运动学，实现了更优的τ极化矢量重建，在Higgs物理中展现出增强的自旋关联分析、CP敏感度及本底抑制能力。

Abstract: The kinematics of tau lepton decay products depend on the tau spin, giving access to the spin correlations and CP structure of the process that produced them. The optimal spin observable is the polarimetric vector, which points along the most likely direction of the tau spin. Determining it requires the momenta of the neutrinos produced in the tau decays, which escape detection and must therefore be inferred. We present TauPolaris, a tool for reconstructing tau polarimetric vectors by estimating the undetected neutrino momenta with a conditional normalizing flow. Rather than performing a point regression, the method models the full conditional density of the neutrino kinematics, providing both the most likely configuration for each event and an estimate of its uncertainty. The resolution of the reconstructed spin observables improves on that obtained from networks trained with a mean-squared-error loss. Using simulated LHC proton-proton collisions, including detector resolution effects, we demonstrate the method in three applications. For tau leptons produced in Higgs boson decays, we show that the presence of quantum entanglement can be distinguished from its absence with a significance of at least $4.3σ$ at the High-Luminosity LHC. We demonstrate an 18% improvement in the sensitivity to CP violation in $H\rightarrowττ$ decays. Finally, we introduce new variables for suppressing the $Z\rightarrowττ$ background in Higgs boson searches.

</details>


### [155] [Hilbert Series of Pseudoscalar Mesons: Operators and Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11118)
*Joydeep Chakrabortty,Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Siddhartha Karmakar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用希尔伯特级数（Hilbert series）方法，在全局味对称性 $SU(3)_L \times SU(3)_R \times SU(2)_{L_H} \times SU(2)_{R_H}$ 及平移对称性约束下，构造了由赝标介子（pseudoscalar mesons）构成的完整非冗余算子基，并将CKM矩阵元作为spurion场系统纳入标准模型弱相互作用，为重介子弱衰变振幅提供了模型无关的参数化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重介子弱衰变振幅的模型无关描述需要一套完整且非冗余的算子基，而以往方法往往依赖人工枚举或特定模型假设，缺乏系统的对称性约束下的构造策略。作者旨在通过希尔伯特级数方法，在给定对称群下系统生成完整算子基，从而建立衰变振幅与威尔逊系数之间的一一对应关系。

Method: 采用希尔伯特级数方法对满足给定对称性的赝标介子算子空间进行枚举与筛选，构建非冗余算子基；将CKM矩阵元提升为在全局对称性下变换的spurion场以系统纳入标准模型弱相互作用；进一步提出一种展开（unfolding）程序，将有效算子与其可能的紫外（ultraviolet）起源联系起来。

Result: 所得算子基为两体和三体重介子弱衰变振幅提供了模型无关的参数化，其中威尔逊系数对应约化强子振幅；所有振幅求和规则（sum rules）均可直接从算子基结构推导。框架再现了已知的味关系（flavor relations），并预言了由对称性约束算子基派生的额外恒等式。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了对称性约束下算子基与重介子弱衰变振幅之间的系统性对应，既统一了已知味关系，又提供了预测新恒等式的机制，并为有效场论（effective field theory）层级上与紫外模型的衔接提供了新路径。

Abstract: We construct the complete non-redundant basis of operators built from pseudoscalar mesons using the Hilbert series method, subject to the global flavor symmetry $SU(3)_L \times SU(3)_R \times SU(2)_{L_H} \times SU(2)_{R_H}$, together with the underlying shift symmetry. The SM weak interactions are incorporated systematically by promoting the CKM matrix elements to spurion fields transforming under the global symmetry. The resulting operator basis provides a model-independent parametrization of two- and three-body weak decay amplitudes of heavy mesons, with the corresponding Wilson coefficients serving as reduced hadronic amplitudes. We demonstrate that all amplitude sum rules follow directly from the structure of the operator basis and propose an unfolding procedure that connects the effective operators to their possible ultraviolet origins. Our framework reproduces the known flavor relations while predicting additional identities arising from the symmetry-constrained operator basis.

</details>


### [156] [Connecting Heavy-Quarkonium Born-Oppenheimer EFT to the Peskin OPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10201)
*Arkadiy I. Syamtomov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了紧凑重夸克偶素（heavy quarkonium）胶子响应的源依赖型QCD到BOEFT（Born-Oppenheimer有效场论）匹配框架。通过规范协变的投影响应将保留的BO通道与由局部逆矩表示的带隙扇区分开，在弱耦合和源-带隙层级假设下，该响应可约化为Peskin构造。在磁学扩展中，文章给出了自旋单态1S态的各向同性序贯M1逆矩和协变动能海鸥项的具体系数表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立一种更为系统、源依赖（source-dependent）的QCD到BOEFT匹配方案，以统一并推广已有的Bhanot-Peskin和Peskin构造，并使磁学匹配系数的计算具有更清晰的理论基础。

Method: 作者引入规范协变的投影响应（gauge-covariant projected response），将动力学保留的BO通道与由局部逆矩（local inverse moments）表示的带隙扇区分离；通过传播-顶点约束（propagation-vertex bounds）建立通道因子化局域OPE的充分判据，并利用弱耦合和源-带隙层级（source-gap hierarchies）将响应约化为Peskin构造；对自旋单态1S态序贯计算了各向同性M1逆矩和协变动能海鸥项的磁学匹配系数。

Result: 在库仑极限下，Bhanot-Peskin电矩和离解截断可从单一谱测度导出；磁学扩展中，自旋单态1S态给出的各向同性序贯M1逆矩为c_{B,GG}^{(1)ij}=5πα_s^2(c_FV_iso^{(s)})^2δ^{ij}/16，协变动能海鸥项为c_{B,dia}^{(1)ij}=-πα_s^2δ^{ij}/4，且两者均为磁学匹配系数的可辨识贡献而非完整值。

Conclusion: 该源依赖型匹配框架为QCD到BOEFT的胶子响应提供了统一且可验证的系统化途径，但在磁学匹配系数中，文中给出的是部分贡献，完整磁学匹配系数仍需进一步计算。

Abstract: We construct a source-dependent QCD-to-BOEFT matching for the gluonic response of compact heavy quarkonium. A gauge-covariant projected response separates BO channels retained dynamically from gapped sectors represented by local inverse moments. Propagation-vertex bounds provide sufficient criteria for a channel-factorized local OPE, while the additional weak-coupling and source-gap hierarchies reduce this response to the Peskin construction. In the Coulombic limit the established Bhanot-Peskin electric moments and dissociation cut arise from one spectral measure. As a magnetic extension, for a spin-singlet $1S$ state the isotropic sequential $M1$ inverse moment gives $c_{B,GG}^{(1)ij}=5πα_s^2 (c_FV_{\rm iso}^{(s)})^2δ^{ij}/16$, while the leading covariant-kinetic seagull gives $c_{B,{\rm dia}}^{(1)ij}=-πα_s^2δ^{ij}/4$. These are identifiable contributions to, rather than the complete value of, the magnetic matching coefficient.

</details>


### [157] [Baryogenesis and CMB spectral distortion from Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10633)
*Zhenhao Zhang,Mingqiu Li,Sichun Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的重子数产生机制：类轴子粒子（axion-like particle）通过修改标准模型中相关规范场（gauge field）的色散关系，改变陈-西蒙斯数（Chern-Simons number），从而源产生重子数不对称（baryon asymmetry）。该机制可自然达到观测到的重子不对称值，同时轴子-光子耦合还会产生宇宙微波背景（CMB）谱畸变，且该畸变在低频处趋于常数，区别于传统的 y 型和 μ 型畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙中重子不对称性的起源是粒子宇宙学的基本问题之一，本文旨在探索一种未涉及的新机制，即利用类轴子粒子对标准模型规范场构型施加影响，从而产生所需的重子不对称度。

Method: 作者论证了类轴子粒子通过改变标准模型中规范场的色散关系来修改陈-西蒙斯数，进而源产生重子数破坏；随后推导了最终重子数与轴子背景演化的定量关系，并据此估计产生的重子不对称度；此外还研究了轴子-光子耦合导致的CMB谱畸变特征。

Result: 该机制能够自然产生与观测相符的重子不对称值；轴子-光子耦合引起CMB谱畸变，且这种畸变在低频极限下趋近于常数，这与传统 y 型和 μ 型畸变的频谱行为显著不同。

Conclusion: 文中提出的机制为早期宇宙重子不对称的起源提供了一种可行的新途径，同时其产生的CMB谱畸变具有可区分的低频特征，可作为该机制潜在的观测检验信号。

Abstract: We discuss a mechanism for generating the baryon asymmetry in the early universe. We show that an axion-like particle can modify the related gauge field configurations in the Standard Model, thereby altering their dispersion relations. This change in the Chern-Simons number can source a violation of baryon number. We derive the relationship between the resulting baryon number and the evolution of the axion background. We estimate the baryon asymmetry produced via this mechanism and show that the observed value can be naturally achieved. We also show that axion photon coupling produces Cosmic Microwave Background spectral distortion. Our results show that the resulting distortion approaches a constant at low frequencies, unlike the conventional y-type and $μ$-type distortions.

</details>

### [158] [Reconstructing Dark Matter Mass and Discriminating Standard and Non-Standard WIMP-Nucleus Interactions with Paleo-Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10105)
*Dionysios P. Theodosopoulos,Katherine Freese,Chris Kelso,Patrick Stengel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了古探测器（paleo-detectors）在检测到暗物质（dark matter, DM）信号时，重建暗物质参数及区分不同类型暗物质与原子核相互作用的能力，涵盖弹性与非弹性散射。结果表明古探测器可重建低至约10 GeV及高达1 TeV范围内的弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）质量，并能在不依赖核反冲方向测量的情况下区分标准的NREFT相互作用与非标准相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已表明古探测器对多种暗物质情景具有灵敏度，但尚缺乏对其在存在暗物质信号时重建参数和区分相互作用类型能力的系统评估，而这对于未来的暗物质搜索与信号解读至关重要。

Method: 研究同时考虑弹性与非弹性的暗物质-原子核散射，针对代表性的核反冲径迹读出方案，评估了弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）质量在不同非相对论有效场论（Non-Relativistic Effective Field Theory, NREFT）相互作用下的重建能力，并对比了标准（自旋无关或自旋相关）与非标准NREFT相互作用的可分辨性。

Result: 古探测器可在多种NREFT相互作用下重建WIMP质量，尤其对小于约10 GeV的质量范围具有独特能力，且可将其重建上限延伸至1 TeV，较传统直接探测实验的可重建质量范围扩展约2倍。此外，在WIMP质量大于约10 GeV时，古探测器可在不测量核反冲方向的情况下排除几乎所有非标准NREFT相互作用，而传统实验通常依赖方向测量才能实现类似判别。

Conclusion: 古探测器不仅能补充传统直接探测实验在低质量WIMP区域的灵敏度空白，还可在参数重建与相互作用类型判别方面提供显著优势，是暗物质探测的重要互补手段。

Abstract: Paleo-detectors record and retain crystal damage in ancient minerals from nuclear recoils induced by dark matter scattering over geological timescales. Previous studies have shown that paleo-detectors can provide sensitivity to a variety of dark matter (DM) scenarios which is complementary to conventional direct-detection experiments. In this paper, we complete the first detailed study of how well paleo-detectors can reconstruct DM parameters or distinguish between different types of DM interactions with nuclei in the presence of a DM signal, considering both elastic and inelastic DM-nucleus scattering. For representative nuclear recoil track read-out scenarios, we demonstrate that weakly interacting massive particle (WIMP) DM masses can be reconstructed for a variety of Non-Relativistic Effective Field Theory (NREFT) interactions between WIMPs and nuclei. In particular, paleo-detectors are projected to be capable of reconstructing WIMP masses $\lesssim$ 10 GeV, a regime that is challenging for conventional direct-detection experiments; further, we find that paleo-detectors could reconstruct WIMP masses up to 1 TeV for hypothetical signals within their accessible parameter space, extending the mass range over which reconstruction is possible by up to a factor of $\sim 2$ compared with analogous studies of conventional direct-detection experiments. In addition, we demonstrate that paleo-detectors could discriminate between canonical spin-independent or spin-dependent NREFT interactions and non-canonical interactions which can depend on the relative velocity or momentum transferred between the WIMP and nucleus. Specifically, at WIMP masses $\gtrsim$ 10 GeV, we project that canonical NREFT interactions can be excluded by paleo-detectors in the cases of nearly all non-canonical interactions without measurement of nuclear recoil direction, which conventional experiments typically require.

</details>

### [159] [Analytical Theory for Anomalous Diffusion in the Anderson Model with Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10868)
*Elizaveta Safonova,Aleksey Lunkin,Mikhail Feigel'man*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在无相互作用的安德森模型（Anderson model）中，通过引入重尾跳跃振幅（heavy-tailed hopping amplitudes），解析地建立了反常输运理论。研究揭示了一个扩展的中间时域，其中有效次扩散指数（subdiffusive effective exponent）出现，即便系统没有任何相互作用或多体效应。作者解析求解输运方程，获得了均方位移（mean-square displacement）的时间依赖，并识别出从中间反常区到渐近扩散输运的连续交叉。接近局域化转变（localization transition）时，次扩散窗口的空间范围参数发散，而向常规扩散的交叉在 $Γ_0^{-1}$ 单位下保持有限，产生了越来越宽的反常输运区，可紧密类似于多体局域化转变附近观测到的Griffiths型输运。研究结果表明，仅凭稀有跳跃过程即可提供微观单粒子机制来解释稳健的输运异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化（many-body localization）转变附近观测到的反常输运通常被归因于Griffiths效应等相互作用机制，但该论文试图探究：在没有任何相互作用或多体效应的情况下，单粒子系统的无序本身能否产生类似的反常输运现象，从而为区分相互作用与无序驱动动力学提供理论基准。

Method: 论文研究具有重尾跳跃振幅分布的无相互作用安德森模型，通过解析求解输运方程来推导均方位移的时间依赖，分析中间反常输运区与渐近扩散行为之间的连续交叉，并考察局域化转变附近次扩散窗口的空间尺度行为。

Result: 结果表明，重尾跳跃分布可在无相互作用系统中产生扩展的中间反常输运时域，其有效次扩散指数显著异于正常扩散；随着局域化转变接近，次扩散窗口在空间中参数性发散，而扩散交叉保持有限时间尺度，从而在空间上形成越来越宽的反常输运区，其行为可与靠近多体局域化转变的Griffiths型输运相媲美。

Conclusion: 稀有跳跃过程本身即为一种微观单粒子机制，能够稳健地产生输运异常，无需任何相互作用或多体效应。该结果确立了一个解析基准，用于区分相互作用诱导的效应与纯无序驱动的动力学。

Abstract: We develop an analytical theory of anomalous transport in a noninteracting Anderson model with heavy-tailed hopping amplitudes. The broad distribution of hopping amplitudes gives rise to an extended intermediate-time regime with a subdiffusive effective exponent, despite the absence of interactions or genuine many-body effects. By solving the transport equations analytically, we derive the time dependence of the mean-square displacement and identify a continuous crossover from an intermediate anomalous regime to asymptotically diffusive transport. As the localization transition is approached, the spatial extent of the subdiffusive window diverges parametrically, while the crossover to conventional diffusion remains finite in units of $Γ_0^{-1}$. This produces an increasingly broad anomalous transport regime in space that can closely resemble Griffiths-type transport observed near the many-body localization transition. Our results demonstrate that rare hopping processes alone provide a microscopic single-particle mechanism for robust transport anomalies, establishing an analytical benchmark for distinguishing interaction-induced effects from disorder-driven dynamics.

</details>


### [160] [Skin-Anderson Localization Transition in Strongly Coupled Disordered Non-Hermitian Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11186)
*S Rahul*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一个由Hatano-Nelson链与厄米链通过非对称链间跃迁耦合而成的双腿梯子体系，其中两条链均含有强无序。研究发现调节链间耦合的非对称性可诱导本征态在趋肤局域与Anderson局域之间发生连续转变，且能量谱呈现线隙拓扑时趋肤效应仍可重新出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序与非厄米效应的相互作用在局域化现象中扮演关键角色：无序通过Anderson局域化使本征态局域，而非厄米非互易性则驱动本征态向边界聚集形成非厄米趋肤效应。作者希望探究这两种竞争机制在具体耦合体系中的相互作用及其导致的局域化转变。

Method: 作者构建了一个双腿梯子模型，由Hatano-Nelson链（非厄米链）通过非对称链间跃迁与一条厄米链（Hermitian chain）耦合而成，并在两条链中同时引入强无序。通过调节链间跃迁的非对称性参数，系统性地研究了本征态局域化性质的演化。

Result: 研究发现，调节链间耦合的非对称性可诱导本征态发生连续转变：从趋肤局域（skin localization）到Anderson局域，再回到趋肤局域。特别值得注意的是，即使能量谱呈现出线隙拓扑（line-gap topology），非厄米趋肤效应仍能重新出现，说明稳健的趋肤局域可超越传统点隙（point-gap）机制的适用范围而存在。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了无序与非厄米效应之间更复杂的相互作用机制，表明非厄米趋肤效应并非局限于点隙拓扑条件，在线隙拓扑体系中也能稳健地存在，为理解非厄米局域化现象提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The interplay between disorder and non-Hermitian effects gives rise to a variety of intriguing localization phenomena. While disorder tends to localize the eigenstates through Anderson localization, non-Hermitian non-reciprocity promotes the formation of skin modes by driving the eigenstates toward the system boundaries giving rise to non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE). In this work, we investigate the interplay between these competing mechanisms in a two-leg ladder consisting of a Hatano-Nelson chain coupled to a Hermitian chain via asymmetric inter-chain hopping, with strong disorder present in both chains. We show that tuning the asymmetry of the inter-chain coupling induces successive transitions in the nature of the eigenstates, from skin localization to Anderson localization and subsequently back to skin localization. Remarkably, the non-Hermitian skin effect re-emerges even though the energy spectrum exhibits a line-gap topology, demonstrating that robust skin localization can persist beyond the conventional point-gap regime.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

### [161] [Kronecker Products of Symmetric Persistent Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11182)
*Masoud Gharahi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在对称（symmetric）设定下，持续性（persistence）性质在Kronecker积（Kronecker product）下保持封闭：任意两个对称持久张量的Kronecker积仍是持久张量，并由此推出对称持久性在迭代Kronecker积和Kronecker幂下封闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久张量（persistent tensors）是一类递归定义的对象，适用于替换方法（substitution method），并可给出张量秩（tensor rank）非平凡的下界。尽管持久性在一般情况下不随Kronecker积保持，探索其在何种限制下得以保持是自然且重要的问题。

Method: 首先建立全局微分恒等式，表明任意齐次多项式（homogeneous polynomials）的Kronecker积之Hessian矩阵（Hessian matrix），在重构约定（restitution convention）所规定的归一化下，等于因子Hessian矩阵的Kronecker积。随后对持久因子证明关于所有$(n-2)$重部分极化的Hessian行列式的极化完全幂恒等式（polarized perfect-power identity），并结合对称持久性的Hessian刻画（Hessian characterization）得出Kronecker积下的封闭性。

Result: 证明对称持久性在Kronecker积下封闭，即两个对称持久张量的Kronecker积仍为持久张量；作为推论，对称持久性对迭代Kronecker积和Kronecker幂均封闭。

Conclusion: 该结果拓展了持久张量的适用范围，表明在对称情形下可以通过Kronecker积构造新的持久张量，从而为获得更多非平凡张量秩下界提供了途径。

Abstract: Persistent tensors form a recursively defined class adapted to the substitution method and yield nontrivial lower bounds on tensor rank. Although persistence is not preserved under Kronecker products in general, we prove that it is preserved in the symmetric setting: the Kronecker product of any two symmetric persistent tensors is again persistent. We first establish a global differentiation identity showing that the Hessian matrix of the Kronecker product of arbitrary homogeneous polynomials is the Kronecker product of the Hessian matrices of the factors, up to the normalization dictated by the restitution convention. For persistent factors, we then prove a polarized perfect-power identity for the Hessian determinants of all $(n-2)$-fold partial polarizations. Combined with the Hessian characterization of symmetric persistence, this yields closure under Kronecker products. As consequences, symmetric persistence is closed under iterated Kronecker products and Kronecker powers.

</details>


### [162] [Log Calabi-Yau compactifications of $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ character varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10918)
*Hülya Argüz,Pierrick Bousseau*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明紧致有向曲面的SL(2,C)特征簇（character variety）及尖点曲面的一般相对SL(2,C)特征簇具有可分正规对数（dlt log）Calabi-Yau紧化（log Calabi-Yau compactification），并将Kutteri-Tehrani-Frohman与Tehrani-Frohman构造的紧化验证为对数规范且对数Calabi-Yau。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL(2,C)特征簇是几何表示论与低维拓扑中的重要研究对象，但其紧化的代数几何性质（特别是奇点与对数Calabi-Yau性质）此前尚不明确，需要一般性判据来系统地验证此类紧化的几何性质。

Method: 作者首先建立了一个一般性结果，给出从仿射簇（affine variety）的正则函数环的滤过（filtration）所诱导的紧化成为对数Calabi-Yau的充分条件；随后将该判据分别应用于紧致情形下Kutteri-Tehrani-Frohman的构造与尖点情形下Tehrani-Frohman的构造。

Result: 成功证明紧致有向曲面的SL(2,C)特征簇与尖点曲面的一般相对SL(2,C)特征簇都容许可分正规对数的对数Calabi-Yau紧化，且此前文献中构造的紧化均为对数规范（log canonical）且对数Calabi-Yau。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个基于代数滤过的通用紧化判据，并将特征簇构造链统一到对数Calabi-Yau框架下，为后续研究特征簇的奇点理论与镜像对称等方向奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove that the $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ character varieties of compact oriented surfaces and the generic relative $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ character varieties of punctured surfaces admit divisorial log terminal (dlt) log Calabi-Yau compactifications. To do this, we establish a general result giving sufficient conditions for a compactification of an affine variety arising from a filtration of its algebra of regular functions to be log Calabi-Yau. We then apply this result to show that the compactifications constructed by Kutteri-Tehrani-Frohman in the compact case and by Tehrani-Frohman in the punctured case are log canonical and log Calabi-Yau.

</details>

### [163] [Degenerate high-order hybrid bound states in the continuum beyond diffraction limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10125)
*Ji Tong Wang,Nicolae C. Panoiu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在具有C6v对称性的周期性光子结构中，揭示了衍射极限以上存在具有高阶拓扑荷和二次能带简并的Γ点简并混合连续谱束缚态（bound states in the continuum, BICs），并通过对称性保护与参数调控相结合实现辐射通道的完全抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在衍射极限以上实现简并BICs的新机制，以拓展连续谱束缚态研究的物理边界并为相关应用提供可能。

Method: 将对称性保护（抑制零级衍射通道辐射）与二次能带简并及参数调谐相结合，以消除一级衍射通道的辐射；并利用有效哈密顿量研究简并点附近的能带结构，表征所有衍射通道中的拓扑动力学。

Result: 在衍射极限以上的周期性光子结构中证明了具有高阶拓扑荷和二次能带简并的Γ点简并混合BICs的存在。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了衍射极限以上实现简并BICs的新机制，为相关新物理现象与实际应用开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: We demonstrate the existence of at-$Γ$ degenerate hybrid bound-states in the continuum (BICs) with high-order topological charges and quadratic band degeneracy in periodic photonic structures with $C_{6v}$ symmetry above the diffraction limit. The degenerate BICs are realized by combining symmetry protection, which suppresses radiation into zeroth-order diffraction channel, with quadratic band degeneracy and parameter tuning to eliminate radiation into first-order diffraction channels. An effective Hamiltonian is used to investigate the band structure around the degeneracy point, and the topological dynamics in all diffraction channels are characterized. This work reveals a novel mechanism for realizing degenerate BICs above the diffraction limit, opening up opportunities for new physics and applications.

</details>


### [164] [Practical Evaluation of FFT-Based Thickness Extraction for Thick-Film Reflectometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10146)
*Zimu Zhou,Enrique A. Lopez-Guerra,Michael Kwan,Chester Chien*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文对比了快速傅里叶变换（FFT）、线性化反射率零点交叉法（LRZ）和光学模型拟合三种方法在厚膜反射测量（thick-film reflectometry）中的表现，以白光干涉测量（WLI）为独立参考。结果显示FFT虽然重复性极佳（RMSE=0.62 μm），但存在系统性偏差；LRZ则显著提高了精度（RMSE=0.20 μm），研究指出偏差来源于有限测量窗口和非整数条纹周期引起的频谱泄漏（spectral leakage）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速傅里叶变换（Fast Fourier Transform, FFT）因其简单和计算效率高而被广泛用于厚膜反射测量，但其在实际厚膜测量条件下的性能缺乏充分的实验评估，这一空白需要填补。

Method: 在量产晶圆上对标称52 μm介电薄膜进行反射测量，分别应用FFT、线性化反射率零点交叉法（Linearized Reflectance Zero-Crossing, LRZ）和光学模型拟合三种方法处理数据，并以白光干涉测量（White-Light Interferometry, WLI）作为独立参考进行交叉验证；同时辅以仿真和理论分析来探究偏差来源。

Result: FFT虽然展现出优异的重复性，但与WLI相比存在系统性偏差（RMSE=0.62 μm）；LRZ显著提升了测量精度（RMSE=0.20 μm）。仿真和理论分析表明，观察到的系统性偏差与由有限测量窗口和非整数条纹周期引起的频谱泄漏（spectral leakage）一致。

Conclusion: 优异的重复性（repeatability）并不必然意味着高精度（accuracy）；基于零点交叉（zero-crossing）的方法在厚膜厚度计量（thick-film thickness metrology）中具有明显优势。

Abstract: Fast Fourier transform (FFT) is widely used for thick-film reflectometry because of its simplicity and computational efficiency. However, its performance under practical thick-film measurement conditions has received limited experimental evaluation. In this work, FFT, Linearized Reflectance Zero-Crossing (LRZ), and optical model fitting were compared using reflectometry measurements from a nominal 52 μm dielectric film acquired on a production wafer, with White-Light Interferometry (WLI) serving as an independent reference. Although FFT exhibited excellent repeatability, it showed systematic deviation from WLI (RMSE = 0.62 μm), whereas LRZ significantly improved accuracy (RMSE = 0.20 μm). Simulations and theoretical analysis indicate that the observed systematic deviation is consistent with spectral leakage caused by finite measurement windows and non-integer fringe periodicity. These results demonstrate that excellent repeatability does not necessarily imply high accuracy and highlight the advantages of zero-crossing-based approaches for thick-film thickness metrology.

</details>


### [165] [Optical-Memory Transport Imaging: A Transport-History Framework for Finite-Memory Tracers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10820)
*Haichun Liu,Jerker Widengren*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出光学记忆输运（optical-memory transport, OMT）成像框架，利用有限记忆光学示踪剂将上游输运历史编码为可测量的复数空间频率响应，并通过局域传递函数极限实现恒速反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将光学示踪剂视作瞬时局部流动速度的测量工具，但实际具有有限记忆的示踪剂编码的是上游输运历史而非瞬时速度，现有框架无法准确建模这一效应。

Method: 引入OMT成像框架，将有限记忆与内部态弛豫通过记忆核（memory kernels）耦合到输运过程中；在结构化照明下，将这些历史转化为可测的复数空间频率响应；利用局域传递函数极限实现恒定速度反演。

Result: 通过Fisher信息分析建立了依赖于记忆核的信息极限，并提出了有限记忆输运成像的设计原则。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限记忆示踪剂在输运成像中的应用提供了理论基础与设计指导，明确了其信息获取的物理边界。

Abstract: Finite-memory optical tracers encode upstream transport histories rather than instantaneous local flow velocities. We introduce optical-memory transport (OMT) imaging, a framework in which finite memory couples internal-state relaxation to transport through memory kernels. Under structured illumination, these histories are converted into measurable complex spatial-frequency response, whose local transfer-function limit yields constant-velocity inversion. Fisher-information analysis establishes kernel-dependent information limits and design principles for finite-memory transport imaging.

</details>

### [166] [Spin Rotatory Strength as the Equilibrium Observable for Chiral-Induced Spin Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10697)
*Rubén Dar\io Guerrero*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出自旋旋转强度（spin rotatory strength）——一种手性奇数、有限频率的自旋-偶极交叉响应——作为手性诱导自旋选择性（chiral-induced spin selectivity）的平衡可观测量。时间反演对称性迫使静态响应为零，而非零的SU(2) Wilson环通量是独立必要条件。对Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型（N=4,6,8）的精确对角化显示，带隙分子稳健地抑制该响应，而近简并系统在小N下表现出放大效应，其在更大尺寸下的持续性仍待研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性是手性分子系统中一个重要的物理现象，但此前缺乏严格定义的平衡可观测量来定量刻画该效应。作者旨在寻找一个满足对称性约束、能够在平衡态下直接表征该现象的理论工具，以克服静态响应因时间反演对称性而消失的限制。

Method: 本文将自旋旋转强度识别为手性诱导自旋选择性的平衡可观测量，指出时间反演对称性需要有限频率而非静态响应，并论证非零SU(2) Wilson环通量构成该响应的独立必要条件。随后利用Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型在N=4、6、8的尺寸下进行精确对角化（exact diagonalization），系统比较带隙（gapped）与近简并（near-degenerate）两类体系的行为。

Result: 精确对角化结果显示，带隙分子稳健地抑制自旋旋转强度响应；相反，近简并系统在小N尺度下表现出该响应的放大效应，但其在更大系统尺寸下是否持续仍是一个开放问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为手性诱导自旋选择性提供了一种基于对称性约束和Wilson环通量条件的严格平衡态表征方案，同时揭示了体系能带结构对响应幅度的关键调控作用，并为未来针对更大系统尺度的数值验证指明方向。

Abstract: We identify the spin rotatory strength---a chirality-odd, finite-frequency spin--dipole cross response---as the equilibrium observable for chiral-induced spin selectivity. Time-reversal symmetry forces the static response to vanish, while nonzero SU(2) Wilson-loop flux is an independent necessary condition. Exact diagonalization of a Kane--Mele--Hubbard model ($N{=}4,6,8$) reveals that gapped molecules robustly suppress the response, whereas near-degenerate systems show amplification at small $N$ whose persistence at larger sizes remains open.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Ripple Signatures of Majorana Hybridization across a Topological Quantum Quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10052)
*Xin-Xin Wang,Jin-Xin Li,Ya-Wen Tang,Lu Qin,Zun-Lue Zhu,Wu-Ming Liu,Liang-Liang Wang,Xing-Dong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用自洽含时Bogoliubov-de Gennes理论，研究淬火（quench）费米子超流体中Majorana边界模的命运，发现拓扑区间淬火后Majorana模存活但发生相干边界振荡，拓扑改变淬火后出现由Majorana边界态相干杂化和干涉引起的密度波纹结构，确立了非平衡边界动力学作为探测Majorana物理的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑（topology）与非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）的交叉已成为前沿领域，量子系统可展现超越平衡态的独特动力学现象，尤其关注跨越拓扑相变的淬火动力学，因其可能揭示潜在Majorana零能态（Majorana zero-energy states）的信息。

Method: 采用自洽含时Bogoliubov-de Gennes（time-dependent Bogoliubov-de Gennes）理论，对费米子超流体（fermionic superfluid）进行量子淬火（quantum quench）动力学模拟，分析Majorana边界模在淬火过程中的演化行为。

Result: 在拓扑区间内的量子淬火中，Majorana边界模得以存活，但因其波函数的非绝热变形而产生相干边界振荡；在突然改变拓扑性质的淬火后，淬后密度分布（post-quench density distribution）中出现显著的波纹图样（ripple pattern），该波纹源自初始分离的Majorana边界态之间的相干杂化与干涉。

Conclusion: 非平衡边界动力学可作为探测Majorana物理（Majorana physics）的新方法，与传统的平衡测量手段互为补充。

Abstract: The crossover between topology and nonequilibrium dynamics has emerged as a rich frontier, in which quantum systems can exhibit unique dynamical phenomena that lie beyond the reach of equilibrium. Of particular interest are quench dynamics across topological phase, as it may reveal the information about the underlying Majorana zero-energy states. Here, we investigate the fate of Majorana boundary modes in a quenched fermionic superfluid using self-consistent time-dependent Bogoliubov-de Gennes theory. For quantum quenches within the topological regime, Majorana boundary modes survive but undergo coherent boundary oscillations arising from the nonadiabatic deformation of their wave functions. Furthermore, a pronounced ripple pattern appears in the post-quench density distribution following a sudden topology-changing quench. Here we identify that these ripple structures originates from the coherent hybridization and interference of the initially separated Majorana boundary states. Our findings establish nonequilibrium boundary dynamics as a new approach for probing Majorana physics, which is complementary to conventional equilibrium measurements.

</details>


### [168] [Tunable Memory Effect in Dissipative Strongly Correlated Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10534)
*Haowei Li,Yu Chen,Hui Zhai*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个一般性理论框架，用于计算强关联量子多体系统在非马尔可夫耗散下的可观测响应，预言处于量子临界态的强关联系统其短时动力学服从普适的t^{2η}标度律，并揭示超越记忆时间后的交叉行为，且提出可用超冷原子实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用量子多体系统在非马尔可夫耗散（non-Markovian dissipation）下的行为极具挑战性，因为强关联效应与记忆效应相互交织，缺乏统一的理论框架来描述系统可观测量对耗散的响应。

Method: 作者发展了一个一般性理论框架，用于计算系统可观测量对耗散的响应；该框架在短时捕获记忆效应，在长时间恢复马尔可夫极限（Markovian limit）。此外，作者提出了利用超冷原子（ultracold atoms）实现具有可调记忆时间的非马尔可夫热库的具体物理方案。

Result: 预言对于临界指数为η的强关联量子临界态，系统可观测量的短时动力学始终遵循t^{2η}标度律；该结果源于强关联与记忆效应的相互作用，不依赖于系统的微观哈密顿量，具有普适性。进一步揭示了在记忆时间尺度之外，该标度律交叉演变为t^{2η−1}或线性依赖t的行为。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在非马尔可夫耗散条件下理解强关联系统响应的通用工具，所预言的普适标度律可通过当前超冷原子实验直接验证。

Abstract: Strongly interacting quantum many-body systems subjected to non-Markovian dissipation pose a formidable challenge due to the interplay between strong correlation effects and memory effects. In this Letter, we develop a general theoretical framework to compute how a system observable responds to dissipation, which captures memory effects at short times and recovers the Markovian limit at longer times. Using this framework, we predict that, for a strongly correlated quantum critical state with critical exponent $η$, the short-time dynamics of a system observable always obeys a $t^{2η}$ scaling law. This emerges as a universal result from the interplay between strong correlation and memory effects, independent of the microscopic Hamiltonian of the system. We further reveal a crossover behavior of this scaling law to either $t^{2η-1}$ or linear-in-$t$ behavior beyond the memory time scale. We propose a concrete physical realization of a non-Markovian bath with tunable memory time using ultracold atoms, where our predictions can be straightforwardly verified in current experiments.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamical Crossover in Landau$-$Zener Tunneling in Dissipative Rydberg Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10639)
*Suvechha Indu,Raka Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了具有时间依赖失谐的Rabi耦合耗散Rydberg格点系统中的激发动力学，通过平均场Lindblad主方程和非厄米哈密顿量框架分析了Landau-Zener跃迁行为，揭示了跨越临界有效封锁强度时的动力学交叉以及Rydberg相互作用对激发寿命的不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Rydberg格点系统中激发动力学及其与Landau-Zener跃迁的关联，特别是耗散效应和时间依赖失谐对系统行为的影响。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(i) 平均场近似下的Lindblad主方程(Lindblad master equation)，用于捕捉Rydberg激发剖面的反铁磁序；(ii) 有效非厄米哈密顿量(effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian)框架，用于直接分析复能量谱及其避免交叉(avoided crossings)所主导的Landau-Zener动力学。

Result: 发现避免交叉附近存在子格（sublattice）布居失衡消失的机制，使两个子格具有相同的Landau-Zener概率；超过临界有效封锁强度(blockade strength)后出现动力学交叉，子格布居失衡持续存在导致子格依赖的Landau-Zener概率。弱耗散和强Rabi耦合下，Rydberg相互作用延长Landau-Zener诱导激发的寿命；弱Rabi耦合下，Rydberg封锁抑制激发并降低Landau-Zener跃迁概率。

Conclusion: Rydberg相互作用、耗散和Rabi耦合强度之间的竞争决定了系统中Landau-Zener动力学的不同机制，为调控Rydberg格点系统中的激发行为提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the excitation dynamics of a Rabi-coupled dissipative Rydberg lattice with a time-dependent detuning. The system is analyzed using (i) a Lindblad master equation within a mean-field approximation and (ii) an effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian framework. While the mean-field approach captures the emergence of an antiferromagnetic order in the Rydberg excitation profile, the non-Hermitian description provides direct insight into the complex energy spectrum and its avoided crossings, which govern the Landau$-$Zener dynamics. We identify a regime in which the sublattice population imbalance vanishes near the avoided crossing, resulting in identical Landau$-$Zener probabilities on the two sublattices. Beyond a critical effective blockade strength there is a dynamical crossover to another regime in which the sublattice population imbalance persists through the avoided crossing, giving rise to sublattice-dependent Landau$-$Zener probabilities. Furthermore, Rydberg interactions prolong the lifetime of Landau$-$Zener-induced excitations in the presence of weak dissipation and strong Rabi coupling. In contrast, for weak Rabi coupling, the Rydberg blockade inhibits excitation and suppresses the Landau$-$Zener transition probability.

</details>


### [170] [Two-component ultracold Bose gases with spin-orbit coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10722)
*G. I. Martone,S. Stringari*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文为讲义性质的综述，系统介绍具有Raman诱导自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate），涵盖其丰富的相位图、量子相变、动力学性质以及超流和转动特性，特别关注条纹相（stripe phase）的超固体（supersolid）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于单粒子色散呈现双极小（double-minimum）结构与两体相互作用之间的相互影响，Raman诱导自旋轨道耦合的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体拥有复杂的相图，理解这些系统的量子相及其动力学行为具有重要的物理意义。

Method: 本文采用理论讲义的形式，通过分析单粒子色散与两体相互作用的协同效应来构建相图；利用Bogoliubov谱（Bogoliubov spectrum）在无限体系中的行为以及受限阱中离散化集体模式频率来研究凝聚体动力学，并通过对动力学特征的考察揭示条纹相的超固体性质。

Result: 该体系存在三种不同的量子相：条纹相、平面波相（plane-wave phase）和单极小相（single-minimum phase），各相具有不同的破缺对称性；自旋轨道耦合显著影响凝聚体动力学，尤其在相变附近表现明显；超流和转动性质也因运动与自旋自由度之间的耦合而深刻改变；条纹相的超固体特征可通过其动力学特性清晰揭示。

Conclusion: Raman诱导自旋轨道耦合的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体展现出丰富的多相结构与复杂的动力学行为，条纹相作为超固体候选体系具有重要的研究价值，其超固体性质可通过动态响应加以明确识别。

Abstract: These lecture notes provide an introduction to Bose-Einstein condensates with Raman-induced spin-orbit coupling. Owing to the interplay between the peculiar single-particle dispersion, featuring a double-minimum structure, and the two-body interaction, these systems possess a complex phase diagram. Three different quantum phases can be observed, i.e., a stripe, a plane-wave, and a single-minimum phase, each characterized by different broken symmetries. The condensate dynamics is also significantly affected by the spin-orbit coupling, as revealed by the behavior of the Bogoliubov spectrum in infinite systems and the behavior of the discretized collective mode frequencies in trapped configurations, especially close to the phase transitions. In turn, the superfluid and rotational properties are also deeply modified by the coupling between the motional and spin degree of freedom. Finally, a special attention is devoted to the stripe phase and its supersolid character, which can be clearly revealed by the study of its dynamic features.

</details>


### [171] [Composite-Boson Ansatz for Fractional Quantum Hall Manifolds of Lattice Bosons at Generic Fillings $ν<1/2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10747)
*Umut Can Turhan,A. Levent Subaşı,Rifat Onur Umucal\ilar*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文针对低通量Hofstadter–Bose–Hubbard模型中有限尺寸晶格分裂Laughlin拟空穴流形，提出了一种复合玻色子（composite boson）尝试波函数方案，通过为每个玻色子附着两个涡旋构造多体尝试基，数值验证其能谱与陈数（Chern number）性质，建立了次半填充分数量子霍尔流形的微观复合玻色子框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hofstadter系统提供了实现分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）物理的晶格途径，但其低能流形往往缺乏简单的波函数描述，这限制了对该系统的深入理解与数值分析。

Method: 在环面（torus）上一般填充ν<1/2的低通量Hofstadter–Bose–Hubbard模型中，为每个玻色子附着两个涡旋以构造约化通量轨道（reduced-flux orbitals），进而构建多体尝试基；利用平移分辨秩（translation-resolved rank）复现低能流形维度，并借助最低能带投影对角化与变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）方法分别处理小尺寸与大尺寸系统。

Result: 所构造的复合玻色子尝试基的平移分辨秩能重现预期的低能流形维度，并提供了对既定拟空穴计数的复合玻色子解释；小尺寸下最低能带投影对角化结果与精确投影谱高度一致，变分蒙特卡洛将秩与能量分析推广至更大系统；多体陈数计算显示局部加通量激发呈现类拟空穴行为，其分数密度缺失与无阿哈罗诺夫–玻姆（Aharonov–Bohm）交换相位均追踪有效填充数。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个微观复合玻色子框架，用以系统组织宽泛家族的次半填充分数量子霍尔晶格玻色子流形。

Abstract: Hofstadter systems provide a lattice route to fractional quantum Hall physics, but their low-energy manifolds often lack simple wave-function descriptions. Here we present a composite-boson ansatz for a broad family of finite-size lattice-split Laughlin-quasihole manifolds in the low-flux Hofstadter--Bose--Hubbard model on a torus at generic fillings \(ν<1/2\). Attaching two vortices to each boson yields reduced-flux orbitals from which we construct a many-body trial basis. Its translation-resolved rank reproduces the expected low-energy-manifold dimension and provides a composite-boson interpretation of the established quasihole counting previously inferred from generalized exclusion rules and thin-torus arguments. For smaller systems, diagonalization within the lowest-band-projected ansatz span closely reproduces the exact projected spectrum, while variational Monte Carlo extends the rank and energetic analysis to larger systems for which explicit subspace construction becomes costly. To probe the fractional quantum Hall character of these manifolds, we calculate their many-body Chern-numbers and show that localized added-flux excitations exhibit quasihole-like behavior, with fractional density depletion and an Aharonov--Bohm-free braiding phase that both track the effective filling. Together, these results establish a microscopic composite-boson framework for organizing a broad family of sub-half-filled fractional quantum Hall manifolds of lattice bosons.

</details>

### [172] [Pure matrix states on block Toeplitz matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10701)
*Tirthankar Bhattacharyya,Ritul Duhan*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了块Toeplitz算子系统（block Toeplitz operator system）$\mathcal{T}_{n,m}$到矩阵代数的纯幺正完全正（pure unital completely positive, ucp）映射，刻画了其纯度等价条件，并利用点分裂引理证明此类映射在马氏Monge-Kantorovich度量下稠密，进而推广了单位圆上正Borel测度的逼近结果至矩阵值完全正映射的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逼近定理表明单位圆上的正正则Borel测度可由有限维Toeplitz结构产生的对象近似，本文旨在将该结果推广到矩阵值的完全正映射（completely positive maps）情形。这一推广需要精细刻画块Toeplitz算子系统上的纯ucp映射，并为后续建立Monge-Kantorovich度量下的稠密性和Hausdorff距离收敛奠定基础。

Method: 首先利用Stinespring等距（Stinespring isometry）$V=(V_1,\dots,V_n)^t$及其关联的矩阵值多项式$Q_V(z)$来刻画$\mathcal{T}_{n,m}$上的pure ucp映射，证明纯度等价于存在唯一到$M_{mn}(\mathbb{C})$的纯ucp扩张，亦等价于$Q_V$具有次数$n-1$且所有根在单位圆上。随后分析$\mathcal{Y}_m$（从$C(\mathbb{T},M_m(\mathbb{C}))$到$M_m(\mathbb{C})$的所有ucp映射构成的紧凸集）的极端点，并结合点分裂引理（point-splitting lemma），在矩阵型Monge-Kantorovich度量$ρ$下证明诱导映射的稠密性，最终导出Hausdorff距离收敛。

Result: 证明了$\varphi:\mathcal{T}_{n,m}\to M_m(\mathbb{C})$是纯ucp映射当且仅当其有唯一纯ucp扩张到$M_{mn}(\mathbb{C})$当且仅当$Q_V$度数为$n-1$且全部根在单位圆上。此外，诱导映射$Φ_{Q_V}$在$\mathcal{Y}_m$中关于度量$ρ$稠密，从而Hausdorff距离$d_H(\mathcal{B}_{mn},\mathcal{Y}_m)\to 0$（$n\to\infty$），其中$\mathcal{B}_{mn}$是归一化$Φ_{Q_V}$的全体。

Conclusion: 该工作将单位圆上正Borel测度通过Toeplitz结构逼近的经典结果推广到矩阵值完全正映射的框架，建立了一套基于纯ucp映射刻画与点分裂技巧的完备逼近理论。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{T}_{n,m} = \mathcal T_n(M_m(\mathbb{C}))$ denote the operator system of all block Toeplitz matrices
  $ T = (( T_{i-j}))_{i,j=1}^n$ with entries $T_k \in M_m(\mathbb C) $
  % T_{k} = \left[ t^{(k)}_{p-q} \right]_{p,q=1}^m.
  \[
  T
  =
  \begin{pmatrix}
  T_0 & T_{-1} & \cdots & T_{-(n-1)}
  T_1 & T_0 & \cdots & T_{-(n-2)}
  \vdots & \vdots & \ddots & \vdots
  T_{n-1} & T_{n-2}& \cdots & T_0
  \end{pmatrix}
  \in M_{mn}(\mathbb{C}).
  \]
  We characterize all pure unital completely positive ({\it{ucp}}) maps from $\mathcal{T}_{n,m}$ to $M_m(\mathbb{C})$.
  Working through the Stinespring isometry $V = (V_1, \ldots, V_n)^t \colon \mathbb{C}^m \to \mathbb{C}^{mn}$ and the matrix-valued polynomial $Q_V(z) = \sum_{i=1}^n z^{n-i} V_i$, we prove that $\varphi$ is pure if and only if it admits a unique pure \ucp extension to $M_{mn}(\mathbb{C})$ if and only if $Q_V$ has degree $n-1$ with all its roots on the unit circle $\T$.
  Every such pure $\varphi$ induces a \ucp map $Φ_{Q_V}$ on $C(\mathbb{T}, M_m(\mathbb{C}))$ given by \[
  Φ_{Q_V}(f) = \int_{\mathbb{T}} Q_V(z)^* f(z) Q_V(z)\, dz. \] Let $\mathcal{Y}_m$ be the compact convex set of all \ucp maps from $C(\mathbb{T}, M_m(\mathbb{C}))$ to $M_m(\mathbb{C})$. Endowing $\mathcal{Y}_m$ with the matricial Monge-Kantorovich metric $ρ$, via an analysis of the extreme points of $\mathcal{Y}_m$ together with a point-splitting lemma, we show that the induced maps as above are $ρ$-dense in $\mathcal{Y}_m$. Consequently, if $\Bmn$ denotes the set of normalized $Φ_{Q_V}$ where $Q_V$ is as above, then the Hausdorff distance $d_H(\Bmn, \mathcal{Y}_m) \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$, extending known results of approximation of positive regular Borel measures on the unit circle to the setting of matrix-valued completely positive maps.

</details>

### [173] [Anisotropic Flow in Ultra-Central Pb$-$Pb Collisions at $\sqrt{\mathrm{s_{NN}}}=5.36$ TeV with ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11199)
*Iris Likmeta*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了ALICE实验中超中心（ultra-central）Pb-Pb对撞中v₃{2}/v₂{2}比值的测量结果，并将其与最新相对论流体力学（relativistic hydrodynamic）模型计算进行对比，旨在检验208Pb核八极形变（octupole deformation）作为解决UCC之谜（UCC puzzle）的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子对撞中各向异性流（anisotropic flow）测量对初始态空间分布及夸克胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）输运性质敏感，但现有流体力学模型在超中心对撞中无法完全复现实验数据中流谐波（flow harmonics）的预期层级关系，形成UCC之谜。此前提出的208Pb核八极形变作为改进方案需要通过更高精度的实验测量加以验证。

Method: 利用ALICE Run 3探测器测量了质心能量√sNN=5.36 TeV的超中心Pb-Pb对撞中的v₃{2}/v₂{2}比值，并将测量结果与最新的相对论流体力学模型计算结果进行系统比较。

Result: 给出了超中心Pb-Pb对撞中v₃{2}/v₂{2}比值的实验测量数据，并与流体力学模型预测进行了对比，用以评估208Pb核八极形变效应对改进模型描述能力的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过对比实验测量与模型计算，为检验208Pb核八极形变假设能否解决UCC之谜提供了最新的实验约束。

Abstract: Anisotropic flow measurements in heavy-ion collisions are sensitive to the spatial distribution of the initial state, and quark-gluon plasma transport properties such as the shear viscosity to entropy density ratio $(η/s)$. State-of-the-art relativistic hydrodynamic models successfully describe such flow measurements over a wide centrality range. However, the hydrodynamic description of anisotropic flow deviates from the experimental data in ultra-central collisions (UCC), where the average geometric anisotropy of the system becomes small and fluctuations dominate the initial-state geometry. This discrepancy constitutes the UCC puzzle, as the expected hierarchy of flow harmonics is not fully reproduced by current modeling approaches. Probing towards ultra-central collisions, effects on flow fluctuations due to the initial spatial anisotropies are suppressed. The measured flow can be explained by quantum fluctuations on the energy distribution of $^{208}$Pb nuclei. An octupole deformation of the $^{208}$Pb nuclei has been proposed as a remedy to improve the modeling of the measured $v_{3} \{2\} / v_{2} \{2\}$ ratio. In this contribution, we present measurements of $v_{3}\{2\}/v_{2}\{2\}$ ratio in ultra-central Pb\textendash Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}} = 5.36$ TeV with ALICE Run 3 detector and compare them with recent hydrodynamic model calculations.

</details>

### [174] [Observation of node-dependent Rydberg molecular bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09950)
*Qing Li,Shi-Yao Shao,Jun Zhang,Han-Chao Chen,Li-Hua Zhang,Bang Liu,Guang-Can Guo,Dong-Sheng Ding,Bao-Sen Shi*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了对Rb(nS)-Rb(5S)里德堡分子（Rydberg molecules）光谱中节点依赖的p波分子信号的实验观测，发现主量子数n的变化直接反映里德堡电子波函数移动节点结构引起的分子束缚能位移，并通过分辨相邻节点相关的两个p波束缚态实现了亚纳米级空间分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长程里德堡分子由高激发里德堡原子与基态原子间的相互作用形成，为探索从纳米到微米尺度的量子现象及奇异少体力相互作用提供了独特平台，而s波与p波散射通道中的形成机制和物理性质存在显著差异，需要实验加以揭示。

Method: 通过实验测量Rb(nS)-Rb(5S)里德堡分子光谱，系统研究主量子数n变化对p波分子束缚态信号的影响，并结合nS电子波函数的局部梯度与能量依赖的p波散射长度之间的协同效应来解释观测到的节点依赖现象。

Result: 实验观测到节点依赖的p波分子信号，主量子数n的变化直接揭示了里德堡电子波函数移动节点结构引起的分子束缚能位移；同时成功分辨出与相邻节点相关的两个p波束缚态，展示了亚纳米级空间分辨率。

Conclusion: 主量子数可作为节点选择性分子束缚态的开关，揭示了更深刻的量子调控机制；所报道的方法为电子-原子散射提供了灵敏的光谱探测手段。

Abstract: Ultralong-range Rydberg molecules, formed by the interaction between a highly excited Rydberg atom and a ground-state atom, provide a unique platform for exploring quantum phenomena spanning nanometer-to-micrometer distances as well as exotic few-body interactions. The formation mechanisms and resultant physical properties differ markedly between s-wave and p-wave scattering channels. Here we report the experimental observation of node-dependent p-wave molecular signals in Rb(nS)-Rb(5S) Rydberg molecular spectra, where variations in the principal quantum number n directly reveal the shift of molecular binding energies induced by the moving nodal structure of the Rydberg electron wavefunction. This node-dependence is attributed to a cooperative effect between the local gradient of the nS-electron wavefunction and the energydependent p-wave scattering length. In addition, resolving two p-wave bound states associated with adjacent nodes highlights the remarkable sub-nanometer spatial resolution achieved in our experiment. Our findings reveal a more profound quantum control mechanism, wherein the principal quantum number acts as a switch for nodal-selective molecular bound states, and the reported method provides a sensitive spectroscopic probe of electron-atom scattering.

</details>
