### [175] [When Complementary Measurements Count the Same Classical Bit Twice: Counterexamples to CQC, ECQC, and Complementarity-Based Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03828)
*Jinbo Wang,Qihang Wang,Kun Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两支路经典零测试（two-branch classical null test）架构，证明了互不偏置测量（mutually unbiased measurements）在维度 d≥3 时可产生冗余信息，从而推翻补余量子关联（CQC）猜想；并在 d=7 时推翻其完全基扩展（ECQC）版本，揭示了不同测量设置读取的信息必须非冗余这一必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互不偏置测量通常被认为能暴露量子态（quantum state）的不同独立侧面，即在一个基下经典的相关性应在互补基下消失。然而在高维情形下，不同设置中恢复的关联未必代表不同信息，这一直觉漏洞尚未被系统检验，作者旨在通过经典构造揭示该漏洞。

Method: 作者构造了一个两支路经典零测试架构：在测量设置选择之前，一个共享比特从两个正交乘积制备中选择其一，生成秩二的经典-经典态（rank-two classical–classical state），随后候选协议照常运行。通过对该经典态的输出模式进行检验，分别设计了针对CQC猜想和其完全基扩展（ECQC）猜想的反例态。

Result: 该两支路经典架构在所有维度 d≥3 下推翻了补余量子关联猜想；另一不同的秩二经典-经典态在 d=7 时推翻了其完全基扩展版本，且超出量沿素数维度无界增长。该架构在 qutrit（三维量子系统）情形下还对所提出的量子关联度量、单边半设备无关（one-sided semi-device-independent）steering判据产生经典假阳性，并驳斥了一个条件概率猜想。

Conclusion: 上述失败揭示了缺失的关键要求：在不同测量设置中读取的信息必须是非冗余的（nonredundant）。该低开销架构还可作为实验和应用中多基评分被赋予量子意义之前的校准测试工具。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased measurements are commonly expected to expose independent facets of a quantum state: a correlation that is classical in one basis should disappear in a complementary basis. In higher dimensions, however, this intuition becomes particularly subtle because correlations recovered in different settings need not represent different information. To expose this loophole, we propose a two-branch classical null test: before the setting is chosen, a shared bit selects one of two orthogonal product preparations, producing a rank-two classical--classical state, and the candidate protocol then runs unchanged. Different settings can read the same bit through different outcome patterns. This two-branch classical architecture disproves the complementary-quantum correlation (CQC) conjecture in every dimension $d\geq3$. A distinct rank-two classical--classical state disproves its complete-basis extension (ECQC) at $d=7$, with an overrun that grows without bound along prime dimensions. Its qutrit CQC instance also gives classical false positives for a proposed quantum-correlation measure and a proposed one-sided semi-device-independent steering criterion, and refutes a conditional-probability conjecture. The failures identify the missing requirement: information read in different settings must be nonredundant. In experiments and applications, the same low-overhead architecture can serve as a calibration test before a multibasis score is assigned quantum meaning.

</details>


### [176] [Complementary Quantum Correlations Are Universal for Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04916)
*Jinbo Wang,Qihang Wang,Kun Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明对于任意双量子比特（two-qubit）态，两个互补局域基测量得到的互信息之和不会重复计数经典关联，其总和受限于测量前的量子互信息。这一结论使得仅通过两组互补测量的关联表即可获得无需量子态层析（tomography-free）的总关联下界，并可进一步催化量子纠缠蒸馏率与量子信道容量的下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从量子系统提取总关联通常需要重构其完整的量子态，但许多实验仅能访问少量测量设置。一种可能的捷径是累加互补测量获得的互信息，然而在维度大于二时，这一做法可能重复计数同一份经典关联，因此需要明确在何种维度该方案是可靠的。

Method: 本文严格证明了双量子比特系统免受上述重复计数问题影响。证明过程将这一保护机制归因于Bloch球上的二元熵曲率（binary-entropy curvature），结合了量子比特的信息排除权衡（information-exclusion tradeoff）与局域退相干（local dephasing）下的数据处理不等式。

Result: 对于每一双量子比特态，在两个互补局域基中观测到的关联之总和受测量前量子的互信息界定。由此，两组关联表即可提供无需层析的总关联下界；得分高于1比特时还能定量认证单向纠缠蒸馏率，应用于量子信道（qubit channel）的Choi态时可给出其量子容量（quantum capacity）的下界。

Conclusion: 该定理同时揭示了互补性的操作性用途，并确定了其关联核算有效的可信二维（two-dimensional）设置。

Abstract: Extracting total correlations from a quantum system usually requires reconstructing its state, whereas many experiments access only a few measurement settings. A possible shortcut is to add the mutual informations obtained from complementary measurements; in dimensions above two, however, this procedure can count the same classical correlation twice. We establish that qubits are protected from such overcounting. For every two-qubit state, the correlations observed in two complementary local bases are bounded by the premeasurement quantum mutual information. The proof traces this protection to binary-entropy curvature on the Bloch ball and combines a qubit information-exclusion tradeoff with data processing under local dephasing. Consequently, two correlation tables give a tomography-free lower bound on total correlation. A score above one bit also certifies a quantitative one-way entanglement-distillation rate; when applied to the Choi state of a qubit channel, the same data lower bound its quantum capacity. The theorem therefore identifies both an operational use of complementarity and the trusted two-dimensional setting in which its correlation accounting is valid.

</details>


### [177] [Global Non-Identifiability of Fubini-Study Geometry from Complete One-Period Endpoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10051)
*Rashid Ahmad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个针对周期驱动有限维量子系统（periodically driven finite-dimensional quantum systems）的全局最坏情形不可辨识性定理（non-identifiability theorem），证明即使拥有按起始时刻完全解析的单周期端点数据，也无法一般地重建固定初始态的周期平均Fubini-Study度量（period-averaged Fubini-Study metric）这一周期内几何量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图解决从单周期端点数据中重建周期内量子动力学几何信息的基本限制问题，揭示完整端点数据与周期内动力学之间存在的信息缺口。

Method: 通过在无限制光滑周期哈密顿类上严格刻画不可辨识性的精确结构：起点索引的端点数据决定单值化矩阵（monodromy）的共轭路径但非其特定酉提升（unitary lift）；观察纤维由取值于monodromy逐点中心化子的光滑参数依赖基环的右陪集构成；并以一个实解析双能级系综提供显式见证。

Result: Fubini-Study泛函在该纤维作用下不具不变性，因此不透过端点观察映射分解；一个交换型单生成元哈密顿族即可产生相同端点数据但不同的周期平均Fubini-Study几何，且该不可辨识性在任何哈密顿参数导数的一致界下依然成立。

Conclusion: 该结果为确定性的全局声明而非一般不可辨识性或实验不可能性主张，精确定位了信息缺失所在：缺失的是被观测monodromy路径的酉提升，而非普通的标量相位自由度。

Abstract: We establish a global, worst-case non-identifiability theorem for periodically driven finite-dimensional quantum systems. On the unrestricted smooth periodic Hamiltonian class, we show that the period-averaged Fubini-Study metric component of a fixed initial state cannot, in general, be reconstructed from exact one-period propagators indexed by every starting time and external parameter. Thus, even complete starting-time-resolved endpoint data are insufficient to determine this intra-period geometric quantity. The obstruction is characterized exactly. The starting-time-indexed endpoint data determine the conjugation path of the monodromy, but not its particular unitary lift. On each fixed-monodromy slice, the observational fibres are precisely the right orbits generated by smooth parameter-dependent based loops taking values in the pointwise centralizer of the monodromy. The Fubini-Study functional is not invariant under this fibre action and therefore does not factor through the endpoint observation map. An explicit real-analytic two-level witness demonstrates the obstruction within a commuting, one-generator Hamiltonian family, so neither non-Abelian time ordering nor Floquet-logarithm ambiguity is required. A continuous family of Hamiltonians produces identical starting-time-indexed one-period endpoint data while yielding different, and on the unrestricted class arbitrarily separated, period-averaged Fubini-Study geometry. Non-identifiability persists under any prescribed uniform bound on the parameter derivative of the Hamiltonian. The result is a deterministic global statement, not a claim of generic non-identifiability or experimental impossibility. It identifies a precise information gap between complete one-period endpoint data and full intra-period dynamics: the missing information is the unitary lift of the observed monodromy path rather than ordinary scalar phase freedom.

</details>


### [178] [Helium-like ions $(Z,e,e)$ in Lagrange Mesh method, interpolating the highly-accurate energy spectra vs. Z](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10695)
*H Olivares-Pilon,JC Lopez-Vieyra,AV Turbiner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种研究非相对论量子力学中氦类（Helium-like）原子离子的替代方法：一是基于拉格朗日网格法（Lagrange-mesh method）的高精度数值方法；二是针对能量随核电荷数 Z 变化的高精度少参数插值公式。该公式通过匹配大 Z 时的 1/Z 展开与第二临界电荷 Z_B 附近的 Puiseux 展开，对任意物理相关的 Z 值可实现 10-14 位有效数字的能量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氦类原子离子的精确能谱计算在量子力学中具有重要意义，但现有方法在精度、效率和通用性方面受限；需要发展既能覆盖任意核电荷数 Z 且具有极高精度（高达 10-14 位有效数字）的计算方案。

Method: 提出两条途径：(I) 基于拉格朗日网格法的数值方法，在单处理器模式下以较少 CPU 时间即可对任意核电荷数 Z 获得 14-15 位有效数字的能谱精度；(II) 构建具有一般性的少参数插值公式，通过匹配大 Z 时的 1/Z 展开（1/Z-expansion）与第二临界电荷 Z_B 附近的 Puiseux 展开（含整数和半整数幂次）来实现能量与 Z 的关系描述，公式参数可针对氦类序列任意激发态进行拟合。

Result: 对于前两个自旋单态（1^1S、2^1S）和前两个自旋三重态（2^3S、3^3S），该带九个自由参数的插值公式在任意物理相关的核电荷数 Z 下可达 10-14 位有效数字的能量精度，在大 Z 极限下具有绝对精度。本文还获得多项此前未曾报道的新结果。

Conclusion: 所述两种方法互补：数值方法提供极高精度的基准数据，插值公式则以紧凑的少参数形式对全部氦类序列的任意激发态能谱提供高精度解析描述，且该方法具有一般性，可推广至其他量子体系。

Abstract: Two alternative approaches for studying Helium-like atomic ions in non-relativistic quantum mechanics are proposed: (I) a numerical approach, based on the Lagrange-mesh method which can easily reach up to 14-15 significant digits in the energy spectrum for any nuclear charge $Z$ with modest CPU time in single processor mode and (II) a highly-accurate, few-parametric interpolation formula for the energies {\it vs.} $Z$. The interpolation formula of general nature is proposed, it can be applied to the energies of any excited state of the helium-like sequence. It is based on matching the $1/Z$-expansion at large $Z$ and the Puiseux expansion with integer and half-integer powers around the so-called second critical charge $Z_B$, introduced by F and D Stillinger (1969, 1974), confirmed by the present authors in 2019 for the ground state $1^1 S$, then revisited here, and extended to the excited states in the present work. For example for the first two spin-singlet $1^1 S$, $2^1 S$ and the first two spin-triplet $2^3 S$, $3^3 S$ states this interpolation formula with nine free parameters can reach an accuracy of 10-14 significant digits (s.d.) in the energies for any physically-relevant nuclear charge $Z$, giving absolute accuracy at large $Z$. Many results are obtained for the first time.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum steering is equivalent to state-preserving conditional expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10783)
*Lauritz van Luijk,Amine Marrakchi,Tobias Osborne,Alexander Stottmeister,Henrik Wilming*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无穷自由度系统中量子信息性质在Haag对偶性（Haag duality）失效时的表现，证明量子导引（quantum steering）与子因子理论（subfactor theory）之间存在直接联系：对于纯全局态，由B上的测量引导A上边缘态的任意系综分解（ensemble decomposition）等价于从交换子$B'$到$A$存在保态条件期望（conditional expectation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有无穷多自由度的系统中，量子信息论的基本结果可能失效，例如纯化的唯一性。已有研究表明，Haag对偶性失效（即交换子$B'$严格大于$A$）时，纯化不再由酉算子相关联。这自然引发一个问题：哪些基本的纠缠性质在这种设定下仍然成立。

Method: 本文在一般Jordan代数（Jordan algebras）框架下证明：幺正正映射（unital positive maps）存在保态左逆，当且仅当系综分解可以被提升（lifted）。对于包含关系$A\hookrightarrow B'$，左逆恰好就是条件期望，由此得到量子导引与条件期望存在性之间的等价刻画。

Result: 证明了对于纯全局态，从$B$上的测量引导$A$上边缘态的任意系综分解的能力，等价于存在从$B'$到$A$的保态条件期望。关键观察是导引等价于系综分解从$A$到$B'$的存在性扩展。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了量子导引与子因子理论之间的直接联系，表明即使在Haag对偶性失效的无穷自由度系统中，量子导引这一基本纠缠性质仍可得到清晰刻画，并通过条件期望的存在性加以判定。

Abstract: In systems with infinitely many degrees of freedom, fundamental results from quantum information theory can fail. An important example is the uniqueness of purifications: Even when two subsystems, described by commuting von Neumann algebras $A$ and $B$, are tomographically complete, purifications of a state on $A$ need not be related by unitaries in $B$. It was recently shown that this occurs precisely when Haag duality fails, i.e., when the commutant $B'$ is strictly larger than $A$. This raises the question of which fundamental entanglement properties survive in such a setting. We show that, for a pure global state, the ability to steer any ensemble decomposition of the marginal state on $A$ by measurements on $B$ is equivalent to the existence of a state-preserving conditional expectation from $B'$ onto $A$. This establishes a direct connection between quantum steering and subfactor theory. The key observation is that steering is equivalent to the existence of extensions of ensemble decompositions from $A$ to $B'$. Working with general Jordan algebras, we prove that unital positive maps have state-preserving left inverses if and only if ensemble decompositions can be lifted. For the inclusion $A\hookrightarrow B'$, a left inverse is precisely a conditional expectation, yielding the characterization above.

</details>


### [180] [A Sharp Local-Question Threshold for GHZ-Equatorial Completeness in Four-Player XOR Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11139)
*Ziao Tang,Chengkai Zhu,Ge Bai,Xin Wang,Ranyiliu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了四玩家二元异或（XOR）游戏实现完美GHZ赤道策略所需的最少活跃问题数。证明当每位玩家至多三个活跃问题时，所有交换子算符值为一的四玩家XOR游戏都允许完美GHZ赤道策略；反之构造了一个每位玩家四个活跃问题的均匀八子句游戏，其交换子算符值为一但相位方程不一致，从而确定四为最少的局部问题阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信息理论中，二元异或游戏与Greenberger–Horne–Zeilinger（GHZ）态及交换子算符值之间关系需被精确刻画。作者希望确定在何种局部问题数量下，四玩家XOR游戏的交换子算符值一不再能保证GHZ赤道实现的存在性，从而建立严格的阈值界限。

Method: 正面结果通过将每个整数关联障碍（integral incidence obstruction）提升为有序非交换反驳（ordered noncommutative refutation），利用原始回路（primitive circuits）、森林匹配（forest matchings）以及三元汉明几何（ternary Hamming geometry）实现。反面分离游戏的构造则利用Klein四元群关联关系阻碍相位系统，并通过偶子群范式（even-subgroup normal form）以及一阶和二阶Magnus系数排除任意长度的反驳。

Result: 证明所有每个玩家至多三个活跃问题的四玩家XOR游戏（交换子算符值为一）均具有完美的GHZ赤道策略；同时构造了一个每位玩家四个活跃问题的均匀八子句游戏，其交换子算符值为一但相位方程不一致，表明四为最少的局部问题阈值。

Conclusion: 四玩家二元XOR游戏在交换子算符值一条件下实现完美GHZ赤道策略的局部问题阈值恰为四（每位玩家）。该结果将相位几何、非交换代数与组合结构联系起来，为更一般地理解XOR游戏的量子策略完备性提供了精确的边界条件。

Abstract: We determine the smallest number of active questions per player at which a four-player binary exclusive-or (XOR) game of commuting-operator value one need not admit a Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) equatorial realization. Such a realization uses the four-qubit GHZ state and equatorial qubit observables, reducing perfect play to additive phase equations. We prove that every four-player XOR game with commuting-operator value one and at most three active questions per player has a perfect GHZ-equatorial strategy. Conversely, we construct a uniform eight-clause game with four active questions per player whose commuting-operator value is one but whose phase equations are inconsistent. Thus four is the sharp local-question threshold. The positive result follows by lifting every integral incidence obstruction to an ordered noncommutative refutation, using primitive circuits, forest matchings, and ternary Hamming geometry. For the separating game, a Klein four-group incidence relation obstructs the phase system, while an even-subgroup normal form and degree-one and degree-two Magnus coefficients exclude refutations of arbitrary length.

</details>


### [181] [Wave-particle-mixedness redistribution in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10334)
*Sumeng Wang,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild时空中两量子比特各向同性态（isotropic states）在Hawking辐射和环境退相干影响下，波特征、粒子特征与混合度（mixedness）的重新分配规律，并发现三者之间的三性关系（triality relation）不受热噪声和环境噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（curved spacetime）背景下，量子信息特征——波特征、粒子特征与混合度——如何受Hawking辐射和退相干通道影响尚不清楚。该研究旨在揭示这些物理过程对量子态特征分布的调控机制，并验证量子态内部固有约束关系是否在热噪声环境下得以保持。

Method: 以Schwarzschild时空中的两量子比特各向同性态为研究对象，在Hawking辐射与环境退相干共同作用下，系统分析了波特征、粒子特征与混合度在不同视界区域（horizon regions）的相对权重变化；进一步将分析推广到相位阻尼（phase damping）、相位翻转（phase flip）和比特翻转（bit flip）三种退相干通道。

Result: Hawking辐射并非简单抑制量子特征，而是改变不同视界区域间的相对权重；相位阻尼单调抑制波特征并增强混合度；相位翻转产生波特征的对称性死亡与复活（death-and-revival）行为；比特翻转主要通过布居数重分布（diagonal population redistribution）重塑粒子特征和混合度。

Conclusion: 尽管Hawking辐射和通道噪声改变了各子系统内波特征、粒子特征与混合度的分布，三者之间的三性关系仍然严格成立，且不受热与环境噪声的影响，表明该关系具有内在稳健性。

Abstract: The redistribution of the wave feature, particle feature, and mixedness is investigated for two-qubit isotropic states in Schwarzschild spacetime under Hawking radiation and environmental decoherence. It is shown that Hawking radiation changes their relative weights among different horizon regions rather than simply suppressing them. The analysis is further extended to phase damping, phase flip, and bit flip channels. Phase damping monotonically suppresses the wave feature and enhances mixedness, phase flip produces a symmetric death-and-revival behavior of the wave feature, and bit flip mainly reshapes the particle feature and mixedness through diagonal population redistribution. However, although Hawking radiation and channel noise affect the distribution of wave feature, particle feature, and mixedness in the subsystems, the triality relation among them still holds and remains unaffected by thermal and environmental noises.

</details>


### [182] [States dressing analysis in a transmon-transmon-bus system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11514)
*P. A. Gladilovich,R. A. Migdisov,A. V. Sabluk,P. S. Burtsev,N. N. Abramov,N. Y. Rudenko,V. I. Chichkov,N. A. Maleeva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验与解析地研究了超导量子处理器中transmon-transmon-bus三体系系统，其中计算态（computational states）被非计算态（noncomputational states）“修饰”（dressing）的程度可在宽范围内调谐。通过三种方法估计修饰程度，强调准确估计和控制计算态修饰对优化超导平台门操作的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中多量子比特门保真度可能受计算态与非计算态之间耦合导致的修饰效应限制，因此需要系统研究该效应及其可控性。

Method: 对transmon-transmon-bus系统进行实验测量与分析计算，利用三种方法估计计算态的修饰程度：完整三元素模型（full three-element model）、有效模式方法（effective mode approach）以及非微扰模式方法（unperturbed mode approach），并将修饰程度在宽范围内进行调节。

Result: 三种方法获得的修饰结果相互对照，揭示了计算态修饰效应在该系统中的可调谐特性，并证实了准确估计修饰程度对于优化超导平台量子门性能的关键作用。

Conclusion: 准确估计并控制计算态的修饰是实现超导平台上量子门优化的必要条件，本研究为此提供了系统的实验与解析框架。

Abstract: The multi-qubit gates fidelity of superconducting quantum processors can be limited due to the dressing of computational states by noncomputational ones. Here, we experimentally and analytically investigate a transmon-transmon-bus system where the computational states dressing is tunable over a broad range. We estimate the dressing using three methods: a full three-element model, an effective mode approach, and an unperturbed mode approach. The obtained results highlight the importance of the accurate estimation and control of the computational states dressing in order to optimize gates on superconducting platform.

</details>


### [183] [State preparation via measurement and feedback: pushing relations, state structures, and non-invertible symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08821)
*Yabo Li,Aditi Mitra,Tsung-Cheng Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对带测量和幺正反馈（measurement and unitary feedback, MF）的量子电路，提出了一种基于"可推动缺陷"（pushable defects）的系统性方法来构造一维目标态的有界深度（finite-depth）MF制备电路，并建立了可推动缺陷集合及其推动关系对MF可制备态的完整分类，同时揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带测量和幺正反馈的量子电路能够以恒定深度制备长程纠缠态，但针对给定目标态系统性地构造MF制备电路的问题此前尚未得到充分探索，因此需要开发一种通用的构造方案。

Method: 提出基于可推动缺陷的概念：可推动缺陷是矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）的虚键算子（virtual-bond operators），可以以支付物理反馈幺正算符为代价被推过张量。利用可推动缺陷的集合及其推动关系对有限深MF可制备态进行分类，并为每一类目标态关联一个可由其通过单轮MF电路制备的伴生态|B⟩，通过迭代该过程直到伴生态可由有限深局域幺正（finite-depth local unitary, FDLU）电路制备。

Result: 对于开边界矩阵乘积态，该方案是完备的：只要存在带左条件反馈修正的有限深MF制备，就能构造出相应的制备电路。此外，该表征揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性之间的联系，包括Tambara-Yamagami对偶算子和具有融合规则L_α L_{α'}=L_{α+α'}+L_{α-α'}的连续余弦对称算子及其推广。

Conclusion: 可推动缺陷及其推动关系为通过测量与反馈进行量子态制备提供了一个统一的原理，并为理解MF制备态的分类结构及与非可逆对称性的深层关联提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum circuits with measurements and unitary feedback (MF) can prepare long-range entangled states in constant depth, but a systematic construction of the MF preparation circuit for a given target state remains underexplored. We develop such a scheme for one-dimensional states, based on the notion of pushable defects: virtual-bond operators of a matrix product state that can be pushed through the tensor at the price of a physical feedback unitary. We show that the set of pushable defects, together with their pushing relations classifies finite-depth MF-preparable states and dictates their preparation circuits. To each class of the target state $|A\rangle$, we associate a state $|B\rangle$ from which $|A\rangle$ can be prepared using a 1-round MF circuit; in particular, $|A\rangle$ is preparable from a product state using a circuit with 1 round of MF whenever $|B\rangle$ is preparable by a finite-depth local unitary (FDLU) circuit. For a general target state, the scheme is obtained by iterating this procedure until the associated state is FDLU-preparable. For open-boundary matrix product states, the scheme is complete: it constructs a preparation circuit whenever finite-depth MF preparation with left-conditioned feedback corrections is possible. Pushable defects and pushing relations thus emerge as a unifying principle for quantum state preparation via measurements and feedback. This characterization further reveals an intrinsic connection between MF circuits and non-invertible symmetries: states with certain classes of pushing relations are related to a product state by Tambara-Yamagami duality operators, or by continuous cosine symmetry operators with fusion rules $L_α L_{α'} = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'}$, together with their generalizations up to (not necessarily transversal) gates.

</details>


### [184] [Coupled-Layer Codes: Beyond Quantum Product Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10069)
*Shuyu Zhang,Tzu-Chieh Wei,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"耦合层编码"（coupled-layer codes）这一统一框架，将乘积码（product codes）构造与拓扑物态的耦合层构造联系起来，通过凝聚第一编码多个独立层之间由Pauli算子产生的一般激发，实现了对既有乘积码构造的推广，并成功复现了X-cube模型、Chamon模型以及非CSS码（如费米环面码和3费米子Walker-Wang模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明，两个码之间的乘积可通过使用第二个码的校验耦合第一码的各层来物理实现。本文旨在进一步统一乘积码构造与物质相的耦合层构造，探索更一般的耦合方式，以涵盖非CSS码等更广泛的编码类型。

Method: 方法的核心理念是在第一编码的多个解耦层之间凝聚由Pauli算子产生的一般激发。这种凝聚由激发代数（excitation algebra）（编码激发信息）及保持代数结构的映射来刻画。层间耦合推广了物理学中的规范对称化（gauging）概念以及同调代数中的映射锥（mapping cone）。此外，本文还通过在自由置换群作用之外引入幺正变换来推广平衡乘积码，并给出了相应的耦合层构造。

Result: 耦合层编码成功复现了X-cube模型和Chamon模型。通过ZX对偶进行平衡操作，能够产生非CSS码，包括二维费米环面码（fermionic toric code）和三维3费米子Walker-Wang模型。

Conclusion: 耦合层编码提供了一个统一的框架，将乘积码构造与物质相耦合层构造联系起来，为构造更广泛的量子纠错码（包括非CSS码）提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Product codes are an important class of quantum error-correcting codes constructed from multiple input codes, which can give rise to asymptotically good quantum low-density parity check codes. In previous work, we showed how the product between two codes can be physically implemented by coupling layers of the first code using checks of the second code. In this work, we further unify product code constructions with coupled-layer constructions of phases of matter by introducing coupled-layer codes. The essential strategy is to condense general excitations created by Pauli operators among multiple decoupled layers of the first code. The condensation is specified by an excitation algebra, which encodes the excitations, along with an algebra-preserving map. This coupling between layers generalizes the notion of gauging in physics as well as the mapping cone in homological algebra, and can be used to produce non-CSS codes. As examples, we show how coupled-layer codes reproduce the X-cube and Chamon models. We further generalize the balanced product code by allowing a unitary transformation in addition to a group action by free permutation and describe its corresponding coupled-layer construction. In particular, we show how balancing by a ZX-duality can reproduce non-CSS codes such as the fermionic toric code and the 3-fermion Walker-Wang model in 3D.

</details>


### [185] [Kinetics of sliding-window quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10081)
*Adithya Sriram,Charles Stahl,Aleksander Kubica,Yaodong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将滑动窗口解码（sliding window decoding, SWD）映射为奇偶性守恒（parity-conserving）的随机动力学过程，建立了实时量子纠错（real-time QEC）的粗粒化有效描述，并识别出解码速率1/W为可解码相的相关微扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子纠错（quantum error correction, QEC）实现要求快速测量并持续处理综合征（syndrome）信息，以避免未处理数据积压。然而，相比"静态"QEC已通过平衡统计力学映射得到完善的理论理解，"实时"QEC目前缺乏类似的理论框架。

Method: 作者提出以随机动力学过程描述SWD的有效理论：其中带Z₂电荷的点粒子经历奇偶守恒的反应-扩散（reaction-diffusion）动力学。该模型在远大于窗口尺寸W的长度和时间尺度上有效描述系统行为，而尺度小于W的物理则导致有效参数产生非平凡的W依赖标度。作者还通过改变SWD的多种微观细节验证了该有效描述的广泛适用性。

Result: 研究发现解码速率1/W是可解码相（decodable phase）的一个相关（relevant）微扰。此外，改变SWD的大量微观细节并不影响其有效描述，表明所提出的动力学理论具有普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作为实时量子纠错中的滑动窗口解码建立了基于随机动力学的粗粒化理论框架，弥补了实时解码在统计力学理解上的空白，且其普适性暗示该描述可推广到更广泛的实时QEC实现方案。

Abstract: Practical implementations of quantum error correction (QEC) require rapid measurement and continuous processing of the syndrome information in order to prevent a backlog of unprocessed data. While ``static'' QEC is theoretically well understood via mappings to equilibrium statistical mechanics models, such an understanding of ``real-time'' QEC is currently lacking. Here, we study the kinetics of sliding window decoding (SWD), an implementation of real-time decoding that acts on temporally local windows of noisy syndrome information and commits to corrections irreversibly at a nonzero rate. We propose an effective description of SWD in terms of a stochastic kinetic process, where $\mathbb{Z}_2$-charged point particles undergo parity-conserving reaction and diffusion. This model describes dynamics at length and time scales large compared to the window size $W$, whereas physics at scales smaller than $W$ leads to nontrivial $W$-dependent scaling of the effective parameters. We identify the rate of decoding $1/W$ as a relevant perturbation to the decodable phase. We also show broad applicability of our results by changing many microscopic details of SWD without affecting the effective description.

</details>


### [186] [Bounds for Apparent Second-Law Violations in Quantum Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10118)
*Domingos S. P. Salazar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对一般量子轨迹动力学中物理熵产生（physical entropy production）σ不满足正向细致涨落定理（detailed fluctuation theorem）的问题，提出了动力学不对称项（dynamical-asymmetry term）σ*，将其补全为Ω=σ+σ*。作者证明了似然比符号（likelihood-ratio sign）在反转奇轨迹观测量中具有最优性，并据此将涨落定理下界迁移到平局修正的物理符号统计量，得到"频繁但温和"（frequent but mild）的定量规律，同时符号失衡为隐藏均值提供下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负随机熵产生通常被称为第二定律的表观违反（apparent violation），但在一般量子轨迹动力学中，物理熵产生σ并不需要满足正向细致涨落定理，因此需要更严格的量化约束来理解其负事件行为。

Method: 提出一种一般的任意耦合（arbitrary-coupling）形式体系，识别出动力学不对称项σ*，使Ω=σ+σ*的均值满足Σ+Σ*。证明似然比符号在反转奇（reversal-odd）轨迹观测量中是最优的，并利用该事实将已有的锋利涨落定理下界迁移到平局修正的物理符号统计量Π_σ=Pr(σ<0)+Pr(σ=0)/2。同时明确了测量记录（measured-record）协议的显式构造，并通过随机有限耦合碰撞模型和相干驱动量子比特与热辅助自旋（thermal ancillas）相互作用的数值审计进行验证。

Result: 当Pr(σ=0)=0时，得到Pr(σ<0)≥[1-⟨Ω⟩/g(⟨Ω⟩)]/2，其中g是a↦a tanh(a/2)的逆函数。物理积分涨落定理（integral fluctuation theorem）同时抑制大幅负事件，产生定量的"频繁但温和"规律；符号失衡（sign imbalance）为隐藏均值Σ*提供下界。

Conclusion: 在一般量子轨迹动力学中，物理熵产生的负事件并非简单违反第二定律，而是可以通过优化后的符号统计量获得严格的定量下界，形成"频繁但温和"的新正则性，且此类约束同时揭示隐藏的动力学不对称信息。

Abstract: Negative stochastic entropy production is commonly called an apparent violation of the second law. In general quantum-trajectory dynamics, however, the physical entropy production $σ$ need not obey a forward detailed fluctuation theorem. A general arbitrary-coupling formulation identifies a dynamical-asymmetry term $σ^\ast$ that completes it into $Ω=σ+σ^\ast$, whose mean is $\langleΩ\rangle=Σ+Σ^\ast$. We prove that the likelihood-ratio sign is optimal among reversal-odd trajectory observables and use this fact to transfer an established sharp fluctuation-theorem floor to the tie-corrected physical sign statistic $Π_σ=\Pr(σ<0)+\Pr(σ=0)/2$. When $\Pr(σ=0)=0$, the result reads $\Pr(σ<0)\ge[1-\langleΩ\rangle/g(\langleΩ\rangle)]/2$, where $g$ is the inverse of $a\mapsto a\tanh(a/2)$. The physical integral fluctuation theorem simultaneously suppresses large negative events, producing a quantitative ``frequent but mild'' law, while the sign imbalance lower-bounds the hidden mean $Σ^\ast$. We formulate the measured-record protocol explicitly and illustrate and numerically audit the tie-corrected theorem in random finite-coupling collision models and a coherently driven qubit interacting with thermal ancillas.

</details>


### [187] [Symmetrized Block-Product Periodic Marginals in Infinite Translation-Invariant Quantum Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10365)
*Xiao Zeng,Kaiyan Yang,Lingxia Zhang,Zizhu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维平移不变量子系统中可能隐藏的有限周期结构，提出了对称块乘积周期边际问题（symmetrized block-product periodic marginal problem），并开发了基于半定规划松弛和对称矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）的两种互补方法，用于诊断隐藏周期序并约束基态能量密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实量子系统中，有限周期结构（finite-period structure）可能被平移平均所隐藏，而仅从局部约化密度矩阵（local reduced density matrix）难以直接探测这类隐藏序。此外，对无限平移不变局域哈密顿量基态能量密度进行上界约束也需要有效的数值工具。因此作者提出系统化框架来回答这一问题。

Method: 作者定义了一个对称化块乘积周期的边际问题：给定n位约化密度矩阵，检查它是否可以通过沿链重复有限p位块态并对p个晶格平移求平均来获得。为此开发两种方法：第一种是基于块置换对称性和正部分转置（positive partial transpose）约束的半定规划（semidefinite programming）松弛，外逼近这类边际的凸包并提供可证明的不可行性检验；第二种是对称矩阵乘积态拟设（symmetrized matrix product state ansatz），构造显式的块乘积周期态并给出变分上界。

Result: 该框架在Majumdar-Ghosh模型、横向场Ising模型、XX、XXZ以及上下文关联性（contextuality）相关自旋模型上进行了基准测试。结果显示方法在精确可解情形中捕获了预期的有限周期结构，并且随着周期和键维数（bond dimension）的增加，变分能量系统性地改善。此外，作者还提出了用于平移不变上下文关联性证人的周期化NPA松弛，并在所测试的例子中恢复了已知的量子极限。

Conclusion: 该框架为从局域数据诊断隐藏周期序和约束无限平移不变局域哈密顿量的基态能量密度提供了有效且互补的途径，并在数值测试中验证了其有效性和收敛性。

Abstract: We study local marginals in one-dimensional translation-invariant quantum systems that may hide finite-period structure. Given an $n$-site reduced density matrix, we ask whether it can be obtained by repeating a finite $p$-site block state along the chain and averaging over the $p$ lattice translations. This defines a symmetrized block-product periodic marginal problem, which provides a route both to diagnosing hidden periodic order from local data and to upper bounding ground-state energy densities of infinite translation-invariant local Hamiltonians. We develop two complementary methods. The first is a semidefinite-programming relaxation based on block permutation symmetry and positive partial transpose constraints, which outer-approximates the convex hull of such marginals and yields certified infeasibility tests. The second is a symmetrized matrix product state ansatz, which constructs explicit block-product periodic states and gives variational upper bounds. We benchmark the framework on the Majumdar-Ghosh model, transverse-field Ising, XX, XXZ, and contextuality-related spin models. The results show that the method captures the expected finite-period structure in exactly solvable cases and gives systematically improving variational energies as the period and bond dimension increase. We also formulate a periodic-NPA relaxation for translation-invariant contextuality witnesses and recover the known quantum limits in the tested examples.

</details>


### [188] [Nanoscale graphitization and defect evolution in silicon-vacancy center-containing nanodiamonds under high-pressure high-temperature annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10632)
*M. De Feudis,B. Yavkin,L. Henry,K. O. Ho,M. -P. Adam,P. Goldner,F. Benedic,S. Desgreniers,J. -F. Roch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用巴黎-爱丁堡压机（Paris-Edinburgh press）结合SOLEIL同步辐射原位X射线衍射，测定了纳米金刚石在高压高温（HPHT）退火过程中的石墨化起始温度（2 GPa下约1800 K，4 GPa下约2120 K），确定了可避免石墨化的HPHT加工窗口，并证明低于该阈值的退火可改善含硅空位（SiV）缺陷纳米金刚石的光学响应，部分分辨出12 K下的SiV精细结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米金刚石中的IV族色心（如硅空位缺陷）是固态量子技术的有力候选，但纳米金刚石通常存在显著晶格应变和结构无序，会劣化其光学性质并阻碍低温下精细光谱结构的解析。HPHT退火被认为可弛豫内部应变，但纳米尺度下金刚石的相稳定性尚未得到充分约束，亟需明确其安全加工窗口。

Method: 采用巴黎-爱丁堡压机（Paris-Edinburgh press）耦合SOLEIL同步辐射原位X射线衍射，研究纳米金刚石在HPHT退火过程中的结构演变。通过构建纳米金刚石-NaCl-Pt专用样品组件，实现精确的压力-温度标定及相变的实时监测。

Result: 衍射数据显示，在所采用的HPHT加热协议下，金刚石到石墨的转变起始温度约为2 GPa下1800 K和4 GPa下2120 K。对低于石墨化阈值退火的样品进行光致发光（photoluminescence）测量，观察到光学响应改善，并在12 K下部分解析出SiV精细结构。

Conclusion: 实验确定的金刚石-石墨转变起始温度定义了纳米金刚石HPHT退火的实用压力-温度加工窗口，为避免可检测石墨化提供了校准框架，可用于可靠的离束（off-beam）退火处理。上述光学测量结果表明纳米尺度相稳定性与含SiV纳米金刚石在HPHT退火后的光学响应之间存在关联，为量子纳米金刚石的可控加工提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Group-IV color centers, such as the silicon-vacancy (SiV) defect, are highly promising for solid-state quantum technologies. However, nanodiamonds typically exhibit significant lattice strain and structural disorder, which degrade their optical properties and hinder the resolution of the fine spectral structure at cryogenic temperatures. High-pressure high-temperature (HPHT) annealing offers a potential route to relax internal strain, although the phase stability of diamond at the nanoscale under such conditions remains poorly constrained. Here, we investigate the structural evolution of nanodiamonds during HPHT annealing using a Paris-Edinburgh press coupled with in situ synchrotron X-ray diffraction at SOLEIL. A dedicated sample assembly combining nanodiamonds - NaCl - Pt enabled accurate pressure-temperature calibration and real-time monitoring of phase transformations. The diffraction data reveal that the onset of diamond-to-graphite transition occurs at approximately 1800 K at 2 GPa and 2120 K at 4 GPa under the applied HPHT heating protocol. These experimentally determined graphitization onsets define a practical pressure-temperature processing window for HPHT annealing of nanodiamonds while avoiding detectable graphitization and provide a calibrated framework for reliable off-beam annealing treatments that avoid graphitization. Photoluminescence measurements on samples annealed below the graphitization threshold show improved optical response, with partial resolution of the SiV fine structure at 12 K. These optical measurements suggest a relationship between nanoscale phase stability and the optical response of individual SiV-containing nanodiamonds following HPHT annealing. The experimentally established HPHT processing window provides a practical framework for the controlled processing of quantum nanodiamonds while avoiding graphitization.

</details>


### [189] [Boosting self hybridized exciton polaritons with metal clad WS2 waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10895)
*Filip Majstorovic,Masoud Taleb,Victor DeManuel-Gonzalez,Kai Rossnagel,Nahid Talebi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过在金属包覆的WS₂波导中嵌入活性激子层，显著增强了光子模式与激子之间的耦合强度，并改变了法布里-珀罗（Fabry-Pérot）型和导波型激子极化激元的色散特性，为混合异质结构中激子-光子相互作用的调控提供了稳健方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中法布里-珀罗型和导波型自杂化激子极化激元的形成可实现长距离激子能量转移和强激子-激子相互作用。为规避耦合强度限制、实现对激子与光子耦合的增强，需要探索有效的结构设计策略。

Method: 在金属包覆的WS₂波导结构中嵌入活性激子层，利用该波导结构对光子模式进行改性，以提升光子模式与激子之间的耦合强度。

Result: 与纯WS₂薄片相比，波导中的光子模式对法布里-珀罗型和导波型激子极化激元均表现出改性后的色散性质，且耦合强度显著增强。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种在混合异质结构中控制激子-光子相互作用及其耦合强度的稳健方法，对调控二维材料的光-物质相互作用具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The formation of Fabry Perot and guided wave self hybridized exciton polaritons in two dimensional materials results in long range exciton energy transfer and strong exciton exciton interactions. Here, we demonstrate that the coupling strength between photonic modes and excitons is significantly boosted by embedding the active excitonic layer in a metal clad WS2 waveguide. The photonic modes in this waveguide exhibit modified dispersion properties for both Fabry Perot type and guided wave exciton polaritons compared to pure WS2 flakes, and show an increased couplingr strength. Our results provide a robust approach for controlling exciton photon interactions and their coupling strength in hybrid heterostructures.

</details>


### [190] [Impact of strain and dark states on spectroscopic measurements of silicon-vacancy centers in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11168)
*Tommy Chin,Kesav V. Narayan,Imran Bashir,Kelsey M. Bates,Liam G. Stanton,Ehsan Khatami,Christopher L. Smallwood*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个计算模型，用于模拟含高浓度硅-空位（SiV⁻）色心金刚石样品的光学多维相干光谱（MDCS）实验，揭示了该系统应变的高度随机性，并发现高应变中心可能与光发射显著解耦，这对SiV⁻色心作为量子传感器的应用具有启示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带负电的硅-空位（SiV⁻）色心是量子技术的重要平台，但关于大量SiV⁻色心如何协同行为的问题尚未解决，需要计算模型来理解高浓度样品中的集体行为和光谱特征。

Method: 开发了一个计算模型，用于模拟近期利用光学多维相干光谱（MDCS）对金刚石中高浓度SiV⁻色心样品进行的实验，并通过对比不同探测方案下的光谱特征来验证模型。

Result: 模拟结果显示该系统中的应变效应高度随机，特征轴向应变为2.8×10⁻⁴，剪切应变为3.5×10⁻⁵；此外，应变值超过1.5×10⁻⁵的高应变色心可能与光发射显著解耦。

Conclusion: 这些发现对SiV⁻色心作为量子传感器的应用具有重要意义，提示需要在实际应用中考虑应变分布对色心光学性能的影响。

Abstract: Negatively charged silicon-vacancy (SiV$^-$) centers in diamond offer an attractive platform for the development of many forms of quantum technology. However, questions remain in connection to how large ensembles of SiV$^-$ centers behave in concert. Here, we develop a computational model designed to simulate recent experiments where optical multidimensional coherent spectroscopy (MDCS) was used to examine a high-concentration sample of SiV$^-$ centers in diamond, revealing significant variations in spectral signature depending on the detection scheme. Simulation results reveal that strain effects are highly random in this system, with a characteristic axial strain of $2.8 \times 10^{-4}$ and a shear strain of $3.5 \times 10^{-5}$. They suggest in addition that highly strained centers (with values exceeding $1.5 \times 10^{-5}$) may become significantly decoupled from optical emission. The results have implications for the use of SiV$^-$ centers as quantum sensors.

</details>


### [191] [Work distribution for strongly coupled many-body open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11196)
*H. T. M. Nghiem,T. A. Costi,S. Campbell,A. K. Mitchell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时间依赖数值重整化群方法，对多体开放系统在非平衡强耦合条件下的量子功分布函数进行了非微扰精确求解，揭示了与安德森正交灾难相关的幂律阈值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体开放系统的非平衡量子热力学在强耦合区域展现出丰富的物理现象，包括强系统-浴关联和非微扰效应，而现有方法难以精确刻画其中的量子功分布函数中连续的多体激发谱。

Method: 将时间依赖数值重整化群（TDNRG）方法扩展到量子功分布函数的计算，该方法直接适用于热力学极限，可处理任意淬火幅度、零温或有限温度情形，并提供指数级精细的低能分辨率。

Result: 数值精确解揭示了当功趋近最小值时，由于安德森正交灾难（Anderson orthogonality catastrophe）导致的幂律阈值行为；在系统-浴关联诱导的涌现低能标度以下，分布呈现普适的标度塌缩。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种非微扰的精确框架，用于研究自旋-玻色子和安德森杂质模型等量子杂质系统在淬火后的量子功分布，并揭示了由系统-浴关联主导的普适临界行为。

Abstract: The non-equilibrium quantum thermodynamics of many-body open systems is notoriously rich, especially in the strong-coupling regime where strong system-bath correlations develop and interactions produce non-perturbative effects. Such systems can be described by `quantum impurity models' where the system and bath are treated on an equal footing as a single composite. Paradigmatic examples are the spin-boson and Anderson impurity models, in which system degrees of freedom interact with either bosonic or fermionic gapless baths. Here we study the quantum work distribution function (WDF) of such systems following a quench. Capturing the full continuum of many-body excitations in the WDF requires a non-perturbative solution of the underlying non-equilibrium quantum impurity problem. For this purpose, we extend the time-dependent numerical renormalization group (TDNRG) approach to the calculation of the WDF, which applies directly in the thermodynamic limit, can be used for arbitrary quench amplitudes at zero or finite temperature, and provides exponentially fine low-energy resolution. Our numerically-exact solution reveals power-law threshold behavior due to the Anderson orthogonality catastrophe when the work approaches its minimum value, with universal scaling collapse of the distribution below an emergent low-energy scale induced by system-bath correlations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Predicting Space Groups of Double Perovskites by LLM with Dynamic Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10483)
*Jongwon Park,Inhyo Lee,Junhyeong Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DyRIS框架，一个基于大语言模型代理（LLM-agent）的空间群预测系统，通过动态少量样本检索与规则引导推理来解决双钙钛矿材料空间群预测中类别不平衡的问题，在3,528条热力学过滤数据上取得了竞争性整体精度和最优的小类空间群（Minor-SG）预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双钙钛矿（Double Perovskites, DPs）具有广泛的组分可调性，但预测其稳定结构的空间群（Space Groups, SGs）十分困难，主要原因是现有数据集严重偏向少数主导的空间群类别，导致少数类（Minor SG）预测性能不佳。

Method: DyRIS框架包含两个核心模块：一是多样性增强的动态少量样本提示（diversity-enhanced dynamic few-shot prompting），在检索上下文示例的同时限制主导空间群的过度呈现；二是基于B/B'阳离子有序性、定量指标（quantitative indicators）以及主要空间群偏置控制（major-SG bias control）的规则引导推理（rule-guided inference），用于精炼并排序最终的前三个空间群候选。

Result: 在3,528条热力学过滤的双钙钛矿数据和训练数据比例0.5的条件下，DyRIS取得了与基线相当的整体准确率，同时获得了最优的整体Top-1宏F1分数，以及全部Minor-SG指标上的最佳性能。相比CrabNet，Minor-SG Top-1准确率提升3.26个百分点；优于最强PyCaret基线的Minor-SG Top-3准确率。消融实验和额外实验进一步验证了各模块的贡献及规则指导推理步骤的不可替代性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，将基于检索的大语言模型推理与晶体学领域知识相结合，是处理不平衡材料数据集中空间群预测问题的有效途径，为材料信息学中的类别不平衡问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Double perovskites (DPs) offer broad compositional tunability, but predicting the space groups (SGs) of stable structures remains difficult because available datasets are often strongly imbalanced toward dominant SG classes. We refer to dominant SG classes as major SGs and underrepresented classes as minor SGs. We introduce Dynamic and Diversity-enhanced Few-shot Retrieval and Rule-Guided Inference for Space-Group Prediction (DyRIS), an LLM-agent-based framework that predicts ranked SG candidates from a given DP composition. DyRIS uses diversity-enhanced dynamic few-shot prompting to retrieve relevant in-context examples while limiting the dominance of frequently represented SGs. It further incorporates rule-guided inference based on B/B' cation ordering, quantitative indicators, and major-SG bias control to refine and rank the final Top-3 SG candidates. We evaluate DyRIS on 3,528 thermodynamically filtered DP entries and compare it with composition-based and descriptor-based baselines. At a training-data ratio of 0.5, DyRIS achieves competitive overall accuracy while obtaining the best Overall Top-1 macro-F1 score and the best performance across all Minor-SG metrics. DyRIS improves Minor-SG Top-1 accuracy by 3.26 percentage points relative to CrabNet and achieves higher Minor-SG Top-3 accuracy than the strongest PyCaret-based baseline. Ablation studies show that diversity-enhanced retrieval, quantitative indicators, major-SG bias control, and B/B' ordering information each contribute to prediction performance. Additional experiments show that the final rule-guided inference step is not easily replaced by conventional classifier- or ranker-based models. These findings demonstrate the potential of combining retrieval-based LLM reasoning with crystallographic domain knowledge for SG prediction in imbalanced materials datasets.

</details>
